// Hall Pass Kiosk
// Server-side Apps Script

// The release number other teachers see in their teacher view. They update their own copy by pasting the
// changed files from the "Update your kiosk" page (asl-study-hall-site/kiosk-update/, built by
// dev/build-update-page.py). Raise it by one with every release (the build script checks it).
var KIOSK_RELEASE = 3;

/* ===== Teachers list (2026-10-17) =====
   Each teacher's copy tells Colleen's kiosk that it is in use: their Google email, their Sheet's name and
   their release number. It goes to the "Kiosk Teachers" tab in Colleen's Sheet (one row per copy), when they
   run setUpMyKiosk and at most once a day when their teacher view is open. Colleen's own kiosk and the
   template never report. The manual tells teachers about it. */
var KIOSK_HOME_SCRIPT_ID_ = "1BbVBbDBo8jHpgFngE8EFnfdVp29qENdeg7TX14NOQfVaTB93zpgzJi5X";
var KIOSK_TEMPLATE_SCRIPT_ID_ = "1B4GOcGi2H_BxJfcZGcTlmizEdNjjFXnYyElGp_MfFFyB2kpWzR8rhBPK";
var KIOSK_HOME_URL_ = "https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbwUqOVMu6CzVjG9KVuD7DBlhNGYLGypZrHMwbp7z64WJgMb9YLCbM9OsjyTOVh5M0A/exec";
var KIOSK_TEACHERS_HEAD_ = ["Email", "Sheet Name", "Release", "First Seen", "Last Seen", "Time Zone", "Copy ID"];

// From a teacher's copy: "this copy is in use". Quiet if it can't reach Colleen's kiosk.
function kioskReportInUse_(force) {
  try {
    var id = ScriptApp.getScriptId();
    if (id === KIOSK_HOME_SCRIPT_ID_ || id === KIOSK_TEMPLATE_SCRIPT_ID_) return "not a teacher's copy";
    var cache = kioskCache_();
    if (!force && cache.get("kiosk:reportedInUse")) return "already today";
    var email = "";
    try { email = Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail() || ""; } catch (e) {}
    UrlFetchApp.fetch(KIOSK_HOME_URL_, {
      method: "post", contentType: "application/json", muteHttpExceptions: true, followRedirects: true,
      payload: JSON.stringify({ kioskTeacher: 1, email: email, sheet: SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getName(),
        release: KIOSK_RELEASE, tz: Session.getScriptTimeZone(), copy: id })
    });
    cache.put("kiosk:reportedInUse", "1", 21600);
    return "reported";
  } catch (err) {
    return "could not report";
  }
}

// On Colleen's kiosk only: record a teacher's copy in the "Kiosk Teachers" tab (one row per copy, updated each time).
function doPost(e) {
  var out = function(t) { return ContentService.createTextOutput(t); };
  try {
    if (ScriptApp.getScriptId() !== KIOSK_HOME_SCRIPT_ID_) return out("not here");
    var d = JSON.parse((e && e.postData && e.postData.contents) || "{}");
    if (!d || d.kioskTeacher !== 1) return out("ignored");
    var clean = function(v, max) { return String(v == null ? "" : v).replace(/[\u0000-\u001f<>]/g, "").trim().slice(0, max); };
    var email = clean(d.email, 120), copy = clean(d.copy, 80).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g, "");
    if (!copy || (email && !/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+$/.test(email))) return out("bad");
    var release = Math.max(0, Math.min(9999, Math.round(Number(d.release) || 0)));
    kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
      var sheet = kioskSeatTab_("Kiosk Teachers", KIOSK_TEACHERS_HEAD_), now = new Date(), row = 0;
      if (sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
        sheet.getRange(2, 7, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().forEach(function(r, i) { if (!row && String(r[0]) === copy) row = i + 2; });
      }
      if (row) {
        sheet.getRange(row, 1, 1, 3).setValues([[email, clean(d.sheet, 120), release]]);
        sheet.getRange(row, 5, 1, 2).setValues([[now, clean(d.tz, 60)]]);
      } else {
        sheet.appendRow([email, clean(d.sheet, 120), release, now, now, clean(d.tz, 60), copy]);
      }
    });
    return out("ok");
  } catch (err) {
    return out("error");
  }
}





function saveBirthdayToggleSettings__write_(
  videoEnabled,
  soundEnabled,
  messageEnabled
) {
  var sheet =
    ensureDisplaySettingsRows_();

  sheet
    .getRange("B23:B24")
    .setValues([
      [
        kioskSettingOn_(
          videoEnabled,
          true
        )
          ? "ON"
          : "OFF"
      ],
      [
        kioskSettingOn_(
          soundEnabled,
          true
        )
          ? "ON"
          : "OFF"
      ]
    ]);

  sheet
    .getRange("B29")
    .setValue(
      kioskSettingOn_(
        messageEnabled,
        true
      )
        ? "ON"
        : "OFF"
    );

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    birthdayVideoEnabled:
      kioskSettingOn_(
        videoEnabled,
        true
      ),
    birthdaySoundEnabled:
      kioskSettingOn_(
        soundEnabled,
        true
      ),
    birthdayMessageEnabled:
      kioskSettingOn_(
        messageEnabled,
        true
      )
  };
}

function getBirthdayVideoAsset() {
  var settings =
    readKioskSettings();

  var result = {
    dataUrl: "",
    sourceName:
      settings.birthdayCustomVideoFileName ||
      "Default Transparent Confetti Animation"
  };

  var videoFile = null;

  if (
    settings.birthdayCustomVideoFileId
  ) {
    try {
      videoFile =
        DriveApp.getFileById(
          settings.birthdayCustomVideoFileId
        );
    } catch (error) {
      videoFile = null;
    }
  }

  if (!videoFile) {
    var defaultFolder =
      DriveApp.getFolderById(
        "1ySyfmuW2oQUtDIda9mOZ4Ex0T9CsUOWF"
      );

    var files =
      defaultFolder.getFilesByName(
        "birthday_full_transparent_720p_compressed.webm"
      );

    if (files.hasNext()) {
      videoFile =
        files.next();
    }
  }

  if (!videoFile) {
    throw new Error(
      "Birthday video could not be found."
    );
  }

  var blob =
    videoFile.getBlob();

  result.dataUrl =
    "data:" +
    (
      blob.getContentType() ||
      "video/webm"
    ) +
    ";base64," +
    Utilities.base64Encode(
      blob.getBytes()
    );

  return result;
}

function getBirthdaySoundAsset() {
  var settings =
    readKioskSettings();

  var result = {
    dataUrl: "",
    sourceName:
      settings.birthdayCustomSoundFileName ||
      "Default Birthday Music"
  };

  var audioFile = null;

  if (
    settings.birthdayCustomSoundFileId
  ) {
    try {
      audioFile =
        DriveApp.getFileById(
          settings.birthdayCustomSoundFileId
        );
    } catch (error) {
      audioFile = null;
    }
  }

  if (!audioFile) {
    var defaultFolder =
      DriveApp.getFolderById(
        "1ySyfmuW2oQUtDIda9mOZ4Ex0T9CsUOWF"
      );

    var files =
      defaultFolder.getFilesByName(
        "Birthday_Overlay_Sound_5sec_MUCH_LOUDER.mp3"
      );

    if (files.hasNext()) {
      audioFile =
        files.next();
    }
  }

  if (!audioFile) {
    throw new Error(
      "Birthday sound could not be found."
    );
  }

  var blob =
    audioFile.getBlob();

  result.dataUrl =
    "data:" +
    (
      blob.getContentType() ||
      "audio/mpeg"
    ) +
    ";base64," +
    Utilities.base64Encode(
      blob.getBytes()
    );

  return result;
}


function getBirthdayUploadFolder_() {
  var folderName =
    "Hall Pass Kiosk Birthday Uploads";

  var folders =
    DriveApp.getFoldersByName(
      folderName
    );

  if (folders.hasNext()) {
    return folders.next();
  }

  return DriveApp.createFolder(
    folderName
  );
}

function saveBirthdayCelebrationUpload__write_(
  kind,
  fileName,
  dataUrl
) {
  kind =
    String(kind || "")
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  if (
    kind !== "video" &&
    kind !== "sound"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Choose either a birthday video or sound."
    );
  }

  var match =
    String(dataUrl || "").match(
      /^data:([^;]+);base64,(.+)$/
    );

  if (!match) {
    throw new Error(
      "Could not read that upload."
    );
  }

  var mimeType =
    String(match[1] || "")
      .toLowerCase();

  var lowerName =
    String(fileName || "")
      .toLowerCase();

  if (kind === "video") {
    if (
      mimeType !== "video/webm" &&
      !/\.webm$/i.test(lowerName)
    ) {
      throw new Error(
        "Birthday video uploads must be WebM files. Transparent WebM is recommended."
      );
    }
  } else {
    var allowedAudio =
      mimeType === "audio/mpeg" ||
      mimeType === "audio/mp3" ||
      mimeType === "audio/wav" ||
      mimeType === "audio/x-wav" ||
      /\.mp3$/i.test(lowerName) ||
      /\.wav$/i.test(lowerName);

    if (!allowedAudio) {
      throw new Error(
        "Birthday sound uploads must be MP3 or WAV files."
      );
    }
  }

  var bytes =
    Utilities.base64Decode(
      match[2]
    );

  var maxBytes =
    kind === "video"
      ? 15 * 1024 * 1024
      : 8 * 1024 * 1024;

  if (bytes.length > maxBytes) {
    throw new Error(
      kind === "video"
        ? "Birthday videos must be 15 MB or smaller."
        : "Birthday sounds must be 8 MB or smaller."
    );
  }

  var blob =
    Utilities.newBlob(
      bytes,
      match[1],
      String(
        fileName ||
        (
          kind === "video"
            ? "birthday.webm"
            : "birthday.mp3"
        )
      )
    );

  var folder =
    getBirthdayUploadFolder_();

  var driveFile =
    folder.createFile(
      blob
    );

  var sheet =
    ensureDisplaySettingsRows_();

  if (kind === "video") {
    sheet
      .getRange("B25")
      .setValue(
        driveFile.getId()
      );

    sheet
      .getRange("B27")
      .setValue(
        driveFile.getName()
      );
  } else {
    sheet
      .getRange("B26")
      .setValue(
        driveFile.getId()
      );

    sheet
      .getRange("B28")
      .setValue(
        driveFile.getName()
      );
  }

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return readKioskSettings();
}

function restoreDefaultBirthdayCelebrationAsset__write_(
  kind
) {
  kind =
    String(kind || "")
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();

  var sheet =
    ensureDisplaySettingsRows_();

  if (kind === "video") {
    sheet
      .getRange("B25")
      .clearContent();

    sheet
      .getRange("B27")
      .clearContent();
  } else if (kind === "sound") {
    sheet
      .getRange("B26")
      .clearContent();

    sheet
      .getRange("B28")
      .clearContent();
  } else {
    throw new Error(
      "Choose either video or sound."
    );
  }

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return readKioskSettings();
}


// =====================================================================
// SPEED LAYER
//
// 1. Saved answers: results that only change when the spreadsheet changes
//    (settings, roster, destinations, bell schedule, fonts) are remembered
//    for a few minutes instead of re-reading the sheet on every request.
// 2. Setup checks: the "is this tab set up correctly?" checks run once,
//    then are skipped until something could have changed.
//
// ACCURACY: everything saved is stamped with a "version". The version
// changes (which throws away every saved answer and re-runs every setup
// check) whenever:
//   - anything is typed/pasted into the spreadsheet        (onEdit)
//   - rows/columns/tabs are added or removed               (kioskOnChange)
//   - the app itself saves settings, photos, fonts, etc.   (kioskRunWrite_)
// and, as a backstop, saved answers expire after KIOSK_CACHE_SECONDS_.
// Live data (who is out of the room, attendance taps) is never saved.
// =====================================================================
// Students start arriving at this time. From then until the first class of
// the day reaches its late bell, the kiosk behaves like a passing period
// for that first class (attendance check-in front and center).
// Change the time here if arrival ever moves. Use "" to turn this off.
var KIOSK_ARRIVAL_START_ = "7:20 AM";

var KIOSK_CACHE_SECONDS_ = 900;        // 15 minutes
var KIOSK_SETUP_CHECK_SECONDS_ = 600;  // 10 minutes
var KIOSK_VERSION_KEY_ = "kiosk:version";
var KIOSK_CHUNK_CHARS_ = 30000;

var kioskVersionMemo_ = null;
var kioskDataMemo_ = {};
var kioskSetupMemo_ = {};
var kioskSetupFlags_ = null;
var kioskWriteDepth_ = 0;
var kioskBypassCache_ = false;

var KIOSK_SETUP_CHECK_NAMES_ = [
  "ensureLogHeaders",
  "ensureSettingsSheet",
  "ensureBackgroundsSheet",
  "ensureFontsSheet",
  "ensureAttendanceSummarySheet_",
  "ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_",
  "ensureAttendanceEmojiBankSheet_",
  "ensureUncheckedOutTimingSetting_",
  "ensureAttendanceReportSettingsRows_",
  "ensureDisplaySettingsRows_",
  "ensureDestinationIconBankSheet_",
  "ensureSeparatedLunchBlocks_",
  "ensureAttendanceFoundation_"
];

function kioskCache_() {
  return CacheService.getScriptCache();
}

function kioskNewVersion_() {
  return String(new Date().getTime()) + "-" +
    String(Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000));
}

// One trip to the cache fetches the version and every setup-check flag.
function kioskLoadVersion_() {
  if (kioskVersionMemo_) return kioskVersionMemo_;

  var keys = [KIOSK_VERSION_KEY_].concat(
    KIOSK_SETUP_CHECK_NAMES_.map(function(name) {
      return "kiosk:setup:" + name;
    })
  );

  var found = {};

  try {
    found = kioskCache_().getAll(keys) || {};
  } catch (err) {
    found = {};
  }

  var version = found[KIOSK_VERSION_KEY_];

  if (!version) {
    version = kioskNewVersion_();

    try {
      kioskCache_().put(KIOSK_VERSION_KEY_, version, 21600);
    } catch (putErr) {}
  }

  kioskVersionMemo_ = version;
  kioskSetupFlags_ = found;

  return version;
}

// Throws away every saved answer and forces every setup check to re-run.
function invalidateKioskCache_() {
  kioskVersionMemo_ = null;
  kioskSetupFlags_ = null;
  kioskDataMemo_ = {};
  kioskSetupMemo_ = {};

  try {
    kioskCache_().put(KIOSK_VERSION_KEY_, kioskNewVersion_(), 21600);
  } catch (err) {}
}

// Runs fullCheck (the original, complete check) unless it already passed
// for the current version. A missing tab always forces the full check.
function kioskSetupCheck_(name, sheetName, fullCheck) {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  function quickResult() {
    if (!sheetName) return true;
    return ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
  }

  if (!kioskBypassCache_) {
    if (kioskSetupMemo_[name]) {
      var memoResult = quickResult();
      if (memoResult) return memoResult;
    }

    var version = kioskLoadVersion_();

    if (
      kioskSetupFlags_ &&
      kioskSetupFlags_["kiosk:setup:" + name] === version
    ) {
      var cachedResult = quickResult();

      if (cachedResult) {
        kioskSetupMemo_[name] = true;
        return cachedResult;
      }
    }
  }

  var versionBefore = kioskBypassCache_ ? null : kioskLoadVersion_();
  var result = fullCheck();

  if (!kioskBypassCache_) {
    kioskSetupMemo_[name] = true;

    try {
      kioskCache_().put(
        "kiosk:setup:" + name,
        versionBefore,
        KIOSK_SETUP_CHECK_SECONDS_
      );
    } catch (err) {}
  }

  return result;
}

// Returns the saved answer for `key`, or computes it and saves it.
// A fresh copy is returned every time so callers can never alter the
// saved version by accident.
function kioskCached_(key, compute) {
  if (kioskBypassCache_) {
    return compute();
  }

  if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(kioskDataMemo_, key)) {
    return JSON.parse(kioskDataMemo_[key]);
  }

  var version = kioskLoadVersion_();
  var cacheKey = "kiosk:data:" + key;
  var text = kioskCacheRead_(cacheKey, version);

  if (text === null) {
    var value = compute();

    text = JSON.stringify(value === undefined ? null : value);

    // Saved under the version read BEFORE computing: if the sheet changed
    // while we were reading it, this copy is discarded automatically.
    if (!kioskBypassCache_) {
      kioskCacheWrite_(cacheKey, version, text);
    }
  }

  if (!kioskBypassCache_) {
    kioskDataMemo_[key] = text;
  }

  return JSON.parse(text);
}

function kioskCacheRead_(cacheKey, version) {
  try {
    var cache = kioskCache_();
    var head = cache.get(cacheKey);

    if (!head) return null;

    var cut = head.indexOf("|");
    var cut2 = head.indexOf("|", cut + 1);

    if (cut < 0 || cut2 < 0) return null;
    if (head.substring(0, cut) !== version) return null;

    var chunks = Number(head.substring(cut + 1, cut2));

    if (chunks === 0) {
      return head.substring(cut2 + 1);
    }

    var keys = [];

    for (var i = 0; i < chunks; i++) {
      keys.push(cacheKey + ":" + version + ":" + i);
    }

    var parts = cache.getAll(keys) || {};
    var text = "";

    for (var j = 0; j < keys.length; j++) {
      if (typeof parts[keys[j]] !== "string") return null;
      text += parts[keys[j]];
    }

    return text;
  } catch (err) {
    return null;
  }
}

function kioskCacheWrite_(cacheKey, version, text, seconds) {
  try {
    var cache = kioskCache_();
    var keep = seconds || KIOSK_CACHE_SECONDS_;

    if (text.length <= KIOSK_CHUNK_CHARS_) {
      cache.put(
        cacheKey,
        version + "|0|" + text,
        keep
      );
      return;
    }

    // Too large to save sensibly (for example many big custom fonts).
    if (text.length > 3000000) return;

    var values = {};
    var count = Math.ceil(text.length / KIOSK_CHUNK_CHARS_);

    for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      values[cacheKey + ":" + version + ":" + i] =
        text.substr(i * KIOSK_CHUNK_CHARS_, KIOSK_CHUNK_CHARS_);
    }

    cache.putAll(values, keep);
    cache.put(
      cacheKey,
      version + "|" + count + "|",
      keep
    );
  } catch (err) {}
}

// Wraps every function that changes settings, rosters, images or fonts.
// While it runs nothing is read from or written to the saved answers, and
// when it finishes the version changes so every kiosk re-reads the sheet.
function kioskRunWrite_(impl, args) {
  kioskWriteDepth_++;
  kioskBypassCache_ = true;

  if (kioskWriteDepth_ === 1) {
    invalidateKioskCache_();
  }

  try {
    return impl.apply(null, args);
  } finally {
    kioskWriteDepth_--;

    if (kioskWriteDepth_ === 0) {
      try {
        SpreadsheetApp.flush();
      } catch (flushErr) {}

      invalidateKioskCache_();
    }
  }
}

function kioskTimeZone_() {
  return kioskCached_("timeZone", function() {
    return (
      SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSpreadsheetTimeZone() ||
      Session.getScriptTimeZone()
    );
  });
}

function kioskTodayKey_() {
  return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), kioskTimeZone_(), "yyyy-MM-dd");
}

// Bell Schedule rows (A:I). null when the tab is missing or empty.
function kioskBellScheduleRows_() {
  return kioskCached_("bellRows", function() {
    ensureSeparatedLunchBlocks_();

    var sheet = SpreadsheetApp
      .getActiveSpreadsheet()
      .getSheetByName("Bell Schedule");

    if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return null;

    return kioskRowsByHeading_(sheet, KIOSK_BELL_COLS_, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  });
}

// Schedule Calendar rows (A:F). Empty list when the tab is missing.
function kioskScheduleCalendarRows_() {
  return kioskCached_("calendarRows", function() {
    var calendar = SpreadsheetApp
      .getActiveSpreadsheet()
      .getSheetByName("Schedule Calendar");

    if (!calendar || calendar.getLastRow() < 2) return [];

    return kioskRowsByHeading_(calendar, KIOSK_CALENDAR_COLS_, 2, calendar.getLastRow() - 1);
  });
}

// Fires when rows, columns or tabs are added/removed by a person (things
// onEdit does not see). Installed automatically; see below.
function kioskOnChange(e) {
  invalidateKioskCache_();
  kioskForgetStartRows_();
  try { kioskCache_().remove("kiosk:bgStamp"); } catch (err) {}
}

/* ===== Reading only the rows that matter =====
   New rows are always added at the bottom of Logs and Attendance Events,
   so instead of reading the whole year every few seconds:
   - Attendance Events: today's rows start at a remembered row.
   - Logs: every pass above the first one still out is finished.
   Starting rows are kept in Script Properties for the day. Any change made
   by hand in the sheet (sorting, deleting rows) forgets them, and the next
   read looks again from the top. */

function kioskDayKeyOf_(value, tz) {
  if (!(value instanceof Date) || isNaN(value.getTime())) return "";
  return Utilities.formatDate(value, tz, "yyyy-MM-dd");
}

function kioskReadJsonProp_(key) {
  try {
    return JSON.parse(
      PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty(key) || "null"
    );
  } catch (err) {
    return null;
  }
}

function kioskWriteJsonProp_(key, value) {
  try {
    PropertiesService.getScriptProperties()
      .setProperty(key, JSON.stringify(value));
  } catch (err) {}
}

function kioskForgetStartRows_() {
  try {
    var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
    props.getKeys().forEach(function(key) {
      if (key.indexOf("kioskStartRow:") === 0) props.deleteProperty(key);
    });
  } catch (err) {}
}

// First row of `sheet` dated today (dates in `dateColumn`), or
// lastRow + 1 when there are none yet.
function kioskTodayStartRow_(sheet, dateColumn) {
  return kioskStartRowForDay_(sheet, dateColumn, kioskTodayKey_(), "today");
}

// First row of `sheet` dated `today` (yyyy-MM-dd) or later; lastRow + 1 when
// none. `label` names the remembered row ("today", "week").
function kioskStartRowForDay_(sheet, dateColumn, today, label) {
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return 2;

  var tz = kioskTimeZone_();
  var propKey = "kioskStartRow:" + label + ":" + sheet.getName();
  var saved = kioskReadJsonProp_(propKey);

  if (
    saved && saved.date === today &&
    saved.row >= 2 && saved.row <= lastRow + 1
  ) {
    var above = saved.row === 2
      ? ""
      : kioskDayKeyOf_(sheet.getRange(saved.row - 1, dateColumn).getValue(), tz);

    if (saved.row === 2 || (above && above < today)) {
      return saved.row;
    }
  }

  // Walk up from the bottom until 50 rows in a row are from earlier days.
  var first = lastRow + 1;
  var olderRun = 0;
  var bottom = lastRow;

  while (bottom >= 2 && olderRun < 50) {
    var top = Math.max(2, bottom - 499);
    var dates = sheet.getRange(top, dateColumn, bottom - top + 1, 1).getValues();

    for (var i = dates.length - 1; i >= 0 && olderRun < 50; i--) {
      var day = kioskDayKeyOf_(dates[i][0], tz);
      if (!day) continue;

      if (day >= today) {
        first = top + i;
        olderRun = 0;
      } else {
        olderRun++;
      }
    }

    bottom = top - 1;
  }

  kioskWriteJsonProp_(propKey, { date: today, row: first });
  return first;
}

// Logs: row of the first pass that may still be out. Starts from the top
// once a day, so nothing older is ever missed for long.
function kioskOpenPassStartRow_(lastRow) {
  var saved = kioskReadJsonProp_("kioskStartRow:openPass:Logs");

  if (
    saved && saved.date === kioskTodayKey_() &&
    saved.row >= 2 && saved.row <= lastRow + 1
  ) {
    return saved.row;
  }

  return 2;
}

function kioskSaveOpenPassStartRow_(row) {
  var key = "kioskStartRow:openPass:Logs";
  var today = kioskTodayKey_();
  var saved = kioskReadJsonProp_(key);

  if (!saved || saved.date !== today || saved.row !== row) {
    kioskWriteJsonProp_(key, { date: today, row: row });
  }
}

// Makes sure the change watcher above exists. Checked at most every 6
// hours. If it cannot be installed, saved answers still expire on their
// own after KIOSK_CACHE_SECONDS_.
function kioskEnsureChangeWatcher_() {
  try {
    var cache = kioskCache_();

    if (cache.get("kiosk:watcherChecked")) return;

    var exists = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().some(function(trigger) {
      return trigger.getHandlerFunction() === "kioskOnChange";
    });

    if (!exists) {
      ScriptApp.newTrigger("kioskOnChange")
        .forSpreadsheet(SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet())
        .onChange()
        .create();
    }

    cache.put("kiosk:watcherChecked", "1", 21600);
  } catch (err) {}
}

// Run this from the editor if you ever want every kiosk to re-read the
// whole spreadsheet immediately.
function refreshKioskDataNow() {
  invalidateKioskCache_();
  return "Saved answers cleared. Kiosks will re-read the spreadsheet.";
}

// Everything the kiosk needs when it opens, in ONE request instead of
// eight to ten. Each part is independent: if one fails, the page asks for
// just that part the old way.
// The kiosk's one background check (every 10 seconds). `request` names the
// parts the page wants this time: passes, settings, bell ({ override }),
// teacher ({ period, className, since }). Each part is answered on its own,
// so one problem never blocks the others.
// Settings > Save: both halves in one trip to Google (they used to be two
// requests, one after the other).
function saveAllSettings(kiosk, attendance, reminder) {
  var savedKiosk = saveKioskSettings(kiosk);
  var savedAttendance = saveAttendanceSettings(attendance);
  var savedReminder = reminder ? saveReminderSettings(reminder) : getReminderSettings();
  return { kiosk: savedKiosk, attendance: savedAttendance, reminder: savedReminder };
}

function getKioskPoll(request) {
  request = request || {};
  var out = {};

  function part(name, fn) {
    if (!request[name]) return;
    try {
      out[name] = fn(request[name]);
    } catch (err) {
      out[name + "Error"] = String(err && err.message || err);
    }
  }

  part("passes", function() { return getActivePasses(); });
  part("settings", function() { return getKioskSettings(); });
  part("bell", function(args) { return getBellScheduleStatus(args.override); });
  part("teacher", function(args) { return getTeacherAttendanceChanges(args); });
  part("week", function() { return getWeekPassMinutes(); });
  part("queue", function() { return getPassQueue(); });
  part("seating", function() { return getSeatingForKiosk(); });

  return out;
}

function getStartupBundle(scheduleOverride) {
  kioskEnsureChangeWatcher_();
  kioskEnsureArchiveTimer_();

  var bundle = {};

  function part(name, fn) {
    try {
      bundle[name] = fn();
    } catch (err) {
      bundle[name] = null;
      bundle[name + "Error"] = String(err && err.message || err);
    }
  }

  part("kioskSettings", function() { return getKioskSettings(); });
  part("backgroundOptions", function() { return getBackgroundOptions(); });
  part("fontOptions", function() { return getFontOptions(); });
  part("attendanceEmojiBank", function() { return getAttendanceEmojiBank(); });
  part("attendanceSettings", function() { return getAttendanceSettings(); });
  part("emojiPlan", function() { return getDailyEmojiPlan(); });
  part("teacherPinSet", function() { return kioskTeacherPinIsSet_(); });
  part("reminderSettings", function() { return getReminderSettings(); });
  part("passQueue", function() { return getPassQueue(); });
  part("seating", function() { return getSeatingForKiosk(); });
  part("roster", function() { return getStudentRoster(); });
  part("destinations", function() { return getDestinationList(); });
  part("activePasses", function() { return getActivePasses(); });
  part("bellScheduleNames", function() { return getBellScheduleNames(); });
  part("bellScheduleStatus", function() {
    return getBellScheduleStatus(scheduleOverride || "__AUTO__");
  });

  if (
    bundle.bellScheduleStatus &&
    bundle.bellScheduleStatus.scheduleName
  ) {
    part("bellScheduleDisplay", function() {
      return getBellScheduleDisplay(
        bundle.bellScheduleStatus.scheduleName
      );
    });
  }

  return bundle;
}
