// Destination icon pictures live in Artwork.html (a data-only file that is
// never sent to the browser as a whole). The kiosk asks for just the icons
// it is showing, so the page itself stays small and loads quickly.
function getDestinationArtwork(requests) {
  var result = {};
  var cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  var wanted = [];
  var seen = {};

  (requests || []).forEach(function(item) {
    var collection = String(item && item.collection || "");
    var key = String(item && item.key || "");
    var id = collection + "||" + key;

    if (!collection || !key || seen[id]) return;

    seen[id] = true;
    wanted.push({
      id: id,
      collection: collection,
      key: key,
      cacheKey: "kiosk:art:" + id
    });
  });

  if (!wanted.length) return result;

  // 1. Icons Google already has on hand (no need to open the big file).
  var cached = {};

  try {
    cached = cache.getAll(
      wanted.map(function(item) { return item.cacheKey; })
    ) || {};
  } catch (cacheErr) {
    cached = {};
  }

  var missing = [];

  wanted.forEach(function(item) {
    if (cached[item.cacheKey]) {
      result[item.id] = cached[item.cacheKey];
    } else {
      missing.push(item);
    }
  });

  if (!missing.length) return result;

  // 2. Anything else comes from the Artwork file.
  var all;

  try {
    all = kioskLoadArtworkFile_();
  } catch (loadErr) {
    // Tell the page exactly what is wrong instead of failing silently.
    result.__error = String(loadErr && loadErr.message || loadErr);
    return result;
  }

  var toCache = {};

  missing.forEach(function(item) {
    var bank = all[item.collection] || {};
    var art = bank[item.key] || bank.Other || "";

    if (art) {
      result[item.id] = art;

      if (art.length < 90000) {
        toCache[item.cacheKey] = art;
      }
    }
  });

  try {
    if (Object.keys(toCache).length) {
      cache.putAll(toCache, 21600);
    }
  } catch (putErr) {}

  return result;
}

// Reads and checks the Artwork file. Throws a plain-language error if the
// file is missing or was not pasted completely.
function kioskLoadArtworkFile_() {
  var text = "";

  try {
    text = HtmlService
      .createTemplateFromFile("Artwork")
      .getRawContent();
  } catch (rawErr) {
    text = "";
  }

  if (!text) {
    try {
      text = HtmlService
        .createHtmlOutputFromFile("Artwork")
        .getContent();
    } catch (fileErr) {
      throw new Error(
        "The Artwork file was not found in this project. Add an HTML " +
        "file named Artwork and paste in the contents of Artwork.txt."
      );
    }
  }

  text = String(text || "");

  var start = text.indexOf("{");
  var end = text.lastIndexOf("}");

  if (start < 0 || end <= start) {
    throw new Error(
      "The Artwork file is empty or incomplete. Paste the full contents " +
      "of Artwork.txt into it again."
    );
  }

  var all;

  try {
    all = JSON.parse(text.substring(start, end + 1));
  } catch (parseErr) {
    throw new Error(
      "The Artwork file is incomplete (it was probably cut off when " +
      "pasted). Paste the full contents of Artwork.txt into it again. " +
      "It should end with }}"
    );
  }

  return all;
}

// Run this from the Apps Script editor (select checkEmojis, click Run, then
// look at the Execution log). Read-only: looks at the real check-ins on the
// Attendance Events tab and reports any emoji two students shared on the same
// day, and any student who got the same emoji two school days in a row.
function checkEmojis() {
  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  var tz = kioskTimeZone_();
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  var lines = [];

  if (lastRow < 2) {
    Logger.log("No check-ins yet.");
    return "No check-ins yet.";
  }

  var values = sheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 12).getValues();
  var byDay = {};   // day -> { studentKey: { name, emoji } }

  values.forEach(function(row) {
    var day = kioskDayKeyOf_(row[1], tz);
    var emoji = String(row[11] || "").trim();
    if (!day || !emoji || String(row[10] || "").trim() === "Cancelled") return;
    var key = attendanceStudentKey_(row[6], row[5]);
    (byDay[day] = byDay[day] || {})[key] = { name: String(row[5] || key), emoji: emoji };
  });

  var days = Object.keys(byDay).sort().slice(-10);   // the last 10 school days
  var problems = 0;

  days.forEach(function(day, d) {
    var students = byDay[day];
    var keys = Object.keys(students);
    var owners = {};

    keys.forEach(function(k) {
      var e = students[k].emoji;
      (owners[e] = owners[e] || []).push(students[k].name);
    });

    var shared = Object.keys(owners).filter(function(e) { return owners[e].length > 1; });
    var repeats = [];
    if (d > 0) {
      var before = byDay[days[d - 1]];
      keys.forEach(function(k) {
        if (before[k] && before[k].emoji === students[k].emoji) {
          repeats.push(students[k].name + " " + students[k].emoji);
        }
      });
    }

    function few(list, n) {
      return list.slice(0, n).join(", ") + (list.length > n ? " and " + (list.length - n) + " more" : "");
    }

    // The fix went live on 2026-10-16, partway through that day.
    var beforeFix = day < "2026-10-17";
    if (!beforeFix) problems += shared.length + repeats.length;

    lines.push(day + (beforeFix ? " (before the fix)" : "") + ": " + keys.length + " students, " +
      Object.keys(owners).length + " different emojis" +
      (shared.length
        ? ". SHARED: " + few(shared.map(function(e) { return e + " " + few(owners[e], 3); }), 4)
        : ". No one shared an emoji") +
      (d === 0 ? "" : repeats.length
        ? ". SAME AS THE DAY BEFORE: " + few(repeats, 5)
        : ". No one repeated the day before"));
  });

  lines.unshift(problems
    ? "PROBLEM: found " + problems + " shared or repeated emoji(s) since the fix."
    : "All good since the fix: no shared emojis and no day-to-day repeats. (Days before the fix are shown for comparison.)");

  var report = lines.join("\n");
  Logger.log(report);
  return report;
}

// Run this from the Apps Script editor (select checkKioskStartup, click Run,
// then look at the Execution log). Read-only: does what the kiosk does when
// it opens, and reports what it gets back and how long each step takes.
function checkKioskStartup() {
  var lines = [];
  var t = Date.now();
  var bundle = getStartupBundle(null);
  lines.push("Startup data: " + (Date.now() - t) + " ms.");

  Object.keys(bundle).forEach(function(key) {
    if (/Error$/.test(key)) lines.push("PROBLEM in " + key.replace(/Error$/, "") + ": " + bundle[key]);
  });

  var size = 0;
  try { size = JSON.stringify(bundle).length; } catch (err) {}
  lines.push("Startup data size: " + Math.round(size / 1024) + " KB.");

  var options = bundle.backgroundOptions || [];
  var chosen = String(bundle.kioskSettings && bundle.kioskSettings.backgroundName || "");
  lines.push("Backgrounds sent to the kiosk: " + options.length + ". Chosen background: \"" + chosen + "\".");

  var waiting = options.filter(function(o) { return o.type === "IMAGE" && !o.value; }).length;
  lines.push("Pictures the kiosk fetches one at a time: " + waiting + ".");

  var names = chosen ? [chosen] : [];
  if (options[0] && names.indexOf(options[0].name) === -1) names.push(options[0].name);

  names.forEach(function(name) {
    var start = Date.now();
    var picture = "";
    var problem = "";
    try { picture = String(getBackgroundImage(name) || ""); } catch (err) { problem = String(err && err.message || err); }
    var kind = /^data:image\//.test(picture) ? "the picture itself" : /^https?:/.test(picture) ? "a web address" : picture ? "something else" : "NOTHING";
    lines.push("\"" + name + "\": the kiosk gets " + kind + ", " + Math.round(picture.length / 1024) + " KB, in " + (Date.now() - start) + " ms" + (problem ? " — PROBLEM: " + problem : "") + ".");

    start = Date.now();
    try { getBackgroundImage(name); } catch (err) {}
    lines.push("   Asking again (should come from the saved copy): " + (Date.now() - start) + " ms.");
  });

  var report = lines.join("\n");
  Logger.log(report);
  return report;
}

// Run this from the Apps Script editor (select checkBackgrounds, click Run,
// then look at the Execution log). Read-only: it reports what is on the
// Backgrounds tab and whether each uploaded picture can still be opened.
function checkBackgrounds() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Backgrounds");
  var lines = [];

  if (!sheet) {
    Logger.log("There is no tab named Backgrounds in this Sheet.");
    return "No Backgrounds tab";
  }

  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  lines.push("Backgrounds tab: " + Math.max(0, lastRow - 1) + " rows below the header.");

  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    var count = lastRow - 1;
    var display = sheet.getRange(2, 1, count, 4).getDisplayValues();
    var values = sheet.getRange(2, 4, count, 1).getValues();
    var formulas = sheet.getRange(2, 4, count, 1).getFormulas();
    var floating = {};

    try {
      (sheet.getImages() || []).forEach(function(image) {
        var anchor = image.getAnchorCell();
        if (anchor && anchor.getColumn() === 4) floating[anchor.getRow()] = true;
      });
    } catch (err) {}

    var working = 0, broken = 0, off = 0;

    display.forEach(function(row, i) {
      var r = i + 2;
      var name = String(row[1] || "").trim();
      if (!name) return;
      if (String(row[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() !== "ON") {
        off++;
        lines.push("Row " + r + " \"" + name + "\": switched off (column A is not ON)");
        return;
      }

      var type = String(row[2] || "IMAGE").trim().toUpperCase();
      var value = values[i][0];
      var stored =
        type === "CSS" ? "a color/gradient" :
        (value && typeof value === "object") ? "a picture inside the cell" :
        /^DRIVEFILE:/i.test(String(value || "")) ? "a Drive upload" :
        formulas[i][0] ? "an =IMAGE() formula" :
        floating[r] ? "a picture floating over the cell" :
        String(value || "").trim() ? "a link" : "NOTHING in column D";

      var picture = "";
      try { picture = String(getBackgroundImageByName_(name) || ""); } catch (err) { picture = ""; }

      // A web address only counts if Google actually hands out the picture.
      var web = "";
      if (/^https?:/i.test(picture)) {
        try {
          var reply = UrlFetchApp.fetch(picture, { muteHttpExceptions: true, followRedirects: true });
          var code = reply.getResponseCode();
          var kind = String(reply.getHeaders()["Content-Type"] || reply.getHeaders()["content-type"] || "");
          if (code !== 200 || kind.indexOf("image/") !== 0) {
            picture = "";
            web = "; its web address answered " + code + " " + kind;
          }
        } catch (err) {
          picture = "";
          web = "; its web address failed: " + (err && err.message || err);
        }
      }

      if (picture) {
        working++;
        lines.push("Row " + r + " \"" + name + "\": OK (" + stored + ")");
      } else if (web) {
        broken++;
        lines.push("Row " + r + " \"" + name + "\": PICTURE WILL NOT LOAD (" + stored + web + ")");
      } else {
        broken++;
        lines.push("Row " + r + " \"" + name + "\": NO PICTURE COMES BACK (column D has " + stored + ")");
      }
    });

    lines.splice(1, 0, "Pictures working: " + working + ". Not working: " + broken + ". Switched off: " + off + ".");
  }

  var report = lines.join("\n");
  Logger.log(report);
  return report;
}

// Run this from the Apps Script editor (select it, click Run, then look at
// the Execution log) to check that the destination icons are in place.
function checkDestinationArtwork() {
  var report;

  try {
    var all = kioskLoadArtworkFile_();
    var styles = Object.keys(all);
    var total = 0;

    var parts = styles.map(function(style) {
      var count = Object.keys(all[style] || {}).length;
      total += count;
      return style + ": " + count;
    });

    report =
      total >= 240
        ? "Artwork OK. " + total + " icons (" + parts.join(", ") + ")."
        : "Artwork is INCOMPLETE. Found " + total +
          " icons, expected 240 (" + parts.join(", ") +
          "). Paste the full contents of Artwork.txt again.";
  } catch (err) {
    report = "Artwork PROBLEM: " + String(err && err.message || err);
  }

  Logger.log(report);
  return report;
}

/* ONE color theme for every Kiosk page (Colleen, 2026-10-17: "when i change one in one place, it stays that way for all pages").
   Saved in the script properties; each page opens in it, and any page's Colors menu changes it. "auto" = match the computer. */
function getKioskTheme() {
  try { return String(PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("KIOSK_THEME") || ""); } catch (err) { return ""; }
}
function setKioskTheme(v) {
  v = String(v || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, "").slice(0, 20) || "auto";
  PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().setProperty("KIOSK_THEME", v);
  return v;
}
function kioskThemed_(out, params) {
  var t = getKioskTheme() || String((params && params.theme) || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, ""), url = "";
  try { url = ScriptApp.getService().getUrl(); } catch (err) {}
  return out.setContent(out.getContent().replace(/<html([^>]*)>/i, function (m, a) {      // data-kiosk-theme: what is saved (also "auto"), for the page's Colors menu; data-kiosk-url: for Pop out
    return "<html" + (t ? a.replace(/\s*data-theme="[^"]*"/, "") : a) + (t ? ' data-kiosk-theme="' + t + '"' + (t === "auto" ? "" : ' data-theme="' + t + '"') : "") + (url ? ' data-kiosk-url="' + url.replace(/"/g, "") + '"' : "") + ">"; }));
}

function doGet(e) {
  var settings = readKioskSettings();
  var view = String(
    e && e.parameter && (e.parameter.view || e.parameter.page) || ""
  ).trim().toLowerCase();

  // Any link that mentions "drill" opens the drill view (?view=drill,
  // ?drill, ?page=Drill, ?mode=drill...), so a slightly different saved
  // bookmark still works.
  var params = (e && e.parameter) || {};
  var wantsDrill = view.indexOf("drill") !== -1 ||
    Object.keys(params).some(function(k) {
      return /drill/i.test(k) || /drill/i.test(String(params[k] || ""));
    });

  // Class memories, the year-end page: ?view=memories&year=2026 (school year starting that August)
  if (view === "memories") {
    return HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(kioskMemoriesPage_(params))
      .setTitle("Class Memories")
      .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1')
      .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
  }

  // Classroom tools in their own small window: ?view=tools&tool=timer|dice|score|rnum|passboard|timeleft
  if (view === "tools") {
    return kioskThemed_(HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile('Tools'), e && e.parameter)
      .setTitle("Classroom tools")
      .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1')
      .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
  }

  // Seating charts: ?view=seating (editor) or ?view=seat&cls=…&key=… (one seat, lit up)
  if (view === "seating" || view === "seat") {
    return kioskThemed_(HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile('Seating'), e && e.parameter)
      .setTitle("Seating charts")
      .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1')
      .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
  }

  if (wantsDrill) {
    return kioskThemed_(HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile('Drill'), e && e.parameter)
      .setTitle("Drill view")
      .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1')
      .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
  }

  if (view === "teacher" || view === "dashboard") {
    try {
      return kioskThemed_(HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile('Dashboard'), e && e.parameter)
        .setTitle(
          (settings.mainTitle || settings.topTitle || "Hall Pass Kiosk") +
          " — Teacher"
        )
        .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1')
        .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
    } catch (dashboardError) {
      return HtmlService.createHtmlOutput(
        '<p style="font:18px sans-serif;padding:24px">The teacher dashboard ' +
        'file is missing. In Apps Script, add an HTML file named ' +
        '<b>Dashboard</b>, paste Dashboard.txt into it, and deploy a new ' +
        'version.</p>'
      );
    }
  }

  var browserTitle = settings.mainTitle || settings.topTitle || "Hall Pass Kiosk";

  // The kiosk layout needs at least 1280px of width. Tablets and phones
  // (iPad mini, etc.) lay the page out at 1280px wide and scale it to fit
  // the screen, so the panels never overlap. Computers ignore this line.
  var page = HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile('Index');

  // ?settings=only: just Teacher Settings, shown inside the teacher view, in its colors (?theme=…).
  if (String(params.settings || "") === "only") {
    var theme = getKioskTheme() || String(params.theme || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, "");      // the one saved color theme first
    page.setContent(
      page.getContent()
        .replace("<html>", "<html" + (theme && theme !== "auto" ? ' data-theme="' + theme + '"' : "") + ">")
        .replace("<body>", '<body data-settings-only="1">')
    );
    return page
      .setTitle("Teacher Settings")
      .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1')
      .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
  }

  return page
    .setTitle(browserTitle)
    .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=1280')
    .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL);
}

// Students sheet:
// A = Period
// B = Class Title
// C = First Name
// D = Last Name
// E = Student ID (optional)
// F = Student Photo: pasted in-cell image OR image URL (optional)
//
// Classes sheet:
// A = Period
// B = Class Title
// C = Class Image: pasted in-cell image OR image URL (optional)


var birthdayScheduleCalendarCache_ = null;

function getBirthdayScheduleCalendarCache_() {
  if (birthdayScheduleCalendarCache_) {
    return birthdayScheduleCalendarCache_;
  }

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var tz = ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() || Session.getScriptTimeZone();
  var calendar = ss.getSheetByName("Schedule Calendar");
  var rows = [];
  var specificByDate = {};
  var weeklyByDay = {};

  if (calendar && calendar.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    rows = kioskRowsByHeading_(calendar, KIOSK_CALENDAR_COLS_, 2, calendar.getLastRow() - 1);

    rows.forEach(function(row) {
      var ruleType = String(row[0] || "").trim();
      var ruleValue = String(row[1] || "").trim();

      if (ruleType === "Specific Date") {
        var dateKey = normalizeScheduleDateKey_(ruleValue);
        if (dateKey) {
          specificByDate[dateKey] = row;
        }
      } else if (ruleType === "Weekday") {
        var dayKey = ruleValue.toLowerCase();
        if (dayKey) {
          weeklyByDay[dayKey] = row;
        }
      }
    });
  }

  birthdayScheduleCalendarCache_ = {
    tz: tz,
    rows: rows,
    specificByDate: specificByDate,
    weeklyByDay: weeklyByDay
  };

  return birthdayScheduleCalendarCache_;
}

function birthdaySchoolDayInfo_(date) {
  var cache = getBirthdayScheduleCalendarCache_();
  var tz = cache.tz;

  var weekday = Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, "EEEE");
  var dateKey = Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, "M/d/yyyy");

  var schoolDay =
    weekday !== "Saturday" &&
    weekday !== "Sunday";

  var specific = cache.specificByDate[
    normalizeScheduleDateKey_(dateKey)
  ] || null;

  var weekly = cache.weeklyByDay[
    weekday.toLowerCase()
  ] || null;

  var selected = specific || weekly;

  if (selected) {
    schoolDay =
      String(selected[3] || "Yes")
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase() !== "no";
  }

  return {
    schoolDay: schoolDay
  };
}

function parseStudentBirthday_(value) {
  if (
    value instanceof Date &&
    !isNaN(value)
  ) {
    return {
      month: value.getMonth() + 1,
      day: value.getDate(),
      year: value.getFullYear()
    };
  }

  var text =
    String(value || "").trim();

  if (!text) {
    return null;
  }

  var match =
    text.match(
      /^(\d{1,2})[\/-](\d{1,2})(?:[\/-](\d{2,4}))?$/
    );

  if (!match) {
    var parsed =
      new Date(text);

    if (isNaN(parsed)) {
      return null;
    }

    return {
      month: parsed.getMonth() + 1,
      day: parsed.getDate(),
      year: parsed.getFullYear()
    };
  }

  var year =
    match[3]
      ? Number(match[3])
      : null;

  if (
    year != null &&
    year < 100
  ) {
    year +=
      year >= 50
        ? 1900
        : 2000;
  }

  return {
    month: Number(match[1]),
    day: Number(match[2]),
    year: year
  };
}

function birthdayOrdinal_(number) {
  number = Number(number) || 0;

  var mod100 =
    number % 100;

  if (
    mod100 >= 11 &&
    mod100 <= 13
  ) {
    return number + "th";
  }

  var mod10 =
    number % 10;

  if (mod10 === 1) return number + "st";
  if (mod10 === 2) return number + "nd";
  if (mod10 === 3) return number + "rd";

  return number + "th";
}

function birthdayContextForToday_(birthdayValue) {
  var parsed =
    parseStudentBirthday_(
      birthdayValue
    );

  if (!parsed) {
    return {
      observedToday: false,
      message: ""
    };
  }

  var tz =
    getBirthdayScheduleCalendarCache_().tz;

  var now =
    new Date();

  var today =
    new Date(
      now.getFullYear(),
      now.getMonth(),
      now.getDate()
    );

  var actualBirthday =
    new Date(
      today.getFullYear(),
      parsed.month - 1,
      parsed.day
    );

  var observedBirthday =
    new Date(
      actualBirthday
    );

  if (
    !birthdaySchoolDayInfo_(
      actualBirthday
    ).schoolDay
  ) {
    var guard = 0;

    while (
      guard < 21
    ) {
      guard += 1;

      observedBirthday.setDate(
        observedBirthday.getDate() - 1
      );

      if (
        birthdaySchoolDayInfo_(
          observedBirthday
        ).schoolDay
      ) {
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  var todayKey =
    Utilities.formatDate(
      today,
      tz,
      "yyyy-MM-dd"
    );

  var observedKey =
    Utilities.formatDate(
      observedBirthday,
      tz,
      "yyyy-MM-dd"
    );

  var age = null;

  if (
    parsed.year != null &&
    isFinite(parsed.year)
  ) {
    age =
      actualBirthday.getFullYear() -
      parsed.year;
  }

  return {
    observedToday:
      todayKey === observedKey,
    message:
      todayKey === observedKey
        ? (
            age != null
              ? "Happy " +
                birthdayOrdinal_(age) +
                " Birthday!"
              : "Happy Birthday!"
          )
        : "",
    age: age
  };
}



/* ===== Pass restriction (Students tab, "Pass Status" column) =====
   "Restricted" = the student may not leave without an escort. The kiosk
   shows their card in red and it cannot be chosen for a hall pass.
   Attendance check-in is not affected. */
var STUDENT_PASS_STATUS_HEADER_ = "Pass Status";
var STUDENT_PASS_STATUS_OPTIONS_ = ["Allowed", "Restricted"];

// Students tab columns are found by their HEADING, so a teacher can move columns around (2026-10-17).
// Each column accepts a few common heading names; a missing heading falls back to the original column.
var KIOSK_STUDENT_COLUMNS_ = {
  period: { names: ["period"], fallback: 1 },
  classTitle: { names: ["class title", "class", "class name", "course", "course name", "course title"], fallback: 2 },
  first: { names: ["first name", "first", "firstname"], fallback: 3 },
  last: { names: ["last name", "last", "lastname", "surname"], fallback: 4 },
  id: { names: ["student id", "id", "student number", "student no", "student", "studentid"], fallback: 5 },
  photo: { names: ["student photo", "photo", "picture", "student picture"], startsWith: true, fallback: 6 },
  dob: { names: ["dob", "birthday", "birth date", "birthdate", "date of birth"], fallback: 7 }
};

function kioskHeadingKey_(text) {
  return String(text || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\([^)]*\)/g, " ").replace(/[^a-z0-9#]+/g, " ").replace(/#/g, " no ").trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
}

// { period: 1, classTitle: 2, first: 3, ... } (1-based column numbers) for this Students tab.
function kioskStudentColumns_(sheet) {
  var lastCol = Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn(), 7);
  var heads = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getDisplayValues()[0].map(kioskHeadingKey_);
  var out = { lastCol: lastCol }, used = {};
  Object.keys(KIOSK_STUDENT_COLUMNS_).forEach(function(key) {
    var spec = KIOSK_STUDENT_COLUMNS_[key], found = 0;
    spec.names.some(function(name) {      // the first name in the list wins, so "student id" beats "student"
      for (var c = 0; c < heads.length; c++) {
        if (used[c]) continue;
        if (heads[c] === name || (spec.startsWith && heads[c].indexOf(name + " ") === 0)) { found = c + 1; return true; }
      }
      return false;
    });
    out[key] = found || spec.fallback;
    if (found) used[found - 1] = true;
  });
  return out;
}

// Any tab teachers edit: its rows in the column order the code expects, matched by HEADING, so the
// columns can be moved (2026-10-17). spec = one list of accepted heading names per expected column;
// a heading that isn't found keeps its original position.
var KIOSK_BELL_COLS_ = [["enabled", "on", "on off"], ["schedule", "schedule name"], ["period"], ["roster period"],
  ["roster opens", "opens"], ["late bell", "class starts", "starts"], ["class ends", "ends"], ["block type", "type"], ["notes", "note"]];
var KIOSK_DEST_COLS_ = [["destination", "place"], ["collection", "icon style"], ["category"], ["icon"], ["enabled", "on", "on off"],
  ["notes", "note"], ["ask i got a pass", "ask pass", "got a pass"], ["optional trip", "optional"]];
var KIOSK_CALENDAR_COLS_ = [["rule type"], ["rule value", "date", "day"], ["schedule"], ["school day"], ["notes", "note"], ["display note"]];

function kioskHeadingMap_(sheet, spec) {
  var lastCol = Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn(), spec.length);
  var heads = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getDisplayValues()[0].map(kioskHeadingKey_);
  var used = {}, map = [];
  spec.forEach(function(names, i) {
    var found = -1;
    names.some(function(name) {
      for (var c = 0; c < heads.length; c++) if (!used[c] && heads[c] === name) { found = c; return true; }
      return false;
    });
    if (found >= 0) used[found] = true;
    map.push(found);
  });
  var found = map.map(function(c) { return c >= 0; });
  map = map.map(function(c, i) { return c >= 0 ? c : i; });      // not found: the original column
  return { cols: map, found: found, lastCol: lastCol, heads: heads };
}

// Rows startRow.. of a tab, each in the spec's order. raw = true for real values (dates), else display text.
function kioskRowsByHeading_(sheet, spec, startRow, numRows, raw) {
  var m = kioskHeadingMap_(sheet, spec);
  var range = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, m.lastCol);
  return (raw ? range.getValues() : range.getDisplayValues()).map(function(r) {
    return m.cols.map(function(c) { return r[c] === undefined ? "" : r[c]; });
  });
}

// Students tab "Commuter" column (Yes/No). Each row is one student in one
// class period, so Yes on a student's 2nd-period row covers only 2nd period.
// Commuters arriving after the late bell count as Present (not tardy); the
// real check-in time and minutes after the bell are still recorded.
function studentCommuterColumn_(sheet) {
  var lastCol = Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn(), 7);
  var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getDisplayValues()[0];

  for (var c = 0; c < headers.length; c++) {
    if (String(headers[c] || "").trim().toLowerCase() === "commuter") {
      return c + 1;
    }
  }

  var col = 8;
  while (col <= lastCol && String(headers[col - 1] || "").trim()) {
    col++;
  }

  try {
    kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, col);
    sheet.getRange(1, col).setValue("Commuter");
    var rule = SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation()
      .requireValueInList(["Yes", "No"], true)
      .setAllowInvalid(false)
      .setHelpText("Yes = this student arrives late to THIS class period because of travel. They are not marked tardy; their check-in time is still recorded.")
      .build();
    sheet
      .getRange(2, col, Math.max(sheet.getMaxRows() - 1, 1), 1)
      .setDataValidation(rule);
  } catch (err) {}

  return col;
}

// Run from the Apps Script editor (select addCommuterColumn, click Run) to
// add the Students tab "Commuter" column right away. Safe to run again: it
// only reports where the column is. The Execution log names the column.
function addCommuterColumn() {
  var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Students");
  if (!sheet) {
    Logger.log("There is no tab named Students.");
    return "There is no tab named Students.";
  }

  var col = studentCommuterColumn_(sheet);
  invalidateKioskCache_();   // the kiosk re-reads the student list with the new column

  var letter = "";
  for (var n = col; n > 0; n = Math.floor((n - 1) / 26)) {
    letter = String.fromCharCode(65 + (n - 1) % 26) + letter;
  }

  var report = "The Commuter column is column " + letter + " on the Students tab. " +
    "Choose Yes on a student's row for the class period they commute to (each row is one class period).";
  Logger.log(report);
  return report;
}

function studentPassStatusColumn_(sheet) {
  var lastCol = Math.max(sheet.getLastColumn(), 7);
  var headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getDisplayValues()[0];

  for (var c = 0; c < headers.length; c++) {
    if (String(headers[c] || "").trim().toLowerCase() === "pass status") {
      return c + 1;
    }
  }

  // Not there yet: add it in the first empty column from H onward.
  var col = 8;
  while (col <= lastCol && String(headers[col - 1] || "").trim()) {
    col++;
  }

  try {
    kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, col);
    sheet.getRange(1, col).setValue(STUDENT_PASS_STATUS_HEADER_);
    var rule = SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation()
      .requireValueInList(STUDENT_PASS_STATUS_OPTIONS_, true)
      .setAllowInvalid(false)
      .setHelpText("Restricted = escort only. The kiosk turns the student's card red and it cannot be chosen for a hall pass.")
      .build();
    sheet
      .getRange(2, col, Math.max(sheet.getMaxRows() - 1, 1), 1)
      .setDataValidation(rule);
  } catch (err) {}

  return col;
}

function studentPassRestricted_(value) {
  var text = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  return text.indexOf("restrict") !== -1 || text.indexOf("escort") !== -1;
}

function getStudentRoster__read_() {
  birthdayScheduleCalendarCache_ = null;

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var studentSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Students");

  if (!studentSheet || studentSheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return [];
  }

  var rowCount = studentSheet.getLastRow() - 1;

  // Startup still avoids touching the photo cells as image objects.
  // Columns are found by their headings (kioskStudentColumns_), so they can be in any order.
  var cols = kioskStudentColumns_(studentSheet);
  var allData =
    studentSheet
      .getRange(2, 1, rowCount, cols.lastCol)
      .getDisplayValues();
  var coreData = allData.map(function(r) {
    return [r[cols.period - 1], r[cols.classTitle - 1], r[cols.first - 1], r[cols.last - 1], r[cols.id - 1]];
  });

  var birthdayValues =
    studentSheet
      .getRange(2, cols.dob, rowCount, 1)
      .getValues();

  var passStatusValues = [];
  try {
    var passStatusColumn = studentPassStatusColumn_(studentSheet);
    passStatusValues = studentSheet
      .getRange(2, passStatusColumn, rowCount, 1)
      .getDisplayValues();
  } catch (passStatusError) {
    passStatusValues = [];
  }

  var commuterValues = [];
  try {
    commuterValues = studentSheet
      .getRange(2, studentCommuterColumn_(studentSheet), rowCount, 1)
      .getDisplayValues();
  } catch (commuterError) {
    commuterValues = [];
  }

  var birthdayContextCache = {};
  var groups = {};
  var order = [];

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    var period = String(coreData[i][0] || "").trim();
    var classTitle = String(coreData[i][1] || "").trim();
    var firstName = String(coreData[i][2] || "").trim();
    var lastName = String(coreData[i][3] || "").trim();
    var studentId = String(coreData[i][4] || "").trim();

    if (!period || !classTitle || !firstName) {
      continue;
    }

    var birthdayValue =
      birthdayValues[i]
        ? birthdayValues[i][0]
        : "";

    var birthdayCacheKey =
      birthdayValue instanceof Date
        ? birthdayValue.getTime()
        : String(birthdayValue || "");

    if (
      !birthdayContextCache[
        birthdayCacheKey
      ]
    ) {
      birthdayContextCache[
        birthdayCacheKey
      ] =
        birthdayContextForToday_(
          birthdayValue
        );
    }

    var birthday =
      birthdayContextCache[
        birthdayCacheKey
      ];

    var key = period + "||" + classTitle;

    if (!groups[key]) {
      groups[key] = {
        key: key,
        period: period,
        classTitle: classTitle,
        label: period + " Period — " + classTitle,
        classImageUrl: "",
        students: []
      };

      order.push(key);
    }

    groups[key].students.push({
      rowNumber: i + 2,
      firstName: firstName,
      lastName: lastName,
      name: firstName,
      fullName: (firstName + " " + lastName).trim(),
      id: studentId,
      imageUrl: "",
      birthdayObservedToday:
        !!birthday.observedToday,
      birthdayMessage:
        String(
          birthday.message || ""
        ),
      birthdayAge:
        birthday.age,
      passRestricted:
        studentPassRestricted_(
          passStatusValues[i] ? passStatusValues[i][0] : ""
        ),
      commuter:
        /^\s*y/i.test(commuterValues[i] ? commuterValues[i][0] : "")
    });
  }

  order.forEach(function(key) {
    var firstNameCounts = {};

    groups[key].students.forEach(function(student) {
      var countKey = student.firstName.toLowerCase();
      firstNameCounts[countKey] =
        (firstNameCounts[countKey] || 0) + 1;
    });

    groups[key].students.forEach(function(student) {
      var countKey = student.firstName.toLowerCase();
      var needsInitial = firstNameCounts[countKey] > 1;

      student.displayName = student.firstName;

      if (needsInitial && student.lastName) {
        student.displayName += " " +
          student.lastName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() +
          ".";
      }
    });

    groups[key].students.sort(function(a, b) {
      var firstCompare =
        a.firstName.localeCompare(b.firstName);

      if (firstCompare !== 0) {
        return firstCompare;
      }

      return a.lastName.localeCompare(b.lastName);
    });
  });

  return order.map(function(key) {
    return groups[key];
  });
}


function getDeferredRosterImages(options) {
  options = options || {};

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var studentSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Students");
  var classSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Classes");

  var result = {
    studentImages: [],
    classImages: {},
    classImageSettings: {}
  };

  // Student photos now load in small batches through getStudentPhotos().
  if (
    !options.skipStudentPhotos &&
    studentSheet &&
    studentSheet.getLastRow() >= 2
  ) {
    var rowCount = studentSheet.getLastRow() - 1;
    var photoRange =
      studentSheet.getRange(2, kioskStudentColumns_(studentSheet).photo, rowCount, 1);

    var photoValues = photoRange.getValues();
    var photoFormulas = photoRange.getFormulas();

    for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
      var imageUrl =
        getImageData_(
          photoValues[i][0],
          photoFormulas[i][0]
        );

      if (imageUrl) {
        result.studentImages.push({
          rowNumber: i + 2,
          imageUrl: imageUrl
        });
      }
    }
  }

  if (classSheet && classSheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    // Existing lightweight class images load with the deferred roster,
    // preserving the behavior that worked before the uploader was added.
    // DRIVEFILE uploads are skipped here and loaded independently.
    result.classImages =
      getClassImageStartupMap_(
        classSheet
      );

    result.classImageSettings =
      getClassImageSettingsMap_(
        classSheet
      );
  }

  return result;
}




// Returns photos for just the requested student rows (one small batch).
// In-cell photos are downloaded here and sent as image data, because the
// links Google gives for in-cell images expire after a short time.
// Result: { "<rowNumber>": "<image data or URL>" }. Rows with no photo are
// simply left out.
function getStudentPhotos(rowNumbers) {
  var result = {};
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Students");

  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return result;
  }

  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();

  var rows = (rowNumbers || [])
    .map(Number)
    .filter(function(row) {
      return isFinite(row) && row >= 2 && row <= lastRow;
    });

  if (!rows.length) {
    return result;
  }

  var minRow = Math.min.apply(null, rows);
  var maxRow = Math.max.apply(null, rows);

  var range = sheet.getRange(minRow, kioskStudentColumns_(sheet).photo, maxRow - minRow + 1, 1);
  var values = range.getValues();
  var formulas = range.getFormulas();

  var fetchRequests = [];
  var fetchItems = [];

  rows.forEach(function(row) {
    var value = values[row - minRow][0];
    var formula = formulas[row - minRow][0];

    if (typeof value === "string" && /^DRIVEFILE:/i.test(value.trim())) {
      var driveData = getDriveFileDataUrl_(
        value.trim().replace(/^DRIVEFILE:/i, "")
      );

      if (driveData) {
        result[row] = driveData;
      }

      return;
    }

    var contentUrl = "";

    try {
      if (
        value &&
        typeof value === "object" &&
        typeof value.getContentUrl === "function"
      ) {
        contentUrl = String(value.getContentUrl() || "").trim();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      contentUrl = "";
    }

    if (contentUrl) {
      fetchRequests.push({
        url: contentUrl,
        muteHttpExceptions: true,
        followRedirects: true
      });

      fetchItems.push({
        row: row,
        contentUrl: contentUrl
      });

      return;
    }

    var url = getImageData_(value, formula);

    if (url) {
      result[row] = url;
    }
  });

  if (fetchRequests.length) {
    var responses = [];

    try {
      // Downloads the whole batch at the same time instead of one by one.
      responses = UrlFetchApp.fetchAll(fetchRequests);
    } catch (fetchErr) {
      responses = [];
    }

    fetchItems.forEach(function(item, index) {
      var dataUrl = "";

      try {
        var response = responses[index];

        if (response && response.getResponseCode() === 200) {
          var blob = response.getBlob();
          var contentType = String(blob.getContentType() || "image/jpeg");

          if (/^image\//i.test(contentType)) {
            dataUrl =
              "data:" +
              contentType +
              ";base64," +
              Utilities.base64Encode(blob.getBytes());
          }
        }
      } catch (readErr) {
        dataUrl = "";
      }

      // If the download did not work, fall back to the temporary link,
      // which is what the app used before.
      result[item.row] = dataUrl || item.contentUrl;
    });
  }

  return result;
}

// Run this once from the Apps Script editor (select it, click Run) to give
// the app permission to download in-cell student photos.
function authorizeStudentPhotoLoading() {
  UrlFetchApp.fetch("https://www.google.com", {
    muteHttpExceptions: true
  });

  return "Student photo loading is authorized.";
}


function normalizeClassImagePeriod_(value) {
  var text =
    String(value || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(
        /\bperiod\b/g,
        ""
      )
      .replace(
        /\s+/g,
        ""
      )
      .trim();

  return text.replace(
    /^(\d+)(st|nd|rd|th)$/i,
    "$1"
  );
}

function normalizeClassImageTitle_(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(
      /\s+/g,
      " "
    )
    .trim();
}

function classImageLookupKey_(
  period,
  classTitle
) {
  return (
    normalizeClassImagePeriod_(
      period
    ) +
    "||" +
    normalizeClassImageTitle_(
      classTitle
    )
  );
}

function classImagePeriodKey_(period) {
  return (
    "__PERIOD__||" +
    normalizeClassImagePeriod_(
      period
    )
  );
}




function getClassImageStartupMap_(sheet) {
  var result = {};

  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return result;
  }

  var rowCount =
    sheet.getLastRow() - 1;

  var classData =
    sheet
      .getRange(
        2,
        1,
        rowCount,
        2
      )
      .getValues();

  var imageRange =
    sheet.getRange(
      2,
      3,
      rowCount,
      1
    );

  var imageValues =
    imageRange.getValues();

  var imageFormulas =
    imageRange.getFormulas();

  var periodCounts = {};

  for (var countIndex = 0; countIndex < rowCount; countIndex++) {
    var countPeriod =
      normalizeClassImagePeriod_(
        classData[countIndex][0]
      );

    if (!countPeriod) continue;

    periodCounts[countPeriod] =
      (periodCounts[countPeriod] || 0) + 1;
  }

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    var period =
      String(
        classData[i][0] || ""
      ).trim();

    var classTitle =
      String(
        classData[i][1] || ""
      ).trim();

    if (!period || !classTitle) {
      continue;
    }

    var key =
      period +
      "||" +
      classTitle;

    var normalizedKey =
      classImageLookupKey_(
        period,
        classTitle
      );

    var periodKey =
      classImagePeriodKey_(
        period
      );

    var rawValue =
      imageValues[i][0];

    // Uploaded Drive files are loaded individually so they cannot bloat
    // the shared startup payload.
    if (
      typeof rawValue ===
        "string" &&
      /^DRIVEFILE:/i.test(
        rawValue.trim()
      )
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    var imageData =
      getClassImageData_(
        rawValue,
        imageFormulas[i][0]
      );

    result[key] =
      imageData;

    result[normalizedKey] =
      imageData;

    var normalizedPeriod =
      normalizeClassImagePeriod_(
        period
      );

    if (
      normalizedPeriod &&
      periodCounts[normalizedPeriod] === 1
    ) {
      result[periodKey] =
        imageData;
    }
  }

  return result;
}

function getClassImageMap_(sheet) {
  var result = {};

  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return result;
  }

  var rowCount = sheet.getLastRow() - 1;

  var classData = sheet.getRange(2, 1, rowCount, 2).getValues();

  var imageRange = sheet.getRange(2, 3, rowCount, 1);
  var imageValues = imageRange.getValues();
  var imageFormulas = imageRange.getFormulas();

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    var period = String(classData[i][0] || "").trim();
    var classTitle = String(classData[i][1] || "").trim();

    if (!period || !classTitle) {
      continue;
    }

    var key = period + "||" + classTitle;

    result[key] = getClassImageData_(
      imageValues[i][0],
      imageFormulas[i][0]
    );
  }

  return result;
}


function getClassImageSettingsMap_(sheet) {
  var result = {};

  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return result;
  }

  var rowCount =
    sheet.getLastRow() - 1;

  var identity =
    sheet
      .getRange(
        2,
        1,
        rowCount,
        2
      )
      .getDisplayValues();

  var settings =
    sheet
      .getRange(
        2,
        4,
        rowCount,
        3
      )
      .getValues();

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    var period =
      String(
        identity[i][0] || ""
      ).trim();

    var classTitle =
      String(
        identity[i][1] || ""
      ).trim();

    if (!period || !classTitle) {
      continue;
    }

    var zoom =
      Number(settings[i][0]);

    var focusX =
      Number(settings[i][1]);

    var focusY =
      Number(settings[i][2]);

    if (!isFinite(zoom)) {
      zoom = 1;
    }

    if (!isFinite(focusX)) {
      focusX = 50;
    }

    if (!isFinite(focusY)) {
      focusY = 50;
    }

    var settingsValue = {
      zoom:
        Math.max(
          1,
          Math.min(
            3,
            zoom
          )
        ),
      focusX:
        Math.max(
          0,
          Math.min(
            100,
            focusX
          )
        ),
      focusY:
        Math.max(
          0,
          Math.min(
            100,
            focusY
          )
        )
    };

    var exactKey =
      period +
      "||" +
      classTitle;

    var normalizedKey =
      classImageLookupKey_(
        period,
        classTitle
      );

    result[exactKey] =
      settingsValue;

    result[normalizedKey] =
      settingsValue;
  }

  return result;
}


function getClassImageData_(cellValue, formula) {
  return getImageData_(cellValue, formula);
}

function getImageData_(cellValue, formula) {
  if (typeof cellValue === "string") {
    var directText = cellValue.trim();

    if (/^DRIVEFILE:/i.test(directText)) {
      return getDriveFileDataUrl_(directText.split(":")[1]);
    }
  }

  // In-cell Google Sheets images already expose a usable temporary content
  // URL. Returning that URL directly is dramatically faster than downloading
  // and base64-encoding every student photo during every roster load.
  try {
    if (
      cellValue &&
      typeof cellValue === "object" &&
      typeof cellValue.getContentUrl === "function"
    ) {
      return String(
        cellValue.getContentUrl() || ""
      ).trim();
    }
  } catch (err) {}

  var formulaUrl =
    extractImageFormulaUrl_(formula);

  if (formulaUrl) {
    return normalizeImageUrl_(
      formulaUrl
    );
  }

  if (typeof cellValue === "string") {
    var text = cellValue.trim();

    if (text) {
      return normalizeImageUrl_(
        text
      );
    }
  }

  return "";
}

function getDriveFileDataUrl_(fileId) {
  try {
    var file = DriveApp.getFileById(fileId);
    var blob = file.getBlob();
    var contentType = blob.getContentType() || "image/png";

    return "data:" +
      contentType +
      ";base64," +
      Utilities.base64Encode(blob.getBytes());
  } catch (err) {
    return "";
  }
}

function getImageUrlFromCell_(cellValue, formula) {
  try {
    if (
      cellValue &&
      typeof cellValue === "object" &&
      typeof cellValue.getContentUrl === "function"
    ) {
      return cellValue.getContentUrl();
    }
  } catch (err) {
  }

  var formulaUrl = extractImageFormulaUrl_(formula);

  if (formulaUrl) {
    return normalizeImageUrl_(formulaUrl);
  }

  if (typeof cellValue === "string") {
    var text = cellValue.trim();

    if (text) {
      return normalizeImageUrl_(text);
    }
  }

  return "";
}

function extractImageFormulaUrl_(formula) {
  formula = String(formula || "").trim();

  if (!formula) {
    return "";
  }

  var match = formula.match(
    /^=IMAGE\s*\(\s*["']([^"']+)["']/i
  );

  return match && match[1] ? match[1] : "";
}

function normalizeImageUrl_(url) {
  url = String(url || "").trim();

  if (!url) {
    return "";
  }

  var driveMatch = url.match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);

  if (!driveMatch) {
    driveMatch = url.match(/[?&]id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
  }

  if (driveMatch && driveMatch[1]) {
    return "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/" +
      driveMatch[1] +
      "=s500";
  }

  return url;
}



function ensureClassImageColumns_(sheet) {
  if (!sheet) return;

  var headers = [
    "Image Zoom",
    "Image Focus X",
    "Image Focus Y"
  ];

  for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) {
    var column =
      4 + i;

    if (
      !String(
        sheet
          .getRange(1, column)
          .getDisplayValue() || ""
      ).trim()
    ) {
      sheet
        .getRange(1, column)
        .setValue(
          headers[i]
        );
    }
  }
}


function getClassImageForDisplay(
  period,
  classTitle
) {
  var ss =
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  var sheet =
    ss.getSheetByName(
      "Classes"
    );

  if (
    !sheet ||
    sheet.getLastRow() < 2
  ) {
    return {
      imageUrl: ""
    };
  }

  var rowCount =
    sheet.getLastRow() - 1;

  var identity =
    sheet
      .getRange(
        2,
        1,
        rowCount,
        2
      )
      .getDisplayValues();

  var wantedKey =
    classImageLookupKey_(
      period,
      classTitle
    );

  var wantedPeriod =
    normalizeClassImagePeriod_(
      period
    );

  var exactRow = -1;
  var periodMatches = [];

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    var rowKey =
      classImageLookupKey_(
        identity[i][0],
        identity[i][1]
      );

    if (
      rowKey ===
      wantedKey
    ) {
      exactRow = i + 2;
      break;
    }

    if (
      normalizeClassImagePeriod_(
        identity[i][0]
      ) ===
      wantedPeriod
    ) {
      periodMatches.push(
        i + 2
      );
    }
  }

  var rowNumber =
    exactRow >= 2
      ? exactRow
      : (
          periodMatches.length === 1
            ? periodMatches[0]
            : -1
        );

  if (rowNumber < 2) {
    return {
      imageUrl: "",
      matchType:
        periodMatches.length > 1
          ? "AMBIGUOUS_PERIOD"
          : "NO_MATCH"
    };
  }

  var imageCell =
    sheet.getRange(
      rowNumber,
      3
    );

  var rawValue =
    imageCell.getValue();

  var imageUrl =
    getClassImageData_(
      rawValue,
      imageCell.getFormula()
    ) || "";

  return {
    imageUrl:
      imageUrl,
    sourceType:
      (
        typeof rawValue === "string" &&
        /^DRIVEFILE:/i.test(
          rawValue.trim()
        )
      )
        ? "DRIVEFILE"
        : "STANDARD",
    matchType:
      exactRow >= 2
        ? "EXACT"
        : "PERIOD_ONLY",
    rowNumber:
      rowNumber
  };
}

function getClassImageEditorOptions() {
  var ss =
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  var sheet =
    ss.getSheetByName(
      "Classes"
    );

  if (
    !sheet ||
    sheet.getLastRow() < 2
  ) {
    return [];
  }

  ensureClassImageColumns_(
    sheet
  );

  var rowCount =
    sheet.getLastRow() - 1;

  var identity =
    sheet
      .getRange(
        2,
        1,
        rowCount,
        2
      )
      .getDisplayValues();

  var imageRange =
    sheet.getRange(
      2,
      3,
      rowCount,
      1
    );

  var imageValues =
    imageRange.getValues();

  var imageFormulas =
    imageRange.getFormulas();

  var cropSettings =
    sheet
      .getRange(
        2,
        4,
        rowCount,
        3
      )
      .getValues();

  var options = [];

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    var period =
      String(
        identity[i][0] || ""
      ).trim();

    var classTitle =
      String(
        identity[i][1] || ""
      ).trim();

    if (!period || !classTitle) {
      continue;
    }

    var imageUrl =
      getClassImageData_(
        imageValues[i][0],
        imageFormulas[i][0]
      );

    var zoom =
      Number(
        cropSettings[i][0]
      );

    var focusX =
      Number(
        cropSettings[i][1]
      );

    var focusY =
      Number(
        cropSettings[i][2]
      );

    if (!isFinite(zoom)) {
      zoom = 1;
    }

    if (!isFinite(focusX)) {
      focusX = 50;
    }

    if (!isFinite(focusY)) {
      focusY = 50;
    }

    options.push({
      rowNumber:
        i + 2,
      key:
        period +
        "||" +
        classTitle,
      period:
        period,
      classTitle:
        classTitle,
      hasImage:
        !!imageUrl,
      imageUrl:
        imageUrl || "",
      zoom:
        Math.max(
          1,
          Math.min(
            3,
            zoom
          )
        ),
      focusX:
        Math.max(
          0,
          Math.min(
            100,
            focusX
          )
        ),
      focusY:
        Math.max(
          0,
          Math.min(
            100,
            focusY
          )
        )
    });
  }

  return options;
}

function getClassImageUploadFolder_() {
  var folderName =
    "Hall Pass Kiosk Class Images";

  var folders =
    DriveApp.getFoldersByName(
      folderName
    );

  if (folders.hasNext()) {
    return folders.next();
  }

  return DriveApp.createFolder(
    folderName
  );
}

function normalizeClassImageCrop_(crop) {
  crop = crop || {};

  var zoom =
    Number(crop.zoom);

  var focusX =
    Number(crop.focusX);

  var focusY =
    Number(crop.focusY);

  if (!isFinite(zoom)) {
    zoom = 1;
  }

  if (!isFinite(focusX)) {
    focusX = 50;
  }

  if (!isFinite(focusY)) {
    focusY = 50;
  }

  return {
    zoom:
      Math.max(
        1,
        Math.min(
          3,
          zoom
        )
      ),
    focusX:
      Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(
          100,
          focusX
        )
      ),
    focusY:
      Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(
          100,
          focusY
        )
      )
  };
}

function saveClassImageCrop__write_(
  rowNumber,
  crop
) {
  rowNumber =
    Number(rowNumber);

  if (
    !isFinite(rowNumber) ||
    rowNumber < 2
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Choose a class first."
    );
  }

  var ss =
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  var sheet =
    ss.getSheetByName(
      "Classes"
    );

  if (
    !sheet ||
    rowNumber >
      sheet.getLastRow()
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "That class row could not be found."
    );
  }

  var period =
    String(
      sheet
        .getRange(
          rowNumber,
          1
        )
        .getDisplayValue() || ""
    ).trim();

  var classTitle =
    String(
      sheet
        .getRange(
          rowNumber,
          2
        )
        .getDisplayValue() || ""
    ).trim();

  if (!period || !classTitle) {
    throw new Error(
      "That row does not contain a class."
    );
  }

  var imageCell =
    sheet.getRange(
      rowNumber,
      3
    );

  var imageUrl =
    getClassImageData_(
      imageCell.getValue(),
      imageCell.getFormula()
    );

  if (!imageUrl) {
    throw new Error(
      "Upload a class image first."
    );
  }

  ensureClassImageColumns_(
    sheet
  );

  var normalized =
    normalizeClassImageCrop_(
      crop
    );

  sheet
    .getRange(
      rowNumber,
      4,
      1,
      3
    )
    .setValues(
      [[
        normalized.zoom,
        normalized.focusX,
        normalized.focusY
      ]]
    );

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    rowNumber:
      rowNumber,
    imageUrl:
      imageUrl,
    zoom:
      normalized.zoom,
    focusX:
      normalized.focusX,
    focusY:
      normalized.focusY
  };
}

function saveClassImageUpload__write_(
  rowNumber,
  dataUrl,
  originalFileName,
  crop
) {
  rowNumber =
    Number(rowNumber);

  if (
    !isFinite(rowNumber) ||
    rowNumber < 2
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Choose a class first."
    );
  }

  var match =
    String(dataUrl || "")
      .match(
        /^data:(image\/(?:png|jpeg|webp|gif));base64,(.+)$/i
      );

  if (!match) {
    throw new Error(
      "Choose a JPG, PNG, WEBP, or GIF image."
    );
  }

  var ss =
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  var sheet =
    ss.getSheetByName(
      "Classes"
    );

  if (
    !sheet ||
    rowNumber >
      sheet.getLastRow()
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "That class row could not be found."
    );
  }

  var period =
    String(
      sheet
        .getRange(
          rowNumber,
          1
        )
        .getDisplayValue() || ""
    ).trim();

  var classTitle =
    String(
      sheet
        .getRange(
          rowNumber,
          2
        )
        .getDisplayValue() || ""
    ).trim();

  if (!period || !classTitle) {
    throw new Error(
      "That row does not contain a class."
    );
  }

  ensureClassImageColumns_(
    sheet
  );

  var normalized =
    normalizeClassImageCrop_(
      crop
    );

  var mimeType =
    String(match[1])
      .toLowerCase();

  var extension = ".jpg";

  if (mimeType === "image/png") {
    extension = ".png";
  } else if (
    mimeType === "image/webp"
  ) {
    extension = ".webp";
  } else if (
    mimeType === "image/gif"
  ) {
    extension = ".gif";
  }

  var safeBase =
    (
      period +
      "_" +
      classTitle
    )
      .replace(
        /[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]+/g,
        "_"
      )
      .replace(
        /^_+|_+$/g,
        ""
      );

  if (!safeBase) {
    safeBase =
      "class";
  }

  var blob =
    Utilities.newBlob(
      Utilities.base64Decode(
        match[2]
      ),
      mimeType,
      safeBase +
      extension
    );

  var folder =
    getClassImageUploadFolder_();

  var file =
    folder.createFile(
      blob
    );

  var imageCell =
    sheet.getRange(
      rowNumber,
      3
    );

  var oldValue =
    imageCell.getValue();

  var oldDriveFileId = "";

  if (
    typeof oldValue ===
    "string"
  ) {
    var oldText =
      oldValue.trim();

    if (
      /^DRIVEFILE:/i.test(
        oldText
      )
    ) {
      oldDriveFileId =
        oldText.split(":")[1] ||
        "";
    }
  }

  imageCell.setValue(
    "DRIVEFILE:" +
    file.getId()
  );

  sheet
    .getRange(
      rowNumber,
      4,
      1,
      3
    )
    .setValues(
      [[
        normalized.zoom,
        normalized.focusX,
        normalized.focusY
      ]]
    );

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  if (oldDriveFileId) {
    try {
      DriveApp
        .getFileById(
          oldDriveFileId
        )
        .setTrashed(
          true
        );
    } catch (oldFileError) {}
  }

  return {
    rowNumber:
      rowNumber,
    imageUrl:
      getDriveFileDataUrl_(
        file.getId()
      ),
    zoom:
      normalized.zoom,
    focusX:
      normalized.focusX,
    focusY:
      normalized.focusY,
    isGif:
      mimeType ===
      "image/gif"
  };
}

function removeClassImage__write_(
  rowNumber
) {
  rowNumber =
    Number(rowNumber);

  if (
    !isFinite(rowNumber) ||
    rowNumber < 2
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Choose a class first."
    );
  }

  var ss =
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  var sheet =
    ss.getSheetByName(
      "Classes"
    );

  if (
    !sheet ||
    rowNumber >
      sheet.getLastRow()
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "That class row could not be found."
    );
  }

  var imageCell =
    sheet.getRange(
      rowNumber,
      3
    );

  var oldValue =
    imageCell.getValue();

  var oldDriveFileId = "";

  if (
    typeof oldValue ===
    "string"
  ) {
    var oldText =
      oldValue.trim();

    if (
      /^DRIVEFILE:/i.test(
        oldText
      )
    ) {
      oldDriveFileId =
        oldText.split(":")[1] ||
        "";
    }
  }

  imageCell.clearContent();

  ensureClassImageColumns_(
    sheet
  );

  sheet
    .getRange(
      rowNumber,
      4,
      1,
      3
    )
    .clearContent();

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  if (oldDriveFileId) {
    try {
      DriveApp
        .getFileById(
          oldDriveFileId
        )
        .setTrashed(
          true
        );
    } catch (oldFileError) {}
  }

  return {
    rowNumber:
      rowNumber,
    removed:
      true
  };
}


function getStudentPhotoOptions() {
  var roster = getStudentRoster();
  var options = [];

  roster.forEach(function(group) {
    group.students.forEach(function(student) {
      options.push({
        rowNumber: Number(student.rowNumber),
        period: group.period,
        classTitle: group.classTitle,
        classLabel: group.label,
        displayName:
          student.displayName ||
          student.firstName ||
          student.name ||
          "",
        fullName:
          student.fullName ||
          student.name ||
          "",
        hasPhoto: !!student.imageUrl,
        imageUrl: student.imageUrl || ""
      });
    });
  });

  return options;
}

function getStudentPhotoUploadFolder_() {
  var folderName = "Hall Pass Kiosk Student Photos";
  var folders = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(folderName);

  if (folders.hasNext()) {
    return folders.next();
  }

  return DriveApp.createFolder(folderName);
}

function saveStudentPhotoUpload__write_(rowNumber, dataUrl, originalFileName) {
  rowNumber = Number(rowNumber);

  if (!isFinite(rowNumber) || rowNumber < 2) {
    throw new Error("Choose a student first.");
  }

  var match = String(dataUrl || "").match(
    /^data:(image\/[a-zA-Z0-9.+-]+);base64,(.+)$/
  );

  if (!match) {
    throw new Error("Could not read that image.");
  }

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Students");

  if (!sheet || rowNumber > sheet.getLastRow()) {
    throw new Error("That student row could not be found.");
  }

  var firstName = String(
    sheet.getRange(rowNumber, kioskStudentColumns_(sheet).first).getDisplayValue() || ""
  ).trim();

  var lastName = String(
    sheet.getRange(rowNumber, kioskStudentColumns_(sheet).last).getDisplayValue() || ""
  ).trim();

  if (!firstName) {
    throw new Error(
      "That student row does not contain a student."
    );
  }

  var fullName =
    (firstName + " " + lastName).trim();

  var photoCell =
    sheet.getRange(rowNumber, kioskStudentColumns_(sheet).photo);

  try {
    // Store the already-cropped image itself in the spreadsheet cell.
    // This travels with copies of the spreadsheet instead of pointing
    // back to a separate Drive file.
    var cellImage =
      SpreadsheetApp
        .newCellImage()
        .setSourceUrl(String(dataUrl))
        .setAltTextTitle(
          fullName + " student photo"
        )
        .setAltTextDescription(
          "Cropped student photo for " +
          fullName
        )
        .build();

    photoCell.setValue(cellImage);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();

    var storedValue =
      photoCell.getValue();

    var storedUrl = "";

    try {
      if (
        storedValue &&
        typeof storedValue.getContentUrl ===
          "function"
      ) {
        storedUrl =
          storedValue.getContentUrl();
      }
    } catch (urlErr) {
      storedUrl = "";
    }

    return {
      rowNumber: rowNumber,
      imageUrl:
        storedUrl || String(dataUrl),
      storageMode: "IN_CELL"
    };

  } catch (cellImageError) {
    // Compatibility fallback: if Google's in-cell image API rejects
    // the image for any reason, keep the existing private-Drive method
    // so the photo is not lost.
    var safeBase =
      (firstName + "_" + lastName)
        .replace(
          /[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]+/g,
          "_"
        )
        .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");

    if (!safeBase) {
      safeBase = "student";
    }

    var extension = ".jpg";

    if (match[1] === "image/png") {
      extension = ".png";
    }

    if (match[1] === "image/webp") {
      extension = ".webp";
    }

    var blob =
      Utilities.newBlob(
        Utilities.base64Decode(
          match[2]
        ),
        match[1],
        safeBase + extension
      );

    var folder =
      getStudentPhotoUploadFolder_();

    var file =
      folder.createFile(blob);

    photoCell.setValue(
      "DRIVEFILE:" + file.getId()
    );

    SpreadsheetApp.flush();

    return {
      rowNumber: rowNumber,
      imageUrl:
        getDriveFileDataUrl_(
          file.getId()
        ),
      storageMode: "DRIVE_FALLBACK",
      warning:
        "Google Sheets could not embed this image in the cell, so it was saved privately in Drive instead."
    };
  }
}


function convertExistingStudentPhotosToInCell__write_() {
  var ss =
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  var sheet =
    ss.getSheetByName("Students");

  if (
    !sheet ||
    sheet.getLastRow() < 2
  ) {
    return {
      converted: 0,
      skipped: 0,
      failed: 0
    };
  }

  var lastRow =
    sheet.getLastRow();

  var range =
    sheet.getRange(
      2,
      kioskStudentColumns_(sheet).photo,
      lastRow - 1,
      1
    );

  var values =
    range.getValues();

  var converted = 0;
  var skipped = 0;
  var failed = 0;

  for (
    var i = 0;
    i < values.length;
    i++
  ) {
    var value =
      values[i][0];

    if (
      typeof value !== "string" ||
      !/^DRIVEFILE:/i.test(
        value.trim()
      )
    ) {
      skipped++;
      continue;
    }

    var fileId =
      value
        .trim()
        .replace(
          /^DRIVEFILE:/i,
          ""
        );

    try {
      var dataUrl =
        getDriveFileDataUrl_(
          fileId
        );

      if (!dataUrl) {
        failed++;
        continue;
      }

      var firstName =
        String(
          sheet
            .getRange(
              i + 2,
              3
            )
            .getDisplayValue() ||
          ""
        ).trim();

      var lastName =
        String(
          sheet
            .getRange(
              i + 2,
              4
            )
            .getDisplayValue() ||
          ""
        ).trim();

      var fullName =
        (
          firstName +
          " " +
          lastName
        ).trim();

      var cellImage =
        SpreadsheetApp
          .newCellImage()
          .setSourceUrl(
            dataUrl
          )
          .setAltTextTitle(
            fullName
              ? fullName +
                " student photo"
              : "Student photo"
          )
          .setAltTextDescription(
            fullName
              ? "Cropped student photo for " +
                fullName
              : "Cropped student photo"
          )
          .build();

      sheet
        .getRange(
          i + 2,
          6
        )
        .setValue(
          cellImage
        );

      converted++;

    } catch (err) {
      failed++;
    }
  }

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    converted: converted,
    skipped: skipped,
    failed: failed
  };
}

    "Categories mirror the final approved master icon banks."
  );
  sheet.getRange("C1").setNote(
    "Each collection contains the same 79 icon names so styles can be switched without changing the icon."
  );

  return sheet;
}

function getDestinationIconOptions_() {
  var bank = ensureDestinationIconBankSheet_();
  var count = Math.max(bank.getLastRow() - 1, 0);
  if (!count) return [];
  return bank.getRange(2, 1, count, 6).getDisplayValues().map(function(row) {
    return {
      collection: String(row[0] || "").trim(),
      category: String(row[1] || "").trim(),
      icon: String(row[2] || "").trim(),
      search: String(row[3] || "").trim(),
      suggestedUse: String(row[4] || "").trim(),
      style: String(row[5] || "").trim()
    };
  }).filter(function(item) {
    return item.collection && item.category && item.icon;
  });
}

function uniqueSortedDestinationValues_(values) {
  var seen = {};
  values.forEach(function(value) {
    value = String(value || "").trim();
    if (value) seen[value] = true;
  });
  return Object.keys(seen).sort(function(a, b) { return a.localeCompare(b); });
}

function applyDestinationDependentValidationForRow_(sheet, rowNumber, options) {
  options = options || getDestinationIconOptions_();

  var dc = kioskHeadingMap_(sheet, KIOSK_DEST_COLS_).cols;      // by heading, so the columns can be moved
  var collectionCell = sheet.getRange(rowNumber, dc[1] + 1);
  var categoryCell = sheet.getRange(rowNumber, dc[2] + 1);
  var iconCell = sheet.getRange(rowNumber, dc[3] + 1);

  // Start each row clean so stale rules from an older layout cannot survive.
  collectionCell.clearDataValidations();
  categoryCell.clearDataValidations();
  iconCell.clearDataValidations();

  var collections = uniqueSortedDestinationValues_(options.map(function(item) {
    return item.collection;
  }));

  collectionCell.setDataValidation(
    SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation()
      .requireValueInList(collections, true)
      .setAllowInvalid(false)
      .setHelpText("Choose an icon collection.")
      .build()
  );

  var collection = String(collectionCell.getDisplayValue() || "").trim();
  var categories = uniqueSortedDestinationValues_(options.filter(function(item) {
    return item.collection === collection;
  }).map(function(item) {
    return item.category;
  }));

  if (categories.length) {
    categoryCell.setDataValidation(
      SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation()
        .requireValueInList(categories, true)
        .setAllowInvalid(false)
        .setHelpText("Choose a category from the selected collection.")
        .build()
    );
  } else {
    categoryCell.clearDataValidations();
  }

  var category = String(categoryCell.getDisplayValue() || "").trim();
  var icons = uniqueSortedDestinationValues_(options.filter(function(item) {
    return item.collection === collection && item.category === category;
  }).map(function(item) {
    return item.icon;
  }));

  if (icons.length) {
    iconCell.setDataValidation(
      SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation()
        .requireValueInList(icons, true)
        .setAllowInvalid(false)
        .setHelpText("Choose an icon from the selected collection and category.")
        .build()
    );
  } else {
    iconCell.clearDataValidations();
  }
}


function normalizePersonalDestinationName_(destination) {
  var raw = String(destination || "").trim();
  var key = raw.toLowerCase();

  var aliases = {
    "restroom": "Bathroom",
    "bathroom": "Bathroom",
    "water fountain": "Water",
    "water refill station": "Water",
    "water": "Water",
    "counselor / student services": "Counselor",
    "counselor": "Counselor",
    "library / media center": "Library",
    "library": "Library",
    "locker": "Locker",
    "ap office": "AP Office",
    "dean's office": "AP Office",
    "deans office": "AP Office",
    "nurse / health office": "Nurse",
    "nurse": "Nurse",
    "technology help": "Chromebook Help Desk",
    "chromebook help desk": "Chromebook Help Desk",
    "bookstore / school store": "Bookstore",
    "bookstore": "Bookstore",
    "main office": "Main Office",
    "printer": "Printer",
    "printer / copier": "Printer",
    "copy room": "Printer",
    "cafeteria": "Cafeteria",
    "lunchroom": "Cafeteria",
    "lost & found": "Lost & Found",
    "lost and found": "Lost & Found",
    "back to my last class": "Back to my last class",
    "other": "Other"
  };

  return aliases[key] || raw;
}

function upgradeDestinationIconSheets__write_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Destinations");
  if (!sheet) sheet = ss.insertSheet("Destinations");

  var requiredDestinations = [
    "Bathroom",
    "Water",
    "Counselor",
    "Library",
    "Locker",
    "AP Office",
    "Nurse",
    "Chromebook Help Desk",
    "Bookstore",
    "Main Office",
    "Printer",
    "Cafeteria",
    "Lost & Found",
    "Back to my last class",
    "Other"
  ];

  var existing = [];
  if (sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    existing = kioskRowsByHeading_(sheet, KIOSK_DEST_COLS_, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  }

  // Preserve any user-created destinations, but ALWAYS restore the user's
  // required destination set. A previous migration could otherwise leave
  // only one destination (for example Bathroom).
  var existingByName = {};
  var destinationNames = [];

  existing.forEach(function(row) {
    var normalized = normalizePersonalDestinationName_(row[0]);
    if (!normalized) return;

    var key = normalized.toLowerCase();

    if (!existingByName[key]) {
      existingByName[key] = row;
      destinationNames.push(normalized);
    }
  });

  requiredDestinations.forEach(function(destination) {
    var key = destination.toLowerCase();
    if (!existingByName[key]) {
      existingByName[key] = null;
      destinationNames.push(destination);
    }
  });

  // Put the required pass destinations first, in the exact order requested.
  var requiredKeys = {};
  requiredDestinations.forEach(function(name, index) {
    requiredKeys[name.toLowerCase()] = index;
  });

  destinationNames.sort(function(a, b) {
    var aKey = a.toLowerCase();
    var bKey = b.toLowerCase();

    var aRequired = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(requiredKeys, aKey);
    var bRequired = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(requiredKeys, bKey);

    if (aRequired && bRequired) {
      return requiredKeys[aKey] - requiredKeys[bKey];
    }

    if (aRequired) return -1;
    if (bRequired) return 1;

    return a.localeCompare(b);
  });

  var headers = [
    "Destination",
    "Collection",
    "Category",
    "Icon",
    "Enabled",
    "Notes"
  ];

  var rows = destinationNames.map(function(destination) {
    var key = destination.toLowerCase();
    var old = existingByName[key] || [];
    var choice = defaultDestinationIconChoice_(destination);

    // Required destinations are always ON after repair.
    var isRequired = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
      requiredKeys,
      key
    );

    var enabled = isRequired
      ? "ON"
      : (
          String(old[4] || old[2] || "").toUpperCase() === "OFF"
            ? "OFF"
            : "ON"
        );

    var notes = String(old[5] || "").trim();

    return [
      destination,
      choice[0],
      choice[1],
      choice[2],
      enabled,
      notes
    ];
  });

  // Clear ALL legacy validation rules, including rules on blank cells that
  // may sit outside getDataRange().
  sheet.getRange(
    1,
    1,
    sheet.getMaxRows(),
    sheet.getMaxColumns()
  ).clearDataValidations();
  sheet.clearContents();

  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length)
    .setValues([headers]);

  if (rows.length) {
    sheet.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, headers.length)
      .setValues(rows);
  }

  ensureDestinationIconBankSheet_();

  var options = getDestinationIconOptions_();
  var maxRows = Math.max(
    sheet.getMaxRows(),
    rows.length + 20
  );

  var collections = uniqueSortedDestinationValues_(
    options.map(function(item) {
      return item.collection;
    })
  );

  var collectionRule = SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation()
    .requireValueInList(collections, true)
    .setAllowInvalid(false)
    .setHelpText("Choose an icon collection.")
    .build();

  sheet.getRange(
    2,
    2,
    Math.max(maxRows - 1, 1),
    1
  ).setDataValidation(collectionRule);

  var enabledRule = SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation()
    .requireValueInList(["ON", "OFF"], true)
    .setAllowInvalid(false)
    .build();

  sheet.getRange(
    2,
    5,
    Math.max(maxRows - 1, 1),
    1
  ).setDataValidation(enabledRule);

  for (
    var r = 2;
    r <= Math.max(sheet.getLastRow(), 2);
    r++
  ) {
    applyDestinationDependentValidationForRow_(
      sheet,
      r,
      options
    );
  }

  sheet.getRange("A1:F1")
    .setFontWeight("bold")
    .setBackground("#17365D")
    .setFontColor("#FFFFFF");

  sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  sheet.autoResizeColumns(1, 6);

  return (
    "Repaired Destinations. Restored all 12 required pass destinations " +
    "and preset them to the approved Doodle collection."
  );
}

// Destinations tab column G: does the kiosk ask "Did you get a pass?" when a
// student leaves for this place (a pass delivered to class, e.g. from the
// counselor or an assistant principal)? Yes for Counselor and AP Office to
// start; never needed for places like the bathroom.
var KIOSK_ASK_PASS_DEFAULT_ = ["counselor", "ap office"];

// Destinations tab column H: trips that can usually wait. Once a student is
// over the weekly limit, leaving for these during class needs the teacher PIN.
var KIOSK_OPTIONAL_TRIP_DEFAULT_ = ["water", "locker", "library", "printer", "bookstore", "cafeteria",
  "chromebook help desk", "lost & found", "other"];

function kioskEnsureOptionalTripColumn_(sheet) {
  try {
    var dm = kioskHeadingMap_(sheet, KIOSK_DEST_COLS_);
    if (dm.found[7]) return;      // already there, wherever the teacher put it
    var col = String(sheet.getRange(1, 8).getDisplayValue() || "").trim() ? dm.lastCol + 1 : 8;
    kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, col);
    sheet.getRange(1, col).setValue("Optional trip");
    var last = sheet.getLastRow();
    if (last < 2) return;
    var names = sheet.getRange(2, dm.cols[0] + 1, last - 1, 1).getDisplayValues();
    sheet.getRange(2, col, last - 1, 1).setValues(names.map(function(r) {
      return [KIOSK_OPTIONAL_TRIP_DEFAULT_.indexOf(String(r[0] || "").trim().toLowerCase()) !== -1 ? "Yes" : "No"];
    }));
    sheet.getRange(2, col, Math.max(sheet.getMaxRows() - 1, 1), 1).setDataValidation(
      SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().requireValueInList(["Yes", "No"], true).setAllowInvalid(false)
        .setHelpText("Yes = a trip that can usually wait. A student over the weekly limit needs the teacher PIN to leave for it during class.")
        .build());
  } catch (err) {}
}

function kioskEnsureAskPassColumn_(sheet) {
  try {
    var dm = kioskHeadingMap_(sheet, KIOSK_DEST_COLS_);
    if (dm.found[6]) return;      // already there, wherever the teacher put it
    var col = String(sheet.getRange(1, 7).getDisplayValue() || "").trim() ? dm.lastCol + 1 : 7;
    kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, col);
    sheet.getRange(1, col).setValue("Ask \"I got a pass\"");
    var last = sheet.getLastRow();
    if (last < 2) return;
    var names = sheet.getRange(2, dm.cols[0] + 1, last - 1, 1).getDisplayValues();
    sheet.getRange(2, col, last - 1, 1).setValues(names.map(function(r) {
      return [KIOSK_ASK_PASS_DEFAULT_.indexOf(String(r[0] || "").trim().toLowerCase()) !== -1 ? "Yes" : "No"];
    }));
    sheet.getRange(2, col, Math.max(sheet.getMaxRows() - 1, 1), 1).setDataValidation(
      SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation().requireValueInList(["Yes", "No"], true).setAllowInvalid(false)
        .setHelpText("Yes = when a student picks this place, the kiosk asks whether a pass was delivered to them. Those trips don't count toward their weekly minutes.")
        .build());
  } catch (err) {}
}

function getDestinationList__read_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Destinations");

  if (
    !sheet ||
    sheet.getLastColumn() < 6 ||
    !kioskHeadingMap_(sheet, KIOSK_DEST_COLS_).found[1]      // no "Collection" heading anywhere (moving it is fine)
  ) {
    upgradeDestinationIconSheets();
    sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Destinations");
  }

  var requiredDestinations = [
    "Bathroom",
    "Water",
    "Counselor",
    "Library",
    "Locker",
    "AP Office",
    "Nurse",
    "Chromebook Help Desk",
    "Bookstore",
    "Main Office",
    "Printer",
    "Cafeteria",
    "Lost & Found",
    "Back to my last class",
    "Other"
  ];

  var data = [];
  var byName = {};

  if (sheet && sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    kioskEnsureAskPassColumn_(sheet);
    kioskEnsureOptionalTripColumn_(sheet);

    data = kioskRowsByHeading_(sheet, KIOSK_DEST_COLS_, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);

    data.forEach(function(row) {
      var destination =
        normalizePersonalDestinationName_(row[0]);

      if (!destination) return;

      var key = destination.toLowerCase();

      byName[key] = {
        destination: destination,
        collection:
          String(row[1] || "").trim() ||
          "Doodle",
        category:
          String(row[2] || "").trim(),
        icon:
          String(row[3] || "").trim(),
        enabled:
          String(row[4] || "")
            .trim()
            .toUpperCase(),
        notes:
          String(row[5] || "").trim(),
        askPass:
          String(row[6] || "").trim(),
        optional:
          String(row[7] || "").trim()
      };
    });
  }

  // PERSONAL CLASSROOM BUILD:
  // Always return the complete required pass list even if the sheet was
  // partially migrated, filtered, or accidentally reduced to one row.
  var result = requiredDestinations.map(
    function(destination) {
      var key = destination.toLowerCase();
      var existing = byName[key];
