      var choice =
        defaultDestinationIconChoice_(
          destination
        );

      var askPass = existing && existing.askPass
        ? /^\s*y/i.test(existing.askPass)
        : KIOSK_ASK_PASS_DEFAULT_.indexOf(key) !== -1;
      var optional = existing && existing.optional
        ? /^\s*y/i.test(existing.optional)
        : KIOSK_OPTIONAL_TRIP_DEFAULT_.indexOf(key) !== -1;

      if (existing) {
        return {
          destination: destination,
          collection:
            existing.collection ||
            choice[0],
          category:
            existing.category ||
            choice[1],
          icon:
            existing.icon ||
            choice[2],
          notes:
            existing.notes || "",
          askPass: askPass,
          optional: optional
        };
      }

      return {
        destination: destination,
        collection: choice[0],
        category: choice[1],
        icon: choice[2],
        notes: "",
        askPass: askPass,
        optional: optional
      };
    }
  );

  return result;
}
function ensureLogHeaders__full_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Logs");

  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet("Logs");
  }

  var headers = [
    "Student Name",
    "Destination",
    "Time Out",
    "Time In",
    "Duration"
  ];

  var currentHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 5).getValues()[0];
  var needsHeaders = false;

  for (var i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) {
    if (currentHeaders[i] !== headers[i]) {
      needsHeaders = true;
      break;
    }
  }

  if (needsHeaders) {
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 5).setValues([headers]);
  }

  return sheet;
}

function ensureSettingsSheet__full_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Settings");

  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet("Settings");
  }

  var defaults = [
    ["Setting", "Value", "What this means"],
    ["Green Through", "5 min", "Students stay GREEN from checkout through this amount of time."],
    ["Yellow Through", "8 min", "After the green limit, students stay YELLOW through this amount of time. Anything longer turns RED."],
    ["Student Menu Style", "Student Cards", "Choose how students find their name."],
    ["Top Title", "Classroom Pass System", "Smaller optional title above the main title."],
    ["Main Title", "Hall Pass Kiosk", "Large optional kiosk title."],
    ["Background", "School- Supplies", "Choose a built-in or custom 16:9 background."],
    ["Custom Background URL", "", "Legacy custom URL field. In-app uploads are preferred."],
    ["Font", "Arial", "Font used on the kiosk display. Teacher Settings always stays in a standard sans-serif."],
    ["Title Order", "Small First", "Choose whether the smaller title appears above or below the main title."],
    ["Class Selection Mode", "Manual", "Manual shows the class chooser. Bell Schedule automatically shows the roster for the current/next class using the Bell Schedule and Schedule Calendar sheets."],
    ["Attendance Enabled", "ON", "Turns on the attendance system once the attendance interface is built. Leave OFF while the foundation is being tested."],
    ["Attendance Confirmation", "2 min", "A check-in must remain active for this long before it officially counts as Present or Tardy."],
    ["Absence Alert Threshold", "3", "Flag a student after this many consecutive class meetings marked Absent."],
    ["Attendance Finalization", "Class End", "Attendance remains editable during class and finalizes when the class-end bell occurs."],
    ["Attendance Card Style", "Random Bank", "Choose Emoji Slot Machine or Solid Colors. Both styles use RED for absent, GREEN for present/tardy, and YELLOW for students who are out of the room."],
    ["Tardy Grace Period", "30 sec", "Students are still counted Present if their original arrival tap occurs within this amount of time after the Late Bell."]
  ];

  var range = sheet.getRange(1, 1, defaults.length, 3);
  var current = range.getValues();
  var changed = false;

  for (var i = 0; i < defaults.length; i++) {
    if (!current[i][0]) {
      current[i][0] = defaults[i][0];
      changed = true;
    }

    if (current[i][1] === "" && defaults[i][1] !== "") {
      current[i][1] = defaults[i][1];
      changed = true;
    }

    if (!current[i][2]) {
      current[i][2] = defaults[i][2];
      changed = true;
    }
  }

  if (changed) {
    range.setValues(current);
  }

  return sheet;
}

function ensureBackgroundsSheet__full_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Backgrounds");

  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet("Backgrounds");
  }

  var headers = [
    "Enabled",
    "Background Name",
    "Source Type",
    "Background Source",
    "Notes",
    "Tone Family",
    "High Contrast",
    "Strong Contrast",
    "Pop Color",
    "Soft Support",
    "Low Contrast",
    "Black",
    "White",
    "Accent Layout",
    "Accent Advanced"
  ];

  var range = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length);
  var current = range.getValues()[0];
  var changed = false;

  for (var c = 0; c < headers.length; c++) {
    if (!current[c]) {
      current[c] = headers[c];
      changed = true;
    }
  }

  if (changed) {
    range.setValues([current]);
  }

  return sheet;
}


/**
 * SAFE, MANUAL color-safety setup.
 *
 * This function NEVER clears, inserts, deletes, moves, or rewrites Backgrounds.
 * It only touches:
 *   L = Dark Text
 *   M = Light Text
 *   P = Preferred Base
 *
 * Run manually once after installing this build. It is NOT called on app startup.
 */
function applyColorSafetyColumns__write_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Backgrounds");

  if (!sheet) {
    throw new Error('Backgrounds sheet not found. Nothing was changed.');
  }

  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) {
    throw new Error('Backgrounds sheet has no data rows. Nothing was changed.');
  }

  // Safety check: A-E must still look like the teacher's background table.
  var headerA = String(sheet.getRange('A1').getDisplayValue() || '').trim();
  var headerB = String(sheet.getRange('B1').getDisplayValue() || '').trim();
  var headerC = String(sheet.getRange('C1').getDisplayValue() || '').trim();
  var headerD = String(sheet.getRange('D1').getDisplayValue() || '').trim();

  if (
    headerA !== 'Enabled' ||
    headerB !== 'Background Name' ||
    headerC !== 'Source Type' ||
    headerD !== 'Background Source'
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      'Backgrounds sheet does not match the expected safe layout. ' +
      'Nothing was changed.'
    );
  }

  var curated = {
    'Soft & Cheerful': ['#203238', '#FFF9FC', 'Light'],
    'Creative & Colorful': ['#1D2937', '#FFF9F5', 'Automatic'],
    'Retro & Techy': ['#17243B', '#FFF9EA', 'Automatic'],
    'Literary & Warm': ['#30261E', '#FFF9ED', 'Automatic'],
    'Bold & Patriotic': ['#19263D', '#FFF9F4', 'Automatic'],
    'Warm & Cultural': ['#241B17', '#FFF8E8', 'Automatic'],
    'Clean & Calming': ['#26364B', '#FCFDFF', 'Light'],
    'Warm / Vintage': ['#30251E', '#FFF5E8', 'Automatic'],
    'Earthy / Academic': ['#31281F', '#FBF3E7', 'Automatic'],
    'Bright & Playful': ['#28263A', '#FFFDF2', 'Automatic'],
    'Soft Vintage / Artistic': ['#372A22', '#FFF7F2', 'Automatic'],
    'Energetic & Sporty': ['#18303A', '#FFF9F2', 'Automatic'],
    'Dark / Chalkboard': ['#11161B', '#F6F1E8', 'Dark'],
    'Cute & Playful': ['#23273A', '#FFF9FC', 'Light'],
    'Light & Cheerful': ['#2A2D45', '#FFFDF6', 'Light'],
    'Sweet & Playful': ['#34273A', '#FFF9FA', 'Light'],
    'Fresh & Fun': ['#263235', '#FFF9F2', 'Automatic'],
    'Bright / Playful': ['#202B24', '#FFFBEA', 'Automatic'],
    'Bright / Playful STEM': ['#1B3141', '#F8FDFF', 'Automatic'],
    'Warm / Bright': ['#302719', '#FFF9E7', 'Automatic'],
    'Jewel Tone': ['#24182C', '#FFF8EA', 'Automatic'],
    'Patriotic / High-Contrast': ['#182747', '#FFF9ED', 'Automatic']
  };

  // Read only names/tone families. We do not touch A-K or N-O.
  var names = sheet.getRange(2, 2, lastRow - 1, 1).getDisplayValues();
  var tones = sheet.getRange(2, 6, lastRow - 1, 1).getDisplayValues();
  var oldDark = sheet.getRange(2, 12, lastRow - 1, 1).getDisplayValues();
  var oldLight = sheet.getRange(2, 13, lastRow - 1, 1).getDisplayValues();

  var darkValues = [];
  var lightValues = [];
  var baseValues = [];

  for (var i = 0; i < lastRow - 1; i++) {
    var name = String(names[i][0] || '').trim();
    var tone = String(tones[i][0] || '').trim();

    if (!name) {
      darkValues.push([oldDark[i][0] || '']);
      lightValues.push([oldLight[i][0] || '']);
      baseValues.push(['']);
      continue;
    }

    var pair = curated[tone] || ['#1F252B', '#FFFDF8', 'Automatic'];
    darkValues.push([pair[0]]);
    lightValues.push([pair[1]]);
    baseValues.push([pair[2]]);
  }

  // Only these three header cells are changed.
  sheet.getRange('L1').setValue('Dark Text');
  sheet.getRange('M1').setValue('Light Text');
  sheet.getRange('P1').setValue('Preferred Base');

  // Only these three data columns are changed.
  sheet.getRange(2, 12, darkValues.length, 1).setValues(darkValues);
  sheet.getRange(2, 13, lightValues.length, 1).setValues(lightValues);
  sheet.getRange(2, 16, baseValues.length, 1).setValues(baseValues);

  return {
    updatedRows: names.filter(function(r) {
      return String(r[0] || '').trim();
    }).length,
    message:
      'Color safety columns updated. Background images/sources and accent layouts were not modified.'
  };
}

function normalizeBackgroundKey_(name) {
  return String(name || "")
    .replace(/_/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s*\(\d+\)\s*$/, "")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase();
}

function getBackgroundPaletteMap_() {
  var sheet = ensureBackgroundsSheet();
  var map = {};

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return map;
  }

  var data = sheet
    .getRange(2, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 15)
    .getDisplayValues();

  data.forEach(function(row) {
    var name = String(row[0] || "").trim();
    if (!name) return;

    var key = normalizeBackgroundKey_(name);
    if (!key) return;

    map[key] = {
      toneFamily: String(row[4] || "").trim(),
      highContrast: String(row[5] || "").trim(),
      strongContrast: String(row[6] || "").trim(),
      popColor: String(row[7] || "").trim(),
      softSupport: String(row[8] || "").trim(),
      lowContrast: String(row[9] || "").trim(),
      // L/M are now teacher-editable style-matched text anchors.
      // Keep black/white aliases for backwards compatibility with older UI code.
      darkText: String(row[10] || "#1F252B").trim() || "#1F252B",
      lightText: String(row[11] || "#FFFDF8").trim() || "#FFFDF8",
      black: String(row[10] || "#1F252B").trim() || "#1F252B",
      white: String(row[11] || "#FFFDF8").trim() || "#FFFDF8",
      preferredBase: String(row[14] || "Automatic").trim() || "Automatic"
    };
  });

  return map;
}


function getOverGridImageMap_(sheet, onlyRow) {
  var map = {};

  if (!sheet || typeof sheet.getImages !== "function") {
    return map;
  }

  try {
    var images = sheet.getImages() || [];

    images.forEach(function(image) {
      try {
        var anchor = image.getAnchorCell();
        if (!anchor) return;

        var row = anchor.getRow();
        var col = anchor.getColumn();

        // Background images belong in column D.
        if (col !== 4) return;

        // When one row was requested, skip encoding every other image.
        if (onlyRow && row !== onlyRow) return;

        var blob = image.getBlob();
        if (!blob) return;

        var contentType = blob.getContentType() || "image/png";

        map[row] =
          "data:" + contentType + ";base64," +
          Utilities.base64Encode(blob.getBytes());
      } catch (imageError) {
        // Ignore one bad image and continue reading the rest.
      }
    });
  } catch (error) {
    // Older environments or unusual sheet image objects: leave map empty.
  }

  return map;
}

function getBackgroundSheetRecords_() {
  var sheet = ensureBackgroundsSheet();
  var records = {};

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return records;
  }

  var rowCount = sheet.getLastRow() - 1;
  var data = sheet.getRange(2, 1, rowCount, 16).getValues();
  var displayData = sheet.getRange(2, 1, rowCount, 16).getDisplayValues();
  var sourceFormulas = sheet.getRange(2, 4, rowCount, 1).getFormulas();
  var overGridImages = getOverGridImageMap_(sheet);

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    var enabled = String(displayData[i][0] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    var name = String(displayData[i][1] || "").trim();
    var type = String(displayData[i][2] || "IMAGE").trim().toUpperCase();

    if (enabled !== "ON" || !name) {
      continue;
    }

    var key = normalizeBackgroundKey_(name);
    if (!key) {
      continue;
    }

    var sourceValue = data[i][3];
    var sourceFormula = sourceFormulas[i][0];
    var sourceText = String(displayData[i][3] || "").trim();
    var imageData = "";

    if (type === "CSS") {
      imageData = sourceText;
    } else {
      // This handles:
      // - image pasted/inserted directly in the cell
      // - =IMAGE("...")
      // - public image URL
      // - viewable Google Drive link / DRIVEFILE token
      imageData = getImageData_(sourceValue, sourceFormula);

      // If the teacher pasted/imported an image OVER the cell rather than
      // using an in-cell image, read the over-grid image anchored to D(row).
      if (!imageData && overGridImages[i + 2]) {
        imageData = overGridImages[i + 2];
      }
    }

    records[key] = {
      name: name,
      type: type === "CSS" ? "CSS" : "IMAGE",
      value: imageData,
      palette: {
        toneFamily: String(displayData[i][5] || "").trim(),
        highContrast: String(displayData[i][6] || "").trim(),
        strongContrast: String(displayData[i][7] || "").trim(),
        popColor: String(displayData[i][8] || "").trim(),
        softSupport: String(displayData[i][9] || "").trim(),
        lowContrast: String(displayData[i][10] || "").trim(),
        darkText: String(displayData[i][11] || "#1F252B").trim() || "#1F252B",
        lightText: String(displayData[i][12] || "#FFFDF8").trim() || "#FFFDF8",
        // Backwards-compatible aliases.
        black: String(displayData[i][11] || "#1F252B").trim() || "#1F252B",
        white: String(displayData[i][12] || "#FFFDF8").trim() || "#FFFDF8",
        preferredBase: String(displayData[i][15] || "Automatic").trim() || "Automatic"
      },
      accentLayout: Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(
          119,
          parseInt(String(displayData[i][13] || "0"), 10) || 0
        )
      ),
      accentAdvanced: String(displayData[i][14] || "").trim()
    };
  }

  return records;
}

function getBackgroundOptions() {
  var sheet = ensureBackgroundsSheet();
  var options = [];

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return options;
  }

  var rowCount =
    sheet.getLastRow() - 1;

  var data =
    sheet.getRange(
      2,
      1,
      rowCount,
      16
    ).getValues();

  var display =
    sheet.getRange(
      2,
      1,
      rowCount,
      16
    ).getDisplayValues();

  var formulas =
    sheet.getRange(
      2,
      4,
      rowCount,
      1
    ).getFormulas();

  var backgroundLinks =
    kioskBackgroundLinks_(sheet);

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    var enabled =
      String(
        display[i][0] || ""
      ).trim().toUpperCase();

    var name =
      String(
        display[i][1] || ""
      ).trim();

    if (
      enabled !== "ON" ||
      !name
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    var rawType =
      String(
        display[i][2] || "IMAGE"
      ).trim().toUpperCase();

    var value = "";

    if (rawType === "CSS") {
      value =
        String(
          display[i][3] || ""
        ).trim();
    } else {
      var sourceValue =
        data[i][3];

      var sourceFormula =
        formulas[i][0];

      // Cheap image sources can be returned immediately.
      // Private DRIVEFILE blobs are intentionally deferred until the selected
      // background is actually needed.
      if (
        typeof sourceValue ===
        "string"
      ) {
        var sourceText =
          sourceValue.trim();

        if (
          sourceText &&
          !/^DRIVEFILE:/i.test(
            sourceText
          )
        ) {
          value =
            normalizeImageUrl_(
              sourceText
            );
        }
      }
      // A picture inside the cell uses its Drive copy's link (column S).
      // Without one yet, it stays blank and the kiosk asks
      // getBackgroundImage, which makes the copy.
      if (
        !value && sourceValue && typeof sourceValue === "object" &&
        backgroundLinks[i + 2]
      ) {
        value = backgroundLinks[i + 2];
      }

      if (!value && sourceFormula) {
        value =
          normalizeImageUrl_(
            extractImageFormulaUrl_(
              sourceFormula
            )
          );
      }
    }

    // Settings tiles only need a small picture: a Drive link can ask for
    // one 480 pixels wide (about a tenth of the full background).
    var previewValue =
      /^https:\/\/lh3\.googleusercontent\.com\/d\/[^=]+=w\d+$/.test(value)
        ? value.replace(/=w\d+$/, "=w480")
        : "";

    options.push({
      name: name,
      type:
        rawType === "CSS"
          ? "CSS"
          : "IMAGE",
      value: value,
      previewValue: previewValue,
      palette: {
        toneFamily:
          String(display[i][5] || "").trim(),
        highContrast:
          String(display[i][6] || "").trim(),
        strongContrast:
          String(display[i][7] || "").trim(),
        popColor:
          String(display[i][8] || "").trim(),
        softSupport:
          String(display[i][9] || "").trim(),
        lowContrast:
          String(display[i][10] || "").trim(),
        darkText:
          String(
            display[i][11] ||
            "#1F252B"
          ).trim() ||
          "#1F252B",
        lightText:
          String(
            display[i][12] ||
            "#FFFDF8"
          ).trim() ||
          "#FFFDF8",
        black:
          String(
            display[i][11] ||
            "#1F252B"
          ).trim() ||
          "#1F252B",
        white:
          String(
            display[i][12] ||
            "#FFFDF8"
          ).trim() ||
          "#FFFDF8",
        preferredBase:
          String(
            display[i][15] ||
            "Automatic"
          ).trim() ||
          "Automatic"
      },
      accentLayout: Math.max(
        0,
        Math.min(
          119,
          parseInt(
            String(
              display[i][13] || "0"
            ),
            10
          ) || 0
        )
      ),
      accentAdvanced:
        String(
          display[i][14] || ""
        ).trim()
    });
  }

  return options;
}

/* Pictures placed inside cells on the Backgrounds tab: Google gives the
   script a temporary web address that browsers can no longer open (found
   2026-10-16; the kiosk showed no custom backgrounds). The script can open
   it, so it fetches the picture and sends the picture itself. Each one is
   kept in the Script Cache for 6 hours; any hand edit to the Sheet
   (kioskOnChange) starts fresh. */
function kioskBackgroundStamp_() {
  var cache = kioskCache_();
  var stamp = cache.get("kiosk:bgStamp");

  if (!stamp) {
    stamp = kioskNewVersion_();
    try { cache.put("kiosk:bgStamp", stamp, 21600); } catch (err) {}
  }

  return stamp;
}

function kioskCellPictureData_(name, url) {
  var key = "kiosk:bgimg:" + normalizeBackgroundKey_(name);
  var stamp = kioskBackgroundStamp_();
  var saved = kioskCacheRead_(key, stamp);

  if (saved) return saved;

  try {
    var reply = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, {
      muteHttpExceptions: true,
      followRedirects: true
    });

    if (reply.getResponseCode() !== 200) return url;

    var blob = reply.getBlob();
    var data =
      "data:" + (blob.getContentType() || "image/png") + ";base64," +
      Utilities.base64Encode(blob.getBytes());

    kioskCacheWrite_(key, stamp, data, 21600);
    return data;
  } catch (err) {
    return url;
  }
}

/* ===== Backgrounds as link-shared Drive pictures (2026-10-16) =====
   Browsers can no longer open Google's temporary address for a picture
   placed inside a cell, but they open a Drive picture link fine (the
   built-in School Supplies picture always worked). So the first time an
   in-cell background is needed, the script copies it into the Drive folder
   "Hall Pass Kiosk Backgrounds", shares the copy view-only with anyone who
   has the link, and saves the link in column S (file ID in column T).
   The kiosk then loads it straight from Drive. Pasting a new picture in
   column D clears that row's link (onEdit), so a fresh copy is made. */
var BACKGROUND_LINK_COLUMN_ = 19;   // S
var BACKGROUND_FILE_COLUMN_ = 20;   // T

function kioskBackgroundLinkFolder_() {
  var name = "Hall Pass Kiosk Backgrounds";
  var folders = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(name);
  return folders.hasNext() ? folders.next() : DriveApp.createFolder(name);
}

function kioskEnsureBackgroundLinkColumns_(sheet) {
  if (sheet.getMaxColumns() < BACKGROUND_FILE_COLUMN_) {
    sheet.insertColumnsAfter(
      sheet.getMaxColumns(),
      BACKGROUND_FILE_COLUMN_ - sheet.getMaxColumns()
    );
  }

  var head = sheet.getRange(1, BACKGROUND_LINK_COLUMN_, 1, 2);
  var current = head.getValues()[0];

  if (!current[0] || !current[1]) {
    head.setValues([["Picture Link (auto)", "Picture File (auto)"]]);
  }
}

// The saved Drive links for every row, keyed by row number ({} if none yet).
function kioskBackgroundLinks_(sheet) {
  var links = {};
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();

  if (lastRow < 2 || sheet.getMaxColumns() < BACKGROUND_LINK_COLUMN_) return links;

  var values = sheet
    .getRange(2, BACKGROUND_LINK_COLUMN_, lastRow - 1, 1)
    .getDisplayValues();

  for (var i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    var link = String(values[i][0] || "").trim();
    if (/^https:\/\//.test(link)) links[i + 2] = link;
  }

  return links;
}

// Makes the Drive copy for one row and returns its link ("" if it can't).
function kioskMakeBackgroundLink_(sheet, row, name, cellImage) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();

  try {
    lock.waitLock(30000);
  } catch (lockErr) {
    return "";
  }

  try {
    // Another request may have just made it.
    if (sheet.getMaxColumns() >= BACKGROUND_LINK_COLUMN_) {
      var already = String(sheet.getRange(row, BACKGROUND_LINK_COLUMN_).getDisplayValue() || "").trim();
      if (/^https:\/\//.test(already)) return already;
    }

    var address = String(cellImage.getContentUrl() || "");
    if (!address) return "";

    var reply = UrlFetchApp.fetch(address, { muteHttpExceptions: true, followRedirects: true });
    if (reply.getResponseCode() !== 200) return "";

    var blob = reply.getBlob();
    var type = blob.getContentType() || "image/png";
    var ending = type.split("/")[1] || "png";
    blob.setName(String(name || "Background") + "." + ending.replace("jpeg", "jpg"));

    var file = kioskBackgroundLinkFolder_().createFile(blob);
    file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);

    var link = "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/" + file.getId() + "=w1920";

    kioskEnsureBackgroundLinkColumns_(sheet);
    sheet
      .getRange(row, BACKGROUND_LINK_COLUMN_, 1, 2)
      .setValues([[link, file.getId()]]);
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();

    return link;
  } catch (err) {
    return "";
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

// Run once from the Apps Script editor (select makeBackgroundLinks, click
// Run) to make the Drive copies for every background now instead of one by
// one as the kiosk needs them. Safe to run again: rows that already have a
// link are skipped.
function makeBackgroundLinks() {
  var sheet = ensureBackgroundsSheet();
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  var lines = [];
  var made = 0, kept = 0, failed = 0;

  if (lastRow < 2) {
    Logger.log("No backgrounds found.");
    return "No backgrounds found.";
  }

  var names = sheet.getRange(2, 2, lastRow - 1, 1).getDisplayValues();
  var values = sheet.getRange(2, 4, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues();
  var links = kioskBackgroundLinks_(sheet);

  for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
    var row = i + 2;
    var name = String(names[i][0] || "").trim();
    var value = values[i][0];

    if (!name || !value || typeof value !== "object" || typeof value.getContentUrl !== "function") continue;
    if (links[row]) { kept++; continue; }

    var link = kioskMakeBackgroundLink_(sheet, row, name, value);
    if (link) { made++; lines.push("Made: " + name); }
    else { failed++; lines.push("COULD NOT MAKE: " + name); }
  }

  lines.unshift("Drive copies made: " + made + ". Already had one: " + kept + ". Problems: " + failed + ".");
  var report = lines.join("\n");
  Logger.log(report);
  return report;
}

function getBackgroundImageByName_(name) {
  var sheet = ensureBackgroundsSheet();
  var wantedKey =
    normalizeBackgroundKey_(name);

  if (
    !wantedKey ||
    sheet.getLastRow() < 2
  ) {
    return "";
  }

  var rowCount =
    sheet.getLastRow() - 1;

  var names =
    sheet.getRange(
      2,
      2,
      rowCount,
      1
    ).getDisplayValues();

  var enabled =
    sheet.getRange(
      2,
      1,
      rowCount,
      1
    ).getDisplayValues();

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    if (
      String(
        enabled[i][0] || ""
      ).trim().toUpperCase() !==
      "ON"
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    if (
      normalizeBackgroundKey_(
        names[i][0]
      ) !== wantedKey
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    var row =
      i + 2;

    var type =
      String(
        sheet
          .getRange(row, 3)
          .getDisplayValue() ||
        "IMAGE"
      ).trim().toUpperCase();

    if (type === "CSS") {
      return String(
        sheet
          .getRange(row, 4)
          .getDisplayValue() ||
        ""
      ).trim();
    }

    var sourceCell =
      sheet.getRange(
        row,
        4
      );

    var value =
      sourceCell.getValue();

    var formula =
      sourceCell.getFormula();

    var imageData =
      getImageData_(
        value,
        formula
      );

    if (
      value && typeof value === "object" &&
      typeof value.getContentUrl === "function"
    ) {
      var savedLink = kioskBackgroundLinks_(sheet)[row] ||
        kioskMakeBackgroundLink_(sheet, row, names[i][0], value);

      // If the Drive copy can't be made (for example sharing is blocked),
      // send the picture itself instead.
      imageData = savedLink ||
        (/^https?:/i.test(String(imageData || ""))
          ? kioskCellPictureData_(name, imageData)
          : imageData);
    }

    if (imageData) {
      return imageData;
    }

    // Over-grid images are checked only for this one requested row.
    var overGrid =
      getOverGridImageMap_(
        sheet,
        row
      );

    return (
      overGrid[row] ||
      ""
    );
  }

  return "";
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
// Background previews for the Settings panel.
//
// A small preview of each background is stored on the Backgrounds tab
// (column Q, with column R recording which image it was made from), so
// the settings panel gets every preview in ONE quick request instead of
// downloading each full-size background.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
var BACKGROUND_PREVIEW_COLUMN_ = 17;   // Q
var BACKGROUND_PREVIEW_SOURCE_COLUMN_ = 18;   // R

// Identifies which image a preview was made from, so a preview is thrown
// away automatically when the background's image is replaced.
function backgroundPreviewSourceKey_(sourceValue, sourceFormula) {
  if (typeof sourceValue === "string" && sourceValue.trim()) {
    return "text:" + sourceValue.trim();
  }

  if (sourceFormula) {
    return "formula:" + String(sourceFormula).trim();
  }

  return "image";
}

// { "<background name>": "<small preview image>" } for every background
// that has an up-to-date stored preview.
function getBackgroundPreviews() {
  var previews = {};
  var sheet = ensureBackgroundsSheet();

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return previews;

  var rowCount = sheet.getLastRow() - 1;
  var info = sheet.getRange(2, 1, rowCount, 4).getValues();
  var formulas = sheet.getRange(2, 4, rowCount, 1).getFormulas();
  var stored = sheet
    .getRange(2, BACKGROUND_PREVIEW_COLUMN_, rowCount, 2)
    .getValues();

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    var name = String(info[i][1] || "").trim();
    var preview = String(stored[i][0] || "");

    if (
      !name ||
      String(info[i][0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() !== "ON" ||
      preview.indexOf("data:image/") !== 0
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    if (
      String(stored[i][1] || "") !==
      backgroundPreviewSourceKey_(info[i][3], formulas[i][0])
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    previews[name] = preview;
  }

  return previews;
}

// Stores a small preview made by the kiosk for one background.
function saveBackgroundPreview(name, dataUrl) {
  dataUrl = String(dataUrl || "");

  if (
    !/^data:image\/(jpeg|png|webp);base64,/.test(dataUrl) ||
    dataUrl.length > 49000
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  var sheet = ensureBackgroundsSheet();
  var wantedKey = normalizeBackgroundKey_(name);

  if (!wantedKey || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return false;

  var rowCount = sheet.getLastRow() - 1;
  var names = sheet.getRange(2, 2, rowCount, 1).getDisplayValues();

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    if (normalizeBackgroundKey_(names[i][0]) !== wantedKey) continue;

    var row = i + 2;
    var sourceCell = sheet.getRange(row, 4);

    if (!String(sheet.getRange(1, BACKGROUND_PREVIEW_COLUMN_).getValue() || "")) {
      sheet
        .getRange(1, BACKGROUND_PREVIEW_COLUMN_, 1, 2)
        .setValues([["Preview (auto)", "Preview Source (auto)"]]);
    }

    sheet
      .getRange(row, BACKGROUND_PREVIEW_COLUMN_, 1, 2)
      .setValues([[
        dataUrl,
        backgroundPreviewSourceKey_(
          sourceCell.getValue(),
          sourceCell.getFormula()
        )
      ]]);

    // Keep the long preview text from stretching the row.
    try {
      sheet
        .getRange(row, BACKGROUND_PREVIEW_COLUMN_, 1, 2)
        .setWrapStrategy(SpreadsheetApp.WrapStrategy.CLIP);
    } catch (wrapErr) {}

    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

function getBackgroundThumbnail(name) {
  return getBackgroundImageByName_(
    name
  );
}

function getBackgroundImage(name) {
  return getBackgroundImageByName_(
    name
  );
}


function saveAccentLayout__write_(backgroundName, layoutIndex) {
  var sheet = ensureBackgroundsSheet();
  var wantedKey = normalizeBackgroundKey_(backgroundName);
  var safeIndex = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(119, parseInt(layoutIndex, 10) || 0)
  );

  if (!wantedKey) {
    throw new Error("Choose a background first.");
  }

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    throw new Error("No backgrounds were found.");
  }

  var names = sheet
    .getRange(2, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1)
    .getDisplayValues();

  for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
    if (normalizeBackgroundKey_(names[i][0]) === wantedKey) {
      sheet.getRange(i + 2, 14).setValue(safeIndex);
      sheet.getRange(i + 2, 15).clearContent();
      SpreadsheetApp.flush();

      return {
        backgroundName: String(names[i][0] || backgroundName),
        accentLayout: safeIndex
      };
    }
  }

  throw new Error(
    'Could not find "' + backgroundName + '" on the Backgrounds sheet.'
  );
}


function saveAccentAdvanced__write_(backgroundName, roleMapJson) {
  var sheet = ensureBackgroundsSheet();
  var wantedKey = normalizeBackgroundKey_(backgroundName);

  if (!wantedKey) {
    throw new Error("Choose a background first.");
  }

  var parsed;
  try {
    parsed = JSON.parse(String(roleMapJson || "{}"));
  } catch (err) {
    throw new Error("Could not save the advanced accent settings.");
  }

  var allowedRoles = [
    "titleText",
    "textColor",
    "titleOutline",
    "titleBox",
    "panelFill",
    "panelBorder",
    "cardFill",
    "cardBorder",
    "iconAccent",
    "primaryButton",
    "navControls",
    "bigTitle",
    "smallTitle",
    "headingText"
  ];

  var allowedSlots = ["high","strong","pop","soft","low"];
  var clean = {};

  allowedRoles.forEach(function(role) {
    var slot = String(parsed[role] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (allowedSlots.indexOf(slot) !== -1) {
      clean[role] = slot;
    }
  });

  var names = sheet
    .getRange(2, 2, Math.max(sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1), 1)
    .getDisplayValues();

  for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
    if (normalizeBackgroundKey_(names[i][0]) === wantedKey) {
      sheet.getRange(i + 2, 15).setValue(JSON.stringify(clean));
      SpreadsheetApp.flush();

      return {
        backgroundName: String(names[i][0] || backgroundName),
        accentAdvanced: JSON.stringify(clean)
      };
    }
  }

  throw new Error(
    'Could not find "' + backgroundName + '" on the Backgrounds sheet.'
  );
}

function clearAccentAdvanced__write_(backgroundName) {
  var sheet = ensureBackgroundsSheet();
  var wantedKey = normalizeBackgroundKey_(backgroundName);

  if (!wantedKey) {
    throw new Error("Choose a background first.");
  }

  var names = sheet
    .getRange(2, 2, Math.max(sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1), 1)
    .getDisplayValues();

  for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
    if (normalizeBackgroundKey_(names[i][0]) === wantedKey) {
      sheet.getRange(i + 2, 15).clearContent();
      SpreadsheetApp.flush();

      return {
        backgroundName: String(names[i][0] || backgroundName),
        accentAdvanced: ""
      };
    }
  }

  throw new Error(
    'Could not find "' + backgroundName + '" on the Backgrounds sheet.'
  );
}

function cleanUploadedName_(name) {
  name = String(name || "").trim();
  name = name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, "");
  name = name.replace(/_/g, " ");
  name = name.replace(/\s*\(\d+\)\s*$/, "");
  name = name.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  return name;
}

function getBackgroundUploadFolder_() {
  var folderName = "Hall Pass Kiosk Background Uploads";
  var folders = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(folderName);

  if (folders.hasNext()) {
    return folders.next();
  }

  return DriveApp.createFolder(folderName);
}

function saveCustomBackgroundUpload__write_(fileName, dataUrl, preferredName) {
  var match = String(dataUrl || "").match(
    /^data:(image\/[a-zA-Z0-9.+-]+);base64,(.+)$/
  );

  if (!match) {
    throw new Error("Could not read that image.");
  }

  var displayName = cleanUploadedName_(preferredName || fileName);

  if (!displayName) {
    displayName = "Custom Background";
  }

  var blob = Utilities.newBlob(
    Utilities.base64Decode(match[2]),
    match[1],
    String(fileName || "background.jpg")
  );

  var folder = getBackgroundUploadFolder_();
  var driveFile = folder.createFile(blob);
  var token = "DRIVEFILE:" + driveFile.getId();

  var sheet = ensureBackgroundsSheet();
  var existingRow = 0;

  if (sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    var names = sheet
      .getRange(2, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1)
      .getDisplayValues();

    for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
      if (String(names[i][0] || "").trim() === displayName) {
        existingRow = i + 2;
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  var row = existingRow || sheet.getLastRow() + 1;

  sheet.getRange(row, 1, 1, 15).setValues([[
    "ON",
    displayName,
    "IMAGE",
    token,
    "Uploaded through Teacher Settings. 16:9.",
    "",
    "",
    "",
    "",
    "",
    "",
    "#000000",
    "#FFFFFF",
    "",
    ""
  ]]);

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    name: displayName,
    options: getBackgroundOptions()
  };
}

function ensureFontsSheet__full_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Fonts");

  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet("Fonts");
    sheet.getRange("A1:D1").setValues([[
      "Font Name",
      "Font Family",
      "Drive Token",
      "Notes"
    ]]);
  }

  return sheet;
}

function getFontOptions__read_() {
  var options = [
    {name: "Arial", family: "Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif", type: "SYSTEM"},
    {name: "Verdana", family: "Verdana, Geneva, sans-serif", type: "SYSTEM"},
    {name: "Georgia", family: "Georgia, serif", type: "SYSTEM"},
    {name: "Lora", family: "\"Lora\", Georgia, serif", type: "WEB"},
    {name: "OpenDyslexic", family: "\"OpenDyslexic\", Arial, sans-serif", type: "WEB"},
    {name: "CG ASL", family: "\"CGasl\", Arial, sans-serif", type: "BUILTIN"},
    {name: "Braille Full Cell", family: "\"BrailleFull\", Arial, sans-serif", type: "BUILTIN"},
    {name: "Braille Dots Only", family: "\"Segoe UI Symbol\", \"Arial Unicode MS\", sans-serif", type: "UNICODE_BRAILLE"}
  ];

  var sheet = ensureFontsSheet();

  if (sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    var data = sheet
      .getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 4)
      .getValues();

    data.forEach(function(row) {
      var name = String(row[0] || "").trim();
      var family = String(row[1] || "").trim();
      var token = String(row[2] || "").trim();

      if (!name || !family || !token) {
        return;
      }

      var dataUrl = getFontDataUrl_(token.replace(/^DRIVEFILE:/i, ""));

      if (dataUrl) {
        options.push({
          name: name,
          family: "\"" + family + "\", Arial, sans-serif",
          type: "CUSTOM",
          familyKey: family,
          dataUrl: dataUrl
        });
      }
    });
  }

  return options;
}

function getFontDataUrl_(fileId) {
  try {
    var file = DriveApp.getFileById(fileId);
    var blob = file.getBlob();
    var contentType = blob.getContentType() || "font/ttf";

    return "data:" +
      contentType +
      ";base64," +
      Utilities.base64Encode(blob.getBytes());
  } catch (err) {
    return "";
  }
}

function getFontUploadFolder_() {
  var folderName = "Hall Pass Kiosk Font Uploads";
  var folders = DriveApp.getFoldersByName(folderName);

  if (folders.hasNext()) {
    return folders.next();
  }

  return DriveApp.createFolder(folderName);
}

function saveFontUploads__write_(files) {
  if (!files || !files.length) {
    throw new Error("Choose at least one font file.");
  }

  var folder = getFontUploadFolder_();
  var sheet = ensureFontsSheet();
  var uploaded = [];

  files.forEach(function(file) {
    var match = String(file.dataUrl || "").match(
      /^data:([^;]+);base64,(.+)$/
    );

    if (!match) {
      return;
    }

    var cleanName = cleanUploadedName_(file.name || "Custom Font");
    var family = cleanName;
    var lower = cleanName.toLowerCase();

    if (lower.indexOf("cgasl") !== -1) {
      cleanName = "CG ASL";
      family = "CGasl";
    } else if (
      lower.indexOf("full cell braille") !== -1 ||
      lower.indexOf("fullcellbraille") !== -1
    ) {
      cleanName = "Braille Full Cell";
      family = "BrailleFullCell";
    } else if (
      lower.indexOf("dots only braille") !== -1 ||
      lower.indexOf("dotsonlybraille") !== -1
    ) {
      cleanName = "Braille Dots Only";
      family = "BrailleDotsOnly";
    }

    var blob = Utilities.newBlob(
      Utilities.base64Decode(match[2]),
      match[1],
      file.name || "font.ttf"
    );

    var driveFile = folder.createFile(blob);
    var token = "DRIVEFILE:" + driveFile.getId();
    var row = sheet.getLastRow() + 1;

    sheet.getRange(row, 1, 1, 4).setValues([[
      cleanName,
      family,
      token,
      "Uploaded through Teacher Settings."
    ]]);

    uploaded.push(cleanName);
  });

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    uploaded: uploaded,
    options: getFontOptions()
  };
}


function normalizeScheduleDateKey_(value) {
  var text = String(value == null ? "" : value).trim();

  var match = text.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/);
  if (match) {
    return Number(match[1]) + "/" + Number(match[2]) + "/" + Number(match[3]);
  }

  var d = new Date(value);
  if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) {
    return (d.getMonth() + 1) + "/" + d.getDate() + "/" + d.getFullYear();
  }

  return text;
}

function parseScheduleClockMinutes_(value) {
  var text = String(value == null ? "" : value).trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!text) return null;

  var match = text.match(/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::\d{2})?\s*(AM|PM)?$/);
  if (!match) return null;

  var hour = Number(match[1]);
  var minute = Number(match[2]);
  var meridiem = match[3] || "";

  if (minute < 0 || minute > 59) return null;

  if (meridiem) {
    hour = hour % 12;
    if (meridiem === "PM") hour += 12;
  }

  if (hour < 0 || hour > 23) return null;
  return hour * 60 + minute;
}



function getBellScheduleDisplay__read_(scheduleName) {
  ensureSeparatedLunchBlocks_();

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Bell Schedule");

  var wanted = String(scheduleName || "").trim();

  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2 || !wanted) {
    return {
      scheduleName: wanted,
      rows: []
    };
  }

  var data = kioskRowsByHeading_(sheet, KIOSK_BELL_COLS_, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);

  var rows = [];

  data.forEach(function(row) {
    var enabled = String(row[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    var rowSchedule = String(row[1] || "").trim();

    if (enabled !== "ON" || rowSchedule !== wanted) {
      return;
    }

    rows.push({
      period: String(row[2] || "").trim(),
      rosterPeriod: String(row[3] || "").trim(),
      rosterOpens: String(row[4] || "").trim(),
      lateBell: String(row[5] || "").trim(),
      classEnds: String(row[6] || "").trim(),
      blockType: String(row[7] || "").trim(),
      notes: String(row[8] || "").trim()
    });
  });

  rows.sort(function(a, b) {
    var aMin = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(a.lateBell);
    var bMin = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(b.lateBell);

    if (aMin == null) aMin = 9999;
    if (bMin == null) bMin = 9999;

    return aMin - bMin;
  });

  var warningBell = "";

  if (rows.length) {
    var firstBell = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(rows[0].lateBell);

    if (firstBell != null) {
      var warningMinutes = Math.max(0, firstBell - 5);
      var warningHour = Math.floor(warningMinutes / 60);
      var warningMinute = Math.round(warningMinutes % 60);
      var suffix = warningHour >= 12 ? "PM" : "AM";
      var hour12 = warningHour % 12;
      if (hour12 === 0) hour12 = 12;

      warningBell =
        hour12 + ":" +
        String(warningMinute).padStart(2, "0") +
        " " + suffix;
    }
  }

  return {
    scheduleName: wanted,
    warningBell: warningBell,
    rows: rows
  };
}

function getBellScheduleNames__read_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Bell Schedule");

  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return [];
  }

  var values = kioskRowsByHeading_(sheet, KIOSK_BELL_COLS_, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);

  var seen = {};
  var names = [];

  values.forEach(function(row) {
    var enabled = String(row[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    var name = String(row[1] || "").trim();

    if (enabled !== "ON" || !name || seen[name]) return;

    seen[name] = true;
    names.push(name);
  });

  return names;
}


var FIFTH_B_LUNCH_NOTE_ =
  "Teacher lunch. Nothing scheduled; 5th period study hall ends with 5A.";

function ensureSeparatedLunchBlocks__full_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Bell Schedule");

  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return;

  var data = kioskRowsByHeading_(sheet, KIOSK_BELL_COLS_, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);
  var bellMap = kioskHeadingMap_(sheet, KIOSK_BELL_COLS_);      // where each column really is (for writing)

  var schedules = {};

  data.forEach(function(row, index) {
    var scheduleName = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    if (!scheduleName) return;

    if (!schedules[scheduleName]) {
      schedules[scheduleName] = { periods: {}, rows: [] };
    }

    var period = String(row[2] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    schedules[scheduleName].periods[period] = true;
    schedules[scheduleName].rows.push({
      rowNumber: index + 2,
      period: period
    });
  });

  function kindFor(name) {
    var lower = String(name || "").toLowerCase();

    if (lower.indexOf("final") !== -1) return "";
    if (
      lower.indexOf("early") !== -1 &&
      lower.indexOf("dismiss") !== -1
    ) return "";

    if (lower.indexOf("late") !== -1 && lower.indexOf("start") !== -1) {
      return "late";
    }

    if (
      lower.indexOf("comm") !== -1 ||
      lower.indexOf("communication") !== -1
    ) {
      return "comm";
    }

    if (lower.indexOf("regular") !== -1) {
      return "regular";
    }

    return "";
  }

  var canonical = {
    regular: [
      ["4A", "4th", "10:21 AM", "10:27 AM", "10:52 AM"],
      ["4B", "4th", "10:52 AM", "10:57 AM", "11:22 AM"],
      ["5A", "5th", "11:22 AM", "11:28 AM", "11:53 AM"],
      ["5B", "Lunch", "11:53 AM", "11:58 AM", "12:23 PM", "Lunch"]
    ],
    late: [
      ["4A", "4th", "11:06 AM", "11:12 AM", "11:37 AM"],
      ["4B", "4th", "11:37 AM", "11:42 AM", "12:07 PM"],
      ["5A", "5th", "12:07 PM", "12:13 PM", "12:38 PM"],
      ["5B", "Lunch", "12:38 PM", "12:43 PM", "1:08 PM", "Lunch"]
    ],
    comm: [
      ["4A", "4th", "10:33 AM", "10:39 AM", "11:04 AM"],
      ["4B", "4th", "11:04 AM", "11:09 AM", "11:34 AM"],
      ["5A", "5th", "11:34 AM", "11:40 AM", "12:05 PM"],
      ["5B", "Lunch", "12:05 PM", "12:10 PM", "12:35 PM", "Lunch"]
    ]
  };

  var deleteRows = [];
  var appendRows = [];

  // 5A = lunch study hall (the 5th period roster). 5B = teacher lunch.
  // Existing app-made 5B rows are switched to "Lunch" once, so the 5th
  // period roster is not reopened at the start of 5B. A 5B row the
  // teacher has edited by hand (different note) is left alone.
  var fifthBUpdates = [];

  data.forEach(function(row, index) {
    var period = String(row[2] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    var blockType = String(row[7] || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    var rowNote = String(row[8] || "").trim();

    if (
      period === "5B" &&
      blockType === "class" &&
      (
        !rowNote ||
        rowNote === "Lunch subperiods shown separately; same roster period."
      )
    ) {
      fifthBUpdates.push(index + 2);
    }
  });

  fifthBUpdates.forEach(function(rowNumber) {
    sheet.getRange(rowNumber, bellMap.cols[3] + 1).setValue("Lunch");
    sheet.getRange(rowNumber, bellMap.cols[7] + 1).setValue("Lunch");
    sheet.getRange(rowNumber, bellMap.cols[8] + 1).setValue(FIFTH_B_LUNCH_NOTE_);
  });

  Object.keys(schedules).forEach(function(scheduleName) {
    var kind = kindFor(scheduleName);
    if (!kind) return;

    var info = schedules[scheduleName];
    var splitAlready =
      info.periods["4A"] &&
      info.periods["4B"] &&
      info.periods["5A"] &&
      info.periods["5B"];

    if (splitAlready) return;

    info.rows.forEach(function(item) {
      if (
        item.period === "4" ||
        item.period === "4AB" ||
        item.period === "4A" ||
        item.period === "4B" ||
        item.period === "5" ||
        item.period === "5AB" ||
        item.period === "5A" ||
        item.period === "5B"
      ) {
        deleteRows.push(item.rowNumber);
      }
    });

    canonical[kind].forEach(function(block) {
      appendRows.push([
        "ON",
        scheduleName,
        block[0],
        block[1],
        block[2],
        block[3],
        block[4],
        block[5] || "Class",
        block[5] === "Lunch"
          ? FIFTH_B_LUNCH_NOTE_
          : "Lunch subperiods shown separately; same roster period."
      ]);
    });
  });

  deleteRows
    .sort(function(a, b) { return b - a; })
    .forEach(function(rowNumber) {
      sheet.deleteRow(rowNumber);
    });

  if (appendRows.length) {
    // each value goes under its own heading, wherever the teacher has put that column
    var wide = appendRows.map(function(r) {
      var out = []; for (var c = 0; c < bellMap.lastCol; c++) out.push("");
      r.forEach(function(v, k) { out[bellMap.cols[k]] = v; });
      return out;
    });
    sheet
      .getRange(sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, wide.length, bellMap.lastCol)
      .setValues(wide);
  }

  if (deleteRows.length || appendRows.length || fifthBUpdates.length) {
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }
}

function getBellScheduleStatus(scheduleOverride) {
  // The schedule rows come from the saved copy (refreshed whenever the
  // sheet changes); only the clock comparison is done fresh every time.
  var tz = kioskTimeZone_();
  var now = new Date();

  var dateKey = Utilities.formatDate(now, tz, "M/d/yyyy");
  var weekday = Utilities.formatDate(now, tz, "EEEE");
  var currentMinutes =
    Number(Utilities.formatDate(now, tz, "H")) * 60 +
    Number(Utilities.formatDate(now, tz, "m")) +
    Number(Utilities.formatDate(now, tz, "s")) / 60;

  var calendarRows = kioskScheduleCalendarRows_();
  var scheduleName = "";
  var schoolDay = true;
  var note = "";
  var matchedRule = "";

  if (weekday === "Saturday" || weekday === "Sunday") {
    schoolDay = false;
  }

  if (calendarRows.length) {
    var rows = calendarRows;

    var specific = null;
    var weekly = null;

    rows.forEach(function(row) {
      var ruleType = String(row[0] || "").trim();
      var ruleValue = String(row[1] || "").trim();

      if (
        ruleType === "Specific Date" &&
        normalizeScheduleDateKey_(ruleValue) ===
          normalizeScheduleDateKey_(dateKey)
      ) {
        specific = row;
      }

      if (
        ruleType === "Weekday" &&
        ruleValue.toLowerCase() === weekday.toLowerCase()
      ) {
        weekly = row;
      }
    });

    var selected = specific || weekly;

    if (selected) {
      scheduleName = String(selected[2] || "").trim();
      schoolDay =
        String(selected[3] || "Yes").trim().toLowerCase() !== "no";
      note = String(selected[5] || "").trim();
      matchedRule = specific ? "Specific Date" : "Weekday";
    }
  }

  var overrideName = String(scheduleOverride || "").trim();

  if (overrideName === "__MANUAL__") {
    return {
      success: true,
      schoolDay: true,
      scheduleName: "",
      phase: "manual",
      activeRosterPeriod: "",
      label: "Manual class selection",
      note: "Automatic schedule temporarily disabled from the kiosk.",
      dateKey: dateKey,
      weekday: weekday,
      manualOverride: true
    };
  }

  if (overrideName && overrideName !== "__AUTO__") {
    scheduleName = overrideName;
    schoolDay = true;
    matchedRule = "Manual Day Type Override";
  }

  if (!schoolDay) {
    return {
      success: true,
      schoolDay: false,
      scheduleName: "",
      phase: "noSchool",
      activeRosterPeriod: "",
      label: note || "No school",
      note: note,
      dateKey: dateKey,
      weekday: weekday
    };
  }

  if (!scheduleName) {
    return {
      success: true,
      schoolDay: true,
      scheduleName: "",
      phase: "manual",
      activeRosterPeriod: "",
      label: note || "No automatic schedule entered",
      note: note,
      dateKey: dateKey,
      weekday: weekday,
      matchedRule: matchedRule
    };
  }

  var data = kioskBellScheduleRows_();

  if (!data) {
    return {
      success: true,
      schoolDay: true,
      scheduleName: scheduleName,
      phase: "manual",
      activeRosterPeriod: "",
      label: "Bell Schedule sheet not found",
      note: note,
      dateKey: dateKey,
      weekday: weekday
    };
  }

  var blocks = [];

  data.forEach(function(row) {
    var enabled = String(row[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    var rowSchedule = String(row[1] || "").trim();

    if (enabled !== "ON" || rowSchedule !== scheduleName) return;

    var opens = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row[4]);
    var late = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row[5]);
    var ends = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row[6]);

    if (opens == null || late == null || ends == null) return;

    blocks.push({
      period: String(row[2] || "").trim(),
      rosterPeriod: String(row[3] || "").trim(),
      rosterOpens: String(row[4] || "").trim(),
      lateBell: String(row[5] || "").trim(),
      classEnds: String(row[6] || "").trim(),
      openMinutes: opens,
      lateMinutes: late,
      endMinutes: ends,
      blockType: String(row[7] || "Class").trim(),
      notes: String(row[8] || "").trim()
    });
  });

  blocks.sort(function(a,b){ return a.openMinutes - b.openMinutes; });

  // Morning arrival: open the first class of the day early.
  var arrivalMinutes = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(KIOSK_ARRIVAL_START_);
  var arrivalBlock = null;
  var arrivalOriginalOpen = null;

  if (
    arrivalMinutes != null &&
    blocks.length &&
    blocks[0].blockType.toLowerCase() === "class" &&
    blocks[0].openMinutes > arrivalMinutes
  ) {
    arrivalBlock = blocks[0];
    arrivalOriginalOpen = arrivalBlock.openMinutes;
    arrivalBlock.openMinutes = arrivalMinutes;
    arrivalBlock.rosterOpens = KIOSK_ARRIVAL_START_;
  }

  var active = null;

  for (var i = 0; i < blocks.length; i++) {
    if (
      currentMinutes >= blocks[i].openMinutes &&
      currentMinutes < blocks[i].endMinutes
    ) {
      active = blocks[i];
      break;
    }
  }

  if (!active) {
    var next = null;
    for (var j = 0; j < blocks.length; j++) {
      if (blocks[j].openMinutes > currentMinutes) {
        next = blocks[j];
        break;
      }
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      schoolDay: true,
      scheduleName: scheduleName,
      phase: next ? "beforeSchool" : "afterSchool",
      activeRosterPeriod: "",
      nextRosterPeriod: next ? next.rosterPeriod : "",
      label: next ? "Before " + next.period : "School Day Complete",
      note: note,
      dateKey: dateKey,
      weekday: weekday
    };
  }

  var isClass = active.blockType.toLowerCase() === "class";
  var isLunch = active.blockType.toLowerCase() === "lunch";

  // A lunch block (like 5B) has no passing period and no roster.
  var phase =
    isLunch
      ? "special"
      : (
          currentMinutes < active.lateMinutes
            ? "passing"
            : (isClass ? "class" : "special")
        );

  return {
    success: true,
    schoolDay: true,
    scheduleName: scheduleName,
    phase: phase,
    activeRosterPeriod: isClass ? active.rosterPeriod : "",
    period: active.period,
    rosterOpens: active.rosterOpens,
    lateBell: active.lateBell,
    classEnds: active.classEnds,
    label:
      phase === "passing"
        ? (
            active === arrivalBlock &&
            currentMinutes < arrivalOriginalOpen
              ? "Arrival — " + active.period
              : "Passing Period — " + active.period
          )
        : active.period,
    note: note || active.notes || "",
    dateKey: dateKey,
    weekday: weekday
  };
}



function setupAttendanceInterface__write_() {
  ensureAttendanceFoundation_();

  var settings = ensureSettingsSheet();
  settings.getRange("B12").setValue("ON");

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    success: true,
    attendanceEnabled: true,
    message: "Attendance interface enabled."
  };
}

function attendanceStudentKey_(studentId, studentName) {
  var id = String(studentId || "").trim();
  var name = String(studentName || "").trim().toLowerCase();

  return id ? "ID:" + id : "NAME:" + name;
}

function attendanceNowMinutes_(date, tz) {
  return (
    Number(Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, "H")) * 60 +
    Number(Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, "m")) +
    Number(Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, "s")) / 60
  );
}


/* ===== Late arrivals: "Late with Pass" vs "Tardy" =====
   Attendance Events column O = Late Type, column P = Minutes Late.
   The Status column still says Tardy for both kinds, so every existing
   rule (colors, finalizing, alerts) keeps working unchanged. */
var ATTENDANCE_LATE_TYPE_COLUMN_ = 15;
var ATTENDANCE_LATE_MINUTES_COLUMN_ = 16;
var ATTENDANCE_LATE_TARDY_ = "Tardy";
var ATTENDANCE_LATE_PASS_ = "Late with Pass";
var ATTENDANCE_LATE_COMMUTER_ = "Commuter";

// Is this check-in from a student marked Commuter = Yes for this period?
function kioskIsCommuter_(payload) {
  var period = String(payload && payload.period || "").trim();
  var key = attendanceStudentKey_(payload && payload.studentId, payload && payload.studentName);
  var found = false;

  (getStudentRoster() || []).some(function(group) {
    if (String(group.period || "").trim() !== period) return false;
    return (group.students || []).some(function(student) {
      if (attendanceStudentKey_(student.id, student.fullName) !== key) return false;
      found = !!student.commuter;
      return true;
    });
  });

  return found;
}

function kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, needed) {
  try {
    var have = sheet.getMaxColumns();
    if (have < needed) {
      sheet.insertColumnsAfter(have, needed - have);
    }
  } catch (err) {}
}

/* Whole minutes after the scheduled class start. Never less than 1 for a
   student who is past the grace period. */
function attendanceMinutesLate_(checkInMinutes, lateBellMinutes) {
  if (checkInMinutes == null || lateBellMinutes == null) {
    return "";
  }
  var diff = Number(checkInMinutes) - Number(lateBellMinutes);
  if (!isFinite(diff) || diff <= 0) {
    return "";
  }
  return Math.max(1, Math.round(diff));
}

function normalizeAttendanceLateType_(value) {
  var text = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  if (text.indexOf("commuter") !== -1) {
    return ATTENDANCE_LATE_COMMUTER_;
  }
  if (text.indexOf("pass") !== -1) {
    return ATTENDANCE_LATE_PASS_;
  }
  return ATTENDANCE_LATE_TARDY_;
}

/* Called by the kiosk when a late student answers the pop-up.
   No answer needs no call: late check-ins are saved as Tardy. */
function setAttendanceLateType(eventId, lateType) {
  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, ATTENDANCE_LATE_MINUTES_COLUMN_);
  var row = findAttendanceEventRow_(eventId);

  if (!row) {
    return { success: false, notFound: true };
  }

  var status = String(
    sheet.getRange(row, 11).getDisplayValue() || ""
  );

  if (status.indexOf("Tardy") === -1) {
    return { success: false, status: status };
  }

  var finalType = normalizeAttendanceLateType_(lateType);
  sheet.getRange(row, ATTENDANCE_LATE_TYPE_COLUMN_).setValue(finalType);
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    success: true,
    eventId: eventId,
    lateType: finalType
  };
}

function beginAttendanceCheckIn(payload) {
  ensureAttendanceFoundation_();

  payload = payload || {};

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var tz = ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() || Session.getScriptTimeZone();
  var now = new Date();

  var lateMinutes = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(
    String(payload.lateBell || "")
  );

  var nowMinutes = attendanceNowMinutes_(now, tz);

  var attendanceConfig =
    getAttendanceSettings();

  var tardyGraceMinutes =
    Number(attendanceConfig.tardyGraceSeconds || 30) / 60;

  var candidate =
    lateMinutes != null &&
    nowMinutes >= (lateMinutes + tardyGraceMinutes)
      ? "Tardy"
      : "Present";

  // Commuters are never marked tardy; the real arrival is still recorded.
  var commuterLate = candidate === "Tardy" && kioskIsCommuter_(payload);
  if (commuterLate) candidate = "Present";

  var confirmationSeconds =
    attendanceConfig.confirmationSeconds;

  var dueAt = new Date(
    now.getTime() + confirmationSeconds * 1000
  );

  // Stamp the student's photo immediately when the arrival tap
  // begins instead of waiting for the confirmation hold to finish.
  var enabledEmojiBank = getAttendanceEmojiBank();
  var preferredEmoji = String(
    payload.preferredEmoji || ""
  ).trim();

  var preferredAllowed =
    enabledEmojiBank.some(function(item) {
      return item.emoji === preferredEmoji;
    });

  var checkInEmoji =
    chooseAttendanceEmoji_(payload.studentId, payload.studentName) ||
    (preferredAllowed ? preferredEmoji : "");

  var result = appendAttendanceEvent_({
    date: now,
    schedule: payload.scheduleName || "",
    period: payload.period || "",
    className: payload.className || "",
    studentName: payload.studentName || "",
    studentId: payload.studentId || "",
    event: "Check-In Started",
    originalCheckIn: now,
    status: "Pending " + candidate,
    lateType: candidate === "Tardy" ? ATTENDANCE_LATE_TARDY_ : (commuterLate ? ATTENDANCE_LATE_COMMUTER_ : ""),
    minutesLate:
      candidate === "Tardy" || commuterLate
        ? attendanceMinutesLate_(nowMinutes, lateMinutes)
        : "",
    emoji: checkInEmoji,
    source: "Kiosk",
    notes:
      "Confirmation due " +
      Utilities.formatDate(dueAt, tz, "h:mm:ss a") +
      ". Tardy grace period: " +
      String(attendanceConfig.tardyGraceSeconds || 30) +
      " sec."
  });

  return {
    success: true,
    eventId: result.eventId,
    studentKey: attendanceStudentKey_(
      payload.studentId,
      payload.studentName
    ),
    statusCandidate: candidate,
    originalCheckInMillis: now.getTime(),
    originalCheckInText:
      Utilities.formatDate(now, tz, "h:mm:ss a"),
    dueAtMillis: dueAt.getTime(),
    confirmationSeconds: confirmationSeconds,
    emoji: checkInEmoji
  };
}

function findAttendanceEventRow_(eventId) {
  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();

  if (!eventId || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return null;
  }

  // Check-ins being confirmed or undone are almost always today's, so
  // look at today's rows first and only then at the whole tab.
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  var starts = [kioskTodayStartRow_(sheet, 2), 2];

  for (var s = 0; s < starts.length; s++) {
    var firstRow = starts[s];
    if (s > 0 && firstRow === starts[0]) break;
    if (firstRow > lastRow) continue;

    var ids = sheet
      .getRange(firstRow, 1, lastRow - firstRow + 1, 1)
      .getDisplayValues();

    for (var i = ids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (String(ids[i][0]) === String(eventId)) {
        return i + firstRow;
      }
    }
  }

  return null;
}


function markAttendanceEarlyDeparture(eventId) {
  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  var row = findAttendanceEventRow_(eventId);

  if (!row) {
    throw new Error("Attendance record could not be found.");
  }

  var currentStatus = String(
    sheet.getRange(row, 11).getDisplayValue() || ""
  );

  if (
    currentStatus !== "Present" &&
    currentStatus !== "Tardy"
  ) {
    return {
      success: false,
      status: currentStatus
    };
  }

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var tz = ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() || Session.getScriptTimeZone();
  var now = new Date();

  sheet.getRange(row, 8).setValue("Early Departure");
  sheet.getRange(row, 10).setValue(now);
  sheet.getRange(row, 11).setValue("Absent");
  // Keep the student's assigned emoji on the attendance record so a
  // student who leaves early and later returns receives the same avatar
  // for the rest of that school day.
  sheet.getRange(row, 14).setValue(
    "Marked absent again after arrival; interpreted as early departure."
  );

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    success: true,
    eventId: eventId,
    status: "Absent",
    earlyDeparture: true,
    departedAtMillis: now.getTime(),
    departedAtText:
      Utilities.formatDate(
        now,
        tz,
        "h:mm:ss a"
      )
  };
}


function batchBeginAttendanceCheckIns(payloads) {
  ensureAttendanceFoundation_();

  payloads = Array.isArray(payloads)
    ? payloads
    : [];

  if (!payloads.length) {
    return {
      success: true,
      results: []
    };
  }

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var tz =
    ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() ||
    Session.getScriptTimeZone();

  var sheet =
    ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();

  var attendanceConfig =
    getAttendanceSettings();

  var confirmationSeconds =
    Number(
      attendanceConfig.confirmationSeconds ||
      120
    );

  var tardyGraceMinutes =
    Number(
      attendanceConfig.tardyGraceSeconds ||
      30
    ) / 60;

  // Read the enabled bank once for the whole batch.
  var enabledEmojiBank =
    getAttendanceEmojiBank();

  var allowedEmoji = {};

  enabledEmojiBank.forEach(function(item) {
    allowedEmoji[
      String(item.emoji || "").trim()
    ] = true;
  });

  var rows = [];
  var results = [];

  payloads.forEach(function(payload) {
    payload = payload || {};

    // The browser sends the actual tap time so rapid taps
    // keep their real arrival order even if saved later.
    var tapMillis =
      Number(payload.clientTapMillis || 0);

    var tapTime =
      tapMillis > 0
        ? new Date(tapMillis)
        : new Date();

    var lateMinutes =
      parseScheduleClockMinutes_(
        String(payload.lateBell || "")
      );

    var tapMinutes =
      attendanceNowMinutes_(
        tapTime,
        tz
      );

    var candidate =
      lateMinutes != null &&
      tapMinutes >=
        (
          lateMinutes +
          tardyGraceMinutes
        )
        ? "Tardy"
        : "Present";

    // Commuters are never marked tardy; the real arrival is still recorded.
    var commuterLate = candidate === "Tardy" && kioskIsCommuter_(payload);
    if (commuterLate) candidate = "Present";

    var dueAt =
      new Date(
        tapTime.getTime() +
        confirmationSeconds * 1000
      );

    var preferredEmoji =
      String(
        payload.preferredEmoji || ""
      ).trim();

    var checkInEmoji =
      chooseAttendanceEmoji_(payload.studentId, payload.studentName) ||
      (allowedEmoji[preferredEmoji] ? preferredEmoji : "");

    var eventId =
      Utilities.getUuid();

    rows.push([
      eventId,
      tapTime,
      String(
        payload.scheduleName || ""
      ),
      String(
        payload.period || ""
      ),
      String(
        payload.className || ""
      ),
      String(
        payload.studentName || ""
      ),
      String(
        payload.studentId || ""
      ),
      "Check-In Started",
      tapTime,
      "",
      "Pending " + candidate,
      checkInEmoji,
      "Kiosk",
      "Confirmation due " +
        Utilities.formatDate(
          dueAt,
          tz,
          "h:mm:ss a"
        ) +
        ". Tardy grace period: " +
        String(
          attendanceConfig
            .tardyGraceSeconds ||
          30
        ) +
        " sec.",
      candidate === "Tardy"
        ? ATTENDANCE_LATE_TARDY_
        : (commuterLate ? ATTENDANCE_LATE_COMMUTER_ : ""),
      candidate === "Tardy" || commuterLate
        ? attendanceMinutesLate_(tapMinutes, lateMinutes)
        : ""
    ]);

    results.push({
      success: true,
      clientRequestId:
        String(
          payload.clientRequestId ||
          ""
        ),
      eventId: eventId,
      studentKey:
        attendanceStudentKey_(
          payload.studentId,
          payload.studentName
        ),
      statusCandidate: candidate,
      commuter: commuterLate,
      minutesLate:
        candidate === "Tardy" || commuterLate
          ? attendanceMinutesLate_(tapMinutes, lateMinutes)
          : "",
      originalCheckInMillis:
        tapTime.getTime(),
      originalCheckInText:
        Utilities.formatDate(
          tapTime,
          tz,
          "h:mm:ss a"
        ),
      dueAtMillis:
        dueAt.getTime(),
      confirmationSeconds:
        confirmationSeconds,
      emoji:
        checkInEmoji
    });
  });

  if (rows.length) {
    kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, ATTENDANCE_LATE_MINUTES_COLUMN_);
    sheet
      .getRange(
        sheet.getLastRow() + 1,
        1,
        rows.length,
        16
      )
      .setValues(rows);
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    results: results
  };
}

function cancelAttendanceCheckIn(eventId) {
  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  var row = findAttendanceEventRow_(eventId);

  if (!row) {
    throw new Error("Attendance check-in could not be found.");
  }

  var status = String(
    sheet.getRange(row, 11).getDisplayValue() || ""
  );

  if (status.indexOf("Pending ") !== 0) {
    return {
      success: false,
      alreadyFinalized: true,
      status: status
    };
  }

  sheet.getRange(row, 8).setValue("Check-In Cancelled");
  sheet.getRange(row, 11).setValue("Cancelled");
  sheet.getRange(row, 14).setValue(
    "Student cancelled before confirmation."
  );

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    success: true,
    eventId: eventId,
    status: "Cancelled"
  };
}

function confirmAttendanceCheckIn(eventId) {
  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  var row = findAttendanceEventRow_(eventId);

  if (!row) {
    throw new Error("Attendance check-in could not be found.");
  }

  var currentStatus = String(
    sheet.getRange(row, 11).getDisplayValue() || ""
  );

  if (
    currentStatus === "Present" ||
    currentStatus === "Tardy"
  ) {
    var existingEmoji = String(
      sheet.getRange(row, 12).getDisplayValue() || ""
    );

    return {
      success: true,
      eventId: eventId,
      status: currentStatus,
      emoji: existingEmoji,
      alreadyConfirmed: true
    };
  }

  if (currentStatus === "Cancelled") {
    return {
      success: false,
      cancelled: true,
      status: "Cancelled"
    };
  }

  var finalStatus =
    currentStatus.indexOf("Tardy") !== -1
      ? "Tardy"
      : "Present";

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var tz = ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() || Session.getScriptTimeZone();
  var now = new Date();

  var emoji = String(
    sheet.getRange(row, 12).getDisplayValue() || ""
  );

  if (!emoji) {
    emoji = chooseAttendanceEmoji_(
      sheet.getRange(row, 7).getDisplayValue(),
      sheet.getRange(row, 6).getDisplayValue()
    );
  }

  var originalValue = sheet.getRange(row, 9).getValue();
  var originalMillis =
    originalValue instanceof Date
      ? originalValue.getTime()
      : now.getTime();

  sheet.getRange(row, 8).setValue("Check-In Confirmed");
  sheet.getRange(row, 10).setValue(now);
  sheet.getRange(row, 11).setValue(finalStatus);
  sheet.getRange(row, 12).setValue(emoji);
  sheet.getRange(row, 14).setValue(
    "Confirmed after attendance hold."
  );

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    success: true,
    eventId: eventId,
    status: finalStatus,
    emoji: emoji,
    originalCheckInMillis: originalMillis,
    originalCheckInText:
      Utilities.formatDate(
        new Date(originalMillis),
        tz,
        "h:mm:ss a"
      ),
    confirmedAtMillis: now.getTime()
  };
}


function resetAttendanceEmojisForToday__write_() {
  ensureAttendanceFoundation_();

  var ss =
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  var tz =
    ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() ||
    Session.getScriptTimeZone();

  // Hand out a fresh set of emojis for today (still all different and
  // still different from yesterday's).
  var newPlan = kioskEmojiPlanForToday_(true);
  var emojiPlan = { date: newPlan.date, emojis: newPlan.emojis };

  var sheet =
    ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return {
      success: true,
      resetCount: 0,
      emojiPlan: emojiPlan
    };
  }

  var firstRow =
    kioskTodayStartRow_(sheet, 2);

  var rowCount =
    sheet.getLastRow() - firstRow + 1;

  if (rowCount < 1) {
    return {
      success: true,
      resetCount: 0,
      emojiPlan: emojiPlan
    };
  }

  var values =
    sheet.getRange(
      firstRow,
      1,
      rowCount,
      14
    ).getValues();

  var display =
    sheet.getRange(
      firstRow,
      1,
      rowCount,
      14
    ).getDisplayValues();

  var todayKey =
    normalizeScheduleDateKey_(
      Utilities.formatDate(
        new Date(),
        tz,
        "M/d/yyyy"
      )
    );

  var studentKeys = {};
  var clearedRows = 0;

  var emojiColumn =
    display.map(function(row) {
      return [
        String(row[11] || "")
      ];
    });

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    var dateValue =
      values[i][1];

    var rowDateKey =
      dateValue instanceof Date
        ? Utilities.formatDate(
            dateValue,
            tz,
            "M/d/yyyy"
          )
        : display[i][1];

    if (
      normalizeScheduleDateKey_(
        rowDateKey
      ) !== todayKey
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    var status =
      String(
        display[i][10] || ""
      ).trim();

    if (status === "Cancelled") {
      continue;
    }

    var existingEmoji =
      String(
        display[i][11] || ""
      ).trim();

    if (!existingEmoji) {
      continue;
    }

    var studentKey =
      attendanceStudentKey_(
        display[i][6],
        display[i][5]
      );

    emojiColumn[i][0] = newPlan.emojis[studentKey] || "";
    clearedRows += 1;

    if (studentKey) {
      studentKeys[studentKey] = true;
    }
  }

  if (clearedRows) {
    sheet
      .getRange(
        firstRow,
        12,
        rowCount,
        1
      )
      .setValues(
        emojiColumn
      );

    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }

  return {
    success: true,
    resetCount:
      Object.keys(
        studentKeys
      ).length,
    clearedRows:
      clearedRows,
    emojiPlan:
      emojiPlan
  };
}


function getAttendanceState(context) {
  ensureAttendanceFoundation_();

  context = context || {};

  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  var settings = getAttendanceSettings();

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return {
      success: true,
      states: [],
      settings: settings
    };
  }

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var tz = ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() || Session.getScriptTimeZone();
  var todayKey = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), tz, "M/d/yyyy");

  kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, ATTENDANCE_LATE_MINUTES_COLUMN_);

  // Only today's rows (they are at the bottom of the tab).
  var firstRow = kioskTodayStartRow_(sheet, 2);
  var rowCount = sheet.getLastRow() - firstRow + 1;

  var values = rowCount > 0
    ? sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1, rowCount, 16).getValues()
    : [];

  var display = rowCount > 0
    ? sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1, rowCount, 16).getDisplayValues()
    : [];

  var wantedPeriod = String(context.period || "").trim();
  var wantedClass = String(context.className || "").trim();

  var latest = {};

  for (var i = 0; i < values.length; i++) {
    var dateValue = values[i][1];
    var rowDateKey = dateValue instanceof Date
      ? Utilities.formatDate(dateValue, tz, "M/d/yyyy")
      : normalizeScheduleDateKey_(display[i][1]);

    if (rowDateKey !== normalizeScheduleDateKey_(todayKey)) {
      continue;
    }

    if (
      wantedPeriod &&
      String(display[i][3] || "").trim() !== wantedPeriod
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    if (
      wantedClass &&
      String(display[i][4] || "").trim() !== wantedClass
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    var key = attendanceStudentKey_(
      display[i][6],
      display[i][5]
    );

    latest[key] = {
      row: i + firstRow,
      eventId: String(display[i][0] || ""),
      studentName: String(display[i][5] || ""),
      studentId: String(display[i][6] || ""),
      event: String(display[i][7] || ""),
      originalCheckIn:
        values[i][8] instanceof Date
          ? values[i][8]
          : null,
      confirmedAt:
        values[i][9] instanceof Date
          ? values[i][9]
          : null,
      status: String(display[i][10] || ""),
      emoji: String(display[i][11] || ""),
      lateType: String(display[i][14] || "").trim(),
      minutesLate: String(display[i][15] || "").trim()
    };
  }

  var now = new Date();
  var states = [];

  Object.keys(latest).forEach(function(key) {
    var item = latest[key];

    if (item.status === "Cancelled") {
      return;
    }

    if (
      item.status.indexOf("Pending ") === 0 &&
      item.originalCheckIn
    ) {
      var dueMillis =
        item.originalCheckIn.getTime() +
        settings.confirmationSeconds * 1000;

      if (now.getTime() >= dueMillis) {
        var confirmed = confirmAttendanceCheckIn(
          item.eventId
        );

        if (confirmed && confirmed.success) {
          item.status = confirmed.status;
          item.emoji = confirmed.emoji;
          item.confirmedAt = new Date(
            confirmed.confirmedAtMillis || now.getTime()
          );
        }
      }
    }

    var originalMillis =
      item.originalCheckIn
        ? item.originalCheckIn.getTime()
        : 0;

    states.push({
      studentKey: key,
      eventId: item.eventId,
      status: item.status,
      emoji: item.emoji,
      lateType: item.lateType || "",
      minutesLate: item.minutesLate || "",
      earlyDeparture:
        item.event === "Early Departure",
      departedAtText:
        item.event === "Early Departure" &&
        item.confirmedAt
          ? Utilities.formatDate(
              item.confirmedAt,
              tz,
              "h:mm:ss a"
            )
          : "",
      originalCheckInMillis: originalMillis,
      originalCheckInText:
        item.originalCheckIn
          ? Utilities.formatDate(
              item.originalCheckIn,
              tz,
              "h:mm:ss a"
            )
          : "",
      dueAtMillis:
        item.status.indexOf("Pending ") === 0 &&
        item.originalCheckIn
          ? originalMillis +
            settings.confirmationSeconds * 1000
          : 0
    });
  });

  return {
    success: true,
    states: states,
    settings: settings
  };
}

function normalizeRosterPeriodForMatch_(value) {
  return String(value || "")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, "")
    .replace(/period/g, "")
    .replace(/st|nd|rd|th/g, "");
}

function checkArrivalScheduleMatch(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Bell Schedule");

  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return {
      level: "none",
      matchingSchedules: []
    };
  }

  var tz = ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() || Session.getScriptTimeZone();
  var now = new Date();
  var nowMinutes = attendanceNowMinutes_(now, tz);

  var wantedPeriod = normalizeRosterPeriodForMatch_(
    payload.period
  );

  var expected = String(
    payload.expectedSchedule || ""
  ).trim();

  var rows = kioskRowsByHeading_(sheet, KIOSK_BELL_COLS_, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1);

  var matches = [];

  // Morning arrival: the first class of each schedule opens early.
  var arrivalMinutes = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(KIOSK_ARRIVAL_START_);
  var firstOpenBySchedule = {};

  rows.forEach(function(row) {
    if (String(row[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() !== "ON") return;

    var scheduleName = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    var rowOpens = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row[4]);
    var rowLate = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row[5]);
    var rowEnds = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row[6]);

    if (
      !scheduleName ||
      rowOpens == null ||
      rowLate == null ||
      rowEnds == null
    ) {
      return;
    }

    if (
      !firstOpenBySchedule[scheduleName] ||
      rowOpens < firstOpenBySchedule[scheduleName].opens
    ) {
      firstOpenBySchedule[scheduleName] = {
        opens: rowOpens,
        isClass:
          String(row[7] || "Class").trim().toLowerCase() === "class"
      };
    }
  });

  rows.forEach(function(row) {
    if (
      String(row[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() !== "ON"
    ) {
      return;
    }

    var schedule = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    var rosterPeriod = normalizeRosterPeriodForMatch_(
      row[3]
    );

    if (!schedule || rosterPeriod !== wantedPeriod) {
      return;
    }

    var opens = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row[4]);
    var late = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row[5]);

    var firstOfDay = firstOpenBySchedule[schedule];

    if (
      arrivalMinutes != null &&
      opens != null &&
      firstOfDay &&
      firstOfDay.isClass &&
      firstOfDay.opens === opens &&
