      opens > arrivalMinutes
    ) {
      opens = arrivalMinutes;
    }

    if (
      opens != null &&
      late != null &&
      nowMinutes >= opens &&
      nowMinutes < late
    ) {
      if (matches.indexOf(schedule) === -1) {
        matches.push(schedule);
      }
    }
  });

  var expectedMatches =
    expected && matches.indexOf(expected) !== -1;

  if (expectedMatches) {
    return {
      level: "normal",
      expectedSchedule: expected,
      matchingSchedules: matches
    };
  }

  if (matches.length === 1) {
    return {
      level: "strong",
      expectedSchedule: expected,
      suggestedSchedule: matches[0],
      matchingSchedules: matches
    };
  }

  if (matches.length > 1) {
    return {
      level: "possible",
      expectedSchedule: expected,
      matchingSchedules: matches
    };
  }

  return {
    level: "unusual",
    expectedSchedule: expected,
    matchingSchedules: []
  };
}

function setupAttendanceFoundation__write_() {
  ensureAttendanceFoundation_();
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    success: true,
    sheets: [
      "Attendance",
      "Attendance Events",
      "Attendance Emoji Bank"
    ],
    message: "Attendance foundation is ready."
  };
}

function ensureAttendanceFoundation__full_() {
  ensureSettingsSheet();
  ensureAttendanceSummarySheet_();
  ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  ensureAttendanceEmojiBankSheet_();
  ensureAttendanceReportSettingsRows_();

  return true;
}

function ensureAttendanceSummarySheet__full_() {
  var ss =
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  var sheet =
    ss.getSheetByName(
      "Attendance"
    );

  if (!sheet) {
    sheet =
      ss.insertSheet(
        "Attendance"
      );
  }

  if (
    String(
      sheet.getRange("R1").getDisplayValue() || ""
    ).trim() ===
      "Arrived Late"
  ) {
    sheet.deleteColumn(18);
  }

  var headers = [
    "Date",
    "Schedule",
    "Period",
    "Class",
    "Present Students",
    "Tardy Students",
    "Absent Students",
    "Never Returned",
    "Present Count",
    "Tardy Count",
    "Absent Count",
    "Finalized At",
    "Consecutive Absence Alerts",
    "Total Enrolled",
    "Early Departures",
    "Report Status",
    "Last Updated",
    "Left Early",
    "Bathroom",
    "Water",
    "Counselor",
    "Library",
    "Locker",
    "AP Office",
    "Nurse",
    "Chromebook Help Desk",
    "Bookstore",
    "Main Office",
    "Printer",
    "Cafeteria",
    "Lost & Found",
    "Back to my last class",
    "Other",
    "Late with Pass Students",
    "Late with Pass Count"
  ];

  kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, headers.length);

  var currentHeaders = sheet
    .getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length)
    .getDisplayValues()[0];

  var headersMatch = headers.every(function(header, index) {
    return String(currentHeaders[index] || "").trim() === header;
  });

  if (headersMatch) {
    return sheet;
  }

  sheet
    .getRange(
      1,
      1,
      1,
      headers.length
    )
    .setValues([headers]);

  sheet.setFrozenRows(1);

  sheet
    .getRange(
      1,
      1,
      1,
      headers.length
    )
    .setFontWeight("bold")
    .setBackground("#17365D")
    .setFontColor("#FFFFFF");

  sheet
    .getRange("A:A")
    .setNumberFormat(
      "ddd m/d/yy"
    );

  sheet
    .getRange("L:L")
    .setNumberFormat(
      "m/d/yyyy h:mm:ss AM/PM"
    );

  sheet
    .getRange("Q:Q")
    .setNumberFormat(
      "m/d/yyyy h:mm:ss AM/PM"
    );

  sheet
    .getRange("E:AH")
    .setWrap(true);

  return sheet;
}

function ensureAttendanceEventsSheet__full_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Attendance Events");

  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet("Attendance Events");
  }

  var headers = [
    "Event ID",
    "Date",
    "Schedule",
    "Period",
    "Class",
    "Student Name",
    "Student ID",
    "Event",
    "Original Check-In",
    "Confirmed At",
    "Status",
    "Emoji",
    "Source",
    "Notes",
    "Late Type",
    "Minutes Late"
  ];

  kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, headers.length);

  var currentHeaders = sheet
    .getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length)
    .getDisplayValues()[0];

  var headersMatch = headers.every(function(header, index) {
    return String(currentHeaders[index] || "").trim() === header;
  });

  if (headersMatch) {
    return sheet;
  }

  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([headers]);
  sheet.setFrozenRows(1);

  sheet.getRange("A1:P1")
    .setFontWeight("bold")
    .setBackground("#17365D")
    .setFontColor("#FFFFFF");

  sheet.getRange("B:B").setNumberFormat("m/d/yyyy");
  sheet.getRange("I:J").setNumberFormat("h:mm:ss AM/PM");
  sheet.getRange("N:N").setWrap(true);

  return sheet;
}

function ensureAttendanceEmojiBankSheet__full_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Attendance Emoji Bank");

  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet("Attendance Emoji Bank");
  }

  var rows = [
    ["ON", "😎", "Funny Faces", "Sunglasses"],
    ["ON", "🤓", "Funny Faces", "Glasses"],
    ["ON", "🥳", "Funny Faces", "Party"],
    ["ON", "🤠", "Funny Faces", "Cowboy"],
    ["ON", "🤩", "Funny Faces", "Star eyes"],
    ["ON", "😂", "Funny Faces", "Laughing"],
    ["ON", "😴", "Funny Faces", "Sleepy"],
    ["ON", "😜", "Funny Faces", "Silly"],

    ["ON", "🧸", "Fun", "Teddy bear"],
    ["ON", "🎀", "Fun", "Bow"],
    ["ON", "🎈", "Fun", "Balloon"],
    ["ON", "🎉", "Fun", "Party popper"],
    ["ON", "🎁", "Fun", "Gift"],
    ["ON", "👑", "Fun", "Crown"],
    ["ON", "💎", "Fun", "Gem"],
    ["ON", "🚀", "Fun", "Rocket"],
    ["ON", "🛸", "Fun", "Flying saucer"],
    ["ON", "👽", "Fun", "Alien"],
    ["ON", "🤖", "Fun", "Robot"],
    ["ON", "👻", "Fun", "Ghost"],
    ["ON", "👂", "Hearing & Deaf", "Ear"],
    ["ON", "🦻", "Hearing & Deaf", "Ear with hearing aid"],
    ["ON", "🤟", "Hearing & Deaf", "ILY hand"],
    ["ON", "👋", "Hearing & Deaf", "Waving hand"],
    ["ON", "🙌", "Hearing & Deaf", "Raised hands"],
    ["ON", "CUSTOM_WHITE_DOG", "Custom Dogs", "Joey custom attendance stamp — same odds as other enabled emojis"],
    ["ON", "CUSTOM_YORKIE", "Custom Dogs", "Jaq custom attendance stamp — same odds as other enabled emojis"]
  ];

  var expectedHeaders = [
    "Enabled",
    "Emoji / Stamp",
    "Category",
    "Notes"
  ];

  if (sheet.getLastRow() >= rows.length + 1) {
    var currentHeaders = sheet
      .getRange(1, 1, 1, expectedHeaders.length)
      .getDisplayValues()[0];

    var headersMatch = expectedHeaders.every(function(header, index) {
      return String(currentHeaders[index] || "").trim() === header;
    });

    if (headersMatch) {
      return sheet;
    }
  }

  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 4).setValues([[
    "Enabled",
    "Emoji / Stamp",
    "Category",
    "Notes"
  ]]);

  // Preserve existing rows and their ON/OFF choices.
  // Append only bank items that are not already present.
  var existing = {};

  if (sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    sheet
      .getRange(2, 2, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1)
      .getDisplayValues()
      .forEach(function(row) {
        var value = String(row[0] || "").trim();
        if (value) existing[value] = true;
      });
  }

  var missing = rows.filter(function(row) {
    return !existing[String(row[1] || "").trim()];
  });

  if (missing.length) {
    sheet
      .getRange(
        sheet.getLastRow() + 1,
        1,
        missing.length,
        4
      )
      .setValues(missing);
  }

  sheet.getRange("A1:D1")
    .setFontWeight("bold")
    .setBackground("#17365D")
    .setFontColor("#FFFFFF");

  sheet.setFrozenRows(1);

  var enabledRule = SpreadsheetApp.newDataValidation()
    .requireValueInList(["ON", "OFF"], true)
    .setAllowInvalid(false)
    .build();

  sheet.getRange(
    2,
    1,
    Math.max(sheet.getMaxRows() - 1, 1),
    1
  ).setDataValidation(enabledRule);

  sheet.autoResizeColumns(1, 4);

  return sheet;
}



function ensureUncheckedOutTimingSetting__full_() {
  var sheet = ensureSettingsSheet();
  var range = sheet.getRange("A30:C30");
  var row = range.getValues()[0];
  var changed = false;

  if (!String(row[0] || "").trim()) {
    row[0] = "Unchecked Students Move to Out Rail";
    changed = true;
  }

  if (!String(row[1] || "").trim()) {
    row[1] = "5 min";
    changed = true;
  }

  if (!String(row[2] || "").trim()) {
    row[2] = "After the Late Bell, move students who never checked in to Currently Out of the Classroom after this many minutes.";
    changed = true;
  }

  if (changed) {
    range.setValues([row]);
  }

  return sheet;
}


function ensureAttendanceReportSettingsRows__full_() {
  var sheet = ensureSettingsSheet();

  var rows = [
    ["Attendance Reports Enabled", "ON", "Automatically create one Attendance summary row per class for each school day."],
    ["Attendance Report Time", "15:15", "Daily time to generate/update attendance summary rows. Email, when enabled, is sent immediately afterward."],
    ["Email Attendance Report", "ON", "Send the completed daily attendance summary by email after the report is generated."],
    ["Attendance Report Email", "", "Email address that receives the daily attendance summary."],
    ["Report Include Present Students", "ON", "Include names of Present students in the daily summary."],
    ["Report Include Tardy Students", "ON", "Include names of Tardy students in the daily summary."],
    ["Report Include Absent Students", "ON", "Include names of Absent students in the daily summary."],
    ["Report Include Never Returned", "ON", "Include students who were still out of the classroom when the report was finalized."],
    ["Report Include Early Departures", "ON", "Include students who checked in and later left early."],
    ["Report Include Counts", "ON", "Include Present, Tardy, Absent, and Total Enrolled counts."],
    ["Report Include Consecutive Absence Alerts", "ON", "Include students who have reached the configured consecutive-absence alert threshold."],
    ["Attendance Report Reconcile", "ON", "Update an existing class/date report row when later attendance corrections are made instead of creating a duplicate row."]
  ];

  var startRow = 31;
  var range = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, rows.length, 3);
  var current = range.getValues();
  var changed = false;

  for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
    if (!String(current[i][0] || "").trim()) {
      current[i][0] = rows[i][0];
      changed = true;
    }

    if (!String(current[i][1] || "").trim() && String(rows[i][1] || "")) {
      current[i][1] = rows[i][1];
      changed = true;
    }

    if (!String(current[i][2] || "").trim()) {
      current[i][2] = rows[i][2];
      changed = true;
    }
  }

  if (changed) {
    range.setValues(current);
  }

  return sheet;
}

function normalizeAttendanceReportTime_(value) {
  var text =
    String(
      value || ""
    ).trim();

  var match =
    text.match(
      /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/
    );

  if (!match) {
    return "15:15";
  }

  var hour =
    Number(match[1]);

  var minute =
    Number(match[2]);

  if (
    !isFinite(hour) ||
    !isFinite(minute) ||
    hour < 0 ||
    hour > 23 ||
    minute < 0 ||
    minute > 59
  ) {
    return "15:15";
  }

  return (
    String(hour).padStart(2, "0") +
    ":" +
    String(minute).padStart(2, "0")
  );
}

function saveAttendanceSettings__write_(settings) {
  ensureAttendanceFoundation_();

  settings = settings || {};

  var sheet =
    ensureAttendanceReportSettingsRows_();

  ensureUncheckedOutTimingSetting_();

  var enabled =
    settings.enabled === true ||
    String(settings.enabled || "").trim().toUpperCase() === "ON";

  var confirmationMinutes = parseMinuteValue(
    settings.confirmationMinutes,
    2
  );

  confirmationMinutes = Math.max(
    0.5,
    Math.min(10, confirmationMinutes)
  );

  var threshold = Number(
    settings.absenceAlertThreshold || 3
  );

  if (!isFinite(threshold)) threshold = 3;
  threshold = Math.max(1, Math.min(10, Math.floor(threshold)));

  var finalization = String(
    settings.finalization || "Class End"
  ).trim();

  if (
    ["Class End", "End of Day"].indexOf(finalization) === -1
  ) {
    finalization = "Class End";
  }

  var emojiMode = String(
    settings.emojiMode || "Random Bank"
  ).trim();

  if (
    emojiMode !== "Random Bank" &&
    emojiMode !== "Solid Colors"
  ) {
    emojiMode = "Random Bank";
  }

  var tardyGraceSeconds = parseSecondValue(
    settings.tardyGraceSeconds,
    30
  );

  tardyGraceSeconds = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(300, tardyGraceSeconds)
  );

  var uncheckedOutMinutes =
    Number(
      settings.uncheckedOutMinutes
    );

  if (!isFinite(uncheckedOutMinutes)) {
    uncheckedOutMinutes = 5;
  }

  uncheckedOutMinutes =
    Math.max(
      0,
      Math.min(
        30,
        uncheckedOutMinutes
      )
    );

  var reportEnabled =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings.reportEnabled,
      true
    );

  var reportTime =
    normalizeAttendanceReportTime_(
      settings.reportTime
    );

  var reportEmailEnabled =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings.reportEmailEnabled,
      true
    );

  var reportEmail =
    String(
      settings.reportEmail || ""
    ).trim();

  var includePresent =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings.reportIncludePresent,
      true
    );

  var includeTardy =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings.reportIncludeTardy,
      true
    );

  var includeAbsent =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings.reportIncludeAbsent,
      true
    );

  var includeNeverReturned =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings.reportIncludeNeverReturned,
      true
    );

  var includeEarlyDepartures =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings.reportIncludeEarlyDepartures,
      true
    );

  var includeCounts =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings.reportIncludeCounts,
      true
    );

  var includeAbsenceAlerts =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings.reportIncludeAbsenceAlerts,
      true
    );

  var reportReconcile =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings.reportReconcile,
      true
    );

  sheet.getRange("B12").setValue(enabled ? "ON" : "OFF");
  sheet.getRange("B13").setValue(confirmationMinutes + " min");
  sheet.getRange("B14").setValue(threshold);
  sheet.getRange("B15").setValue(finalization);

  sheet.getRange("A16:C16").setValues([[
    "Attendance Card Style",
    emojiMode,
    "Emoji Slot Machine keeps the emoji/photo area. Solid Colors uses the full card for the student name. Both styles use RED absent, GREEN present/tardy, and YELLOW out of room."
  ]]);

  sheet.getRange("B17").setValue(tardyGraceSeconds + " sec");
  sheet.getRange("B30").setValue(uncheckedOutMinutes + " min");

  sheet
    .getRange("B31:B42")
    .setValues([
      [reportEnabled ? "ON" : "OFF"],
      [reportTime],
      [reportEmailEnabled ? "ON" : "OFF"],
      [reportEmail],
      [includePresent ? "ON" : "OFF"],
      [includeTardy ? "ON" : "OFF"],
      [includeAbsent ? "ON" : "OFF"],
      [includeNeverReturned ? "ON" : "OFF"],
      [includeEarlyDepartures ? "ON" : "OFF"],
      [includeCounts ? "ON" : "OFF"],
      [includeAbsenceAlerts ? "ON" : "OFF"],
      [reportReconcile ? "ON" : "OFF"]
    ]);

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  try {
    syncAttendanceReportTrigger_();
  } catch (triggerError) {
    /* Trigger authorization can be completed later; settings still save. */
  }

  return getAttendanceSettings();
}


function ensureAttendanceUiEnabledOnce_() {
  ensureAttendanceFoundation_();

  var sheet = ensureSettingsSheet();

  var migrationLabel = String(
    sheet.getRange("A18").getDisplayValue() || ""
  ).trim();

  var migrationValue = String(
    sheet.getRange("B18").getDisplayValue() || ""
  ).trim();

  if (
    migrationLabel !== "Attendance UI Initialized" ||
    migrationValue !== "YES"
  ) {
    sheet.getRange("A18").setValue("Attendance UI Initialized");
    sheet.getRange("B18").setValue("YES");
    sheet.getRange("C18").setValue(
      "One-time migration: turns Attendance ON so the check-in interface is visible. After this, the Attendance setting can be turned off normally."
    );

    sheet.getRange("B12").setValue("ON");
    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
  }
}

function getAttendanceSettings__read_() {
  var sheet = ensureAttendanceReportSettingsRows_();
  ensureUncheckedOutTimingSetting_();

  var migration = sheet
    .getRange("A18:C18")
    .getDisplayValues()[0];

  if (
    String(migration[0] || "").trim() !== "Attendance UI Initialized" ||
    String(migration[1] || "").trim() !== "YES"
  ) {
    sheet.getRange("A18:C18").setValues([[
      "Attendance UI Initialized",
      "YES",
      "One-time migration: turns Attendance ON so the check-in interface is visible. After this, the Attendance setting can be turned off normally."
    ]]);

    sheet.getRange("B12").setValue("ON");
  }

  var values = sheet
    .getRange("B12:B42")
    .getDisplayValues()
    .map(function(row) {
      return row[0];
    });

  function valueAt(rowNumber, fallback) {
    var value = values[rowNumber - 12];
    return value == null || value === "" ? fallback : value;
  }

  var enabled = String(
    valueAt(12, "ON")
  ).trim().toUpperCase();

  var confirmationMinutes = parseMinuteValue(
    valueAt(13, "2 min"),
    2
  );

  var absenceAlertThreshold = Number(
    valueAt(14, "3")
  );

  if (!isFinite(absenceAlertThreshold) || absenceAlertThreshold < 1) {
    absenceAlertThreshold = 3;
  }

  var finalization = String(
    valueAt(15, "Class End")
  ).trim();

  var emojiMode = String(
    valueAt(16, "Random Bank")
  ).trim();

  if (
    emojiMode !== "Random Bank" &&
    emojiMode !== "Solid Colors"
  ) {
    emojiMode = "Random Bank";
  }

  var tardyGraceSeconds = parseSecondValue(
    valueAt(17, "30 sec"),
    30
  );

  tardyGraceSeconds = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(300, tardyGraceSeconds)
  );

  var uncheckedOutMinutes = parseMinuteValue(
    valueAt(30, "5 min"),
    5
  );

  uncheckedOutMinutes = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(30, uncheckedOutMinutes)
  );

  var reportEnabled = kioskSettingOn_(
    valueAt(31, "ON"),
    true
  );

  var reportTime = normalizeAttendanceReportTime_(
    valueAt(32, "15:15")
  );

  var reportEmailEnabled = kioskSettingOn_(
    valueAt(33, "ON"),
    true
  );

  var reportEmail = String(
    valueAt(34, "")
  ).trim();

  if (!reportEmail) {
    try {
      reportEmail = String(
        Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail() || ""
      ).trim();
    } catch (error) {
      reportEmail = "";
    }
  }

  var reportIncludePresent = kioskSettingOn_(valueAt(35, "ON"), true);
  var reportIncludeTardy = kioskSettingOn_(valueAt(36, "ON"), true);
  var reportIncludeAbsent = kioskSettingOn_(valueAt(37, "ON"), true);
  var reportIncludeNeverReturned = kioskSettingOn_(valueAt(38, "ON"), true);
  var reportIncludeEarlyDepartures = kioskSettingOn_(valueAt(39, "ON"), true);
  var reportIncludeCounts = kioskSettingOn_(valueAt(40, "ON"), true);
  var reportIncludeAbsenceAlerts = kioskSettingOn_(valueAt(41, "ON"), true);
  var reportReconcile = kioskSettingOn_(valueAt(42, "ON"), true);

  return {
    enabled: enabled === "ON",
    confirmationMinutes: confirmationMinutes,
    confirmationSeconds: Math.round(confirmationMinutes * 60),
    absenceAlertThreshold: Math.floor(absenceAlertThreshold),
    finalization: finalization,
    emojiMode: emojiMode,
    tardyGraceSeconds: tardyGraceSeconds,
    uncheckedOutMinutes: uncheckedOutMinutes,
    reportEnabled: reportEnabled,
    reportTime: reportTime,
    reportEmailEnabled: reportEmailEnabled,
    reportEmail: reportEmail,
    reportIncludePresent: reportIncludePresent,
    reportIncludeTardy: reportIncludeTardy,
    reportIncludeAbsent: reportIncludeAbsent,
    reportIncludeNeverReturned: reportIncludeNeverReturned,
    reportIncludeEarlyDepartures: reportIncludeEarlyDepartures,
    reportIncludeCounts: reportIncludeCounts,
    reportIncludeAbsenceAlerts: reportIncludeAbsenceAlerts,
    reportReconcile: reportReconcile
  };
}

function getAttendanceEmojiBank__read_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Attendance Emoji Bank");

  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    sheet = ensureAttendanceEmojiBankSheet_();
  }

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    return [];
  }

  var rows = sheet
    .getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 4)
    .getDisplayValues();

  return rows
    .filter(function(row) {
      return (
        String(row[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() !== "OFF" &&
        String(row[1] || "").trim()
      );
    })
    .map(function(row) {
      return {
        emoji: String(row[1] || "").trim(),
        category: String(row[2] || "").trim(),
        notes: String(row[3] || "").trim()
      };
    });
}

// Returns this student's emoji from today's emoji plan. Without a
// student, falls back to a random emoji from the bank.
function chooseAttendanceEmoji_(studentId, studentName) {
  if (studentId || studentName) {
    try {
      var planned = kioskEmojiForStudent_(
        attendanceStudentKey_(studentId, studentName)
      );
      if (planned) return planned;
    } catch (planErr) {}
  }

  var options = kioskEmojiBankList_();

  if (!options.length) {
    return "🐸";
  }

  return options[Math.floor(Math.random() * options.length)];
}

/* ===== Daily emoji plan =====
   Each school day every student gets one emoji that nobody else has that
   day (across all classes) and that is different from the student's own
   emoji the day before. The plan is built once per day for the whole
   roster and kept in Script Properties:
     { date: "yyyy-MM-dd", emojis: { studentKey: emoji },
       yesterday: { studentKey: emoji } }
   If the roster is bigger than the emoji bank, the extra students share
   the least-used emojis (still never their own emoji from yesterday). */

var KIOSK_EMOJI_PLAN_PROP_ = "attendanceEmojiPlan";
var kioskEmojiPlanMemo_ = null;

function kioskEmojiBankList_() {
  var seen = {};
  var list = [];

  (getAttendanceEmojiBank() || []).forEach(function(item) {
    var emoji = String(item && item.emoji || "").trim();
    if (emoji && !seen[emoji]) {
      seen[emoji] = true;
      list.push(emoji);
    }
  });

  return list;
}

function kioskShuffle_(list) {
  var copy = list.slice();
  for (var i = copy.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    var j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    var t = copy[i]; copy[i] = copy[j]; copy[j] = t;
  }
  return copy;
}

// Pure: gives each key in `keys` an emoji. `assigned` holds emojis already
// handed out today (kept as they are). Returns the new assignments only.
function kioskAssignEmojis_(keys, bank, yesterday, assigned) {
  yesterday = yesterday || {};
  assigned = assigned || {};

  var useCount = {};
  bank.forEach(function(e) { useCount[e] = 0; });
  Object.keys(assigned).forEach(function(k) {
    var e = assigned[k];
    useCount[e] = (useCount[e] || 0) + 1;
  });

  var result = {};
  var order = kioskShuffle_(keys.filter(function(k) {
    return !assigned[k];
  }));

  order.forEach(function(key) {
    var avoid = yesterday[key];
    var choices = kioskShuffle_(bank).filter(function(e) {
      return e !== avoid;
    });

    if (!choices.length) {
      choices = bank.slice();
    }

    var best = null;
    choices.forEach(function(e) {
      if (best === null || useCount[e] < useCount[best]) best = e;
    });

    // Only this student's own emoji from yesterday is still free: swap
    // with a student from this plan who can take that emoji instead.
    var free = bank.filter(function(e) { return !useCount[e]; });
    if (
      best !== null && useCount[best] > 0 &&
      free.length === 1 && free[0] === avoid
    ) {
      var swapKey = null;
      Object.keys(result).some(function(other) {
        if (yesterday[other] !== avoid && result[other] !== avoid) {
          swapKey = other;
          return true;
        }
        return false;
      });

      if (swapKey) {
        best = result[swapKey];
        useCount[best]--;
        result[swapKey] = avoid;
        useCount[avoid] = (useCount[avoid] || 0) + 1;
      }
    }

    if (best === null) best = "🐸";
    result[key] = best;
    useCount[best] = (useCount[best] || 0) + 1;
  });

  return result;
}

function kioskRosterKeys_() {
  var keys = [];
  var seen = {};

  (getStudentRoster() || []).forEach(function(group) {
    (group && group.students || []).forEach(function(student) {
      var key = attendanceStudentKey_(
        student.id,
        student.fullName ||
          ((student.firstName || "") + " " + (student.lastName || "")).trim()
      );
      if (!seen[key]) {
        seen[key] = true;
        keys.push(key);
      }
    });
  });

  return keys;
}

// Script Properties values are limited to about 9 KB, so the plan is
// stored in pieces.
function kioskReadEmojiPlan_() {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var count = Number(props.getProperty(KIOSK_EMOJI_PLAN_PROP_ + ":n") || 0);
  var text = "";

  for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    text += props.getProperty(KIOSK_EMOJI_PLAN_PROP_ + ":" + i) || "";
  }

  try {
    var plan = JSON.parse(text);
    if (plan && plan.emojis) return plan;
  } catch (err) {}

  return { date: "", emojis: {}, yesterday: {} };
}

function kioskWriteEmojiPlan_(plan) {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var text = JSON.stringify(plan);
  var size = 8000;
  var pieces = {};
  var count = Math.ceil(text.length / size) || 1;

  for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    pieces[KIOSK_EMOJI_PLAN_PROP_ + ":" + i] =
      text.slice(i * size, (i + 1) * size);
  }
  pieces[KIOSK_EMOJI_PLAN_PROP_ + ":n"] = String(count);

  props.setProperties(pieces);
  kioskEmojiPlanMemo_ = plan;
}

function kioskWithEmojiLock_(fn) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(20000);
  try {
    return fn();
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

// Today's plan, built for the whole roster on the first call of the day.
// `rebuild` makes a fresh plan for today (the reset button).
function kioskEmojiPlanForToday_(rebuild) {
  var today = kioskTodayKey_();

  if (!rebuild && kioskEmojiPlanMemo_ && kioskEmojiPlanMemo_.date === today) {
    return kioskEmojiPlanMemo_;
  }

  var plan = kioskReadEmojiPlan_();

  if (plan.date === today && !rebuild) {
    kioskEmojiPlanMemo_ = plan;
    return plan;
  }

  return kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var current = kioskReadEmojiPlan_();

    if (current.date === today && !rebuild) {
      kioskEmojiPlanMemo_ = current;
      return current;
    }

    var yesterday =
      current.date === today
        ? (current.yesterday || {})
        : (current.emojis || {});

    var fresh = {
      date: today,
      emojis: kioskAssignEmojis_(
        kioskRosterKeys_(),
        kioskEmojiBankList_(),
        yesterday,
        {}
      ),
      yesterday: yesterday
    };

    kioskWriteEmojiPlan_(fresh);
    return fresh;
  });
}

// A student added to the roster during the day gets an emoji nobody
// has yet.
function kioskEmojiForStudent_(studentKey) {
  var plan = kioskEmojiPlanForToday_(false);

  if (plan.emojis[studentKey]) {
    return plan.emojis[studentKey];
  }

  return kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var current = kioskReadEmojiPlan_();

    if (current.date !== plan.date) {
      current = plan;
    }

    if (!current.emojis[studentKey]) {
      var added = kioskAssignEmojis_(
        [studentKey],
        kioskEmojiBankList_(),
        current.yesterday || {},
        current.emojis
      );
      current.emojis[studentKey] = added[studentKey];
      kioskWriteEmojiPlan_(current);
    }

    return current.emojis[studentKey];
  });
}

// For the kiosk page: today's date and every student's emoji.
function getDailyEmojiPlan() {
  var plan = kioskEmojiPlanForToday_(false);
  return { date: plan.date, emojis: plan.emojis };
}

function getAttendanceFoundationStatus() {
  ensureAttendanceFoundation_();

  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  return {
    ready: true,
    attendanceSheet: !!ss.getSheetByName("Attendance"),
    eventsSheet: !!ss.getSheetByName("Attendance Events"),
    emojiBankSheet: !!ss.getSheetByName("Attendance Emoji Bank"),
    settings: getAttendanceSettings(),
    emojiCount: getAttendanceEmojiBank().length
  };
}

function appendAttendanceEvent_(eventData) {
  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var tz = ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() || Session.getScriptTimeZone();
  var now = new Date();

  eventData = eventData || {};

  var eventId =
    Utilities.getUuid();

  var eventDate =
    eventData.date instanceof Date
      ? eventData.date
      : now;

  var row = [
    eventId,
    eventDate,
    String(eventData.schedule || ""),
    String(eventData.period || ""),
    String(eventData.className || ""),
    String(eventData.studentName || ""),
    String(eventData.studentId || ""),
    String(eventData.event || ""),
    eventData.originalCheckIn instanceof Date
      ? eventData.originalCheckIn
      : "",
    eventData.confirmedAt instanceof Date
      ? eventData.confirmedAt
      : "",
    String(eventData.status || ""),
    String(eventData.emoji || ""),
    String(eventData.source || "Kiosk"),
    String(eventData.notes || ""),
    String(eventData.lateType || ""),
    eventData.minutesLate === 0 || eventData.minutesLate
      ? eventData.minutesLate
      : ""
  ];

  sheet.appendRow(row);

  return {
    eventId: eventId,
    timestamp: Utilities.formatDate(
      now,
      tz,
      "M/d/yyyy h:mm:ss a"
    )
  };
}


function parseSecondValue(value, fallback) {
  var text = String(value == null ? "" : value).trim().toLowerCase();

  if (!text) return Number(fallback || 0);

  var numeric = Number(
    text
      .replace(/seconds?/g, "")
      .replace(/secs?/g, "")
      .replace(/sec/g, "")
      .replace(/s$/g, "")
      .trim()
  );

  if (!isFinite(numeric)) {
    return Number(fallback || 0);
  }

  return numeric;
}

function parseMinuteValue(value, fallback) {
  if (typeof value === "number" && isFinite(value)) {
    return value;
  }

  var match = String(value || "").match(/[\d.]+/);

  if (!match) {
    return fallback;
  }

  var parsed = Number(match[0]);

  if (!isFinite(parsed) || parsed < 0) {
    return fallback;
  }

  return parsed;
}


function kioskSettingOn_(value, fallback) {
  var text =
    String(
      value == null ? "" : value
    )
      .trim()
      .toUpperCase();

  if (!text) return !!fallback;

  return (
    text === "ON" ||
    text === "TRUE" ||
    text === "YES" ||
    text === "1"
  );
}

function normalizeLiveBellScheduleUrl_(value) {
  var url =
    String(value || "").trim();

  if (!url) {
    return "https://bell.plus/907263";
  }

  if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(url)) {
    url = "https://" + url;
  }

  return /^https?:\/\/[^\s]+$/i.test(url)
    ? url
    : "https://bell.plus/907263";
}

function ensureDisplaySettingsRows__full_() {
  var sheet = ensureSettingsSheet();

  var rows = [
    ["Out of Classroom Panel", "ON", "Show or hide the Currently Out of the Classroom panel."],
    ["Bell Schedule Chart", "ON", "Show or hide the period/time chart. The DAY dropdown always stays visible."],
    ["Live Bell Schedule", "ON", "Show or hide the embedded live schedule."],
    ["Live Bell Schedule URL", "https://bell.plus/907263", "Paste a live schedule URL. bell.plus is a suggested option."],
    ["Birthday Celebration Video", "ON", "Play the birthday celebration video when an observed birthday student checks in."],
    ["Birthday Celebration Sound", "ON", "Play the birthday celebration sound when an observed birthday student checks in."],
    ["Birthday Custom Video File ID", "", "Blank uses the built-in/default birthday video."],
    ["Birthday Custom Sound File ID", "", "Blank uses the built-in/default birthday sound."],
    ["Birthday Custom Video File Name", "", "Display name for the currently uploaded custom birthday video."],
    ["Birthday Custom Sound File Name", "", "Display name for the currently uploaded custom birthday sound."],
    ["Birthday Celebration Message", "ON", "Show or hide the superimposed Happy Birthday message over the celebration."]
  ];

  var startRow = 19;
  var range = sheet.getRange(startRow, 1, rows.length, 3);
  var current = range.getValues();
  var changed = false;

  for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
    if (!current[i][0]) {
      current[i][0] = rows[i][0];
      changed = true;
    }

    if (current[i][1] === "") {
      current[i][1] = rows[i][1];
      changed = true;
    }

    if (!current[i][2]) {
      current[i][2] = rows[i][2];
      changed = true;
    }
  }

  if (changed) {
    range.setValues(current);
  }

  return sheet;
}

function readKioskSettings__read_() {
  var sheet = ensureSettingsSheet();

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 29) {
    ensureDisplaySettingsRows_();
  }

  var values = sheet
    .getRange("B2:B29")
    .getDisplayValues()
    .map(function(row) {
      return row[0];
    });

  function valueAt(rowNumber, fallback) {
    var value = values[rowNumber - 2];
    return value == null || value === "" ? fallback : value;
  }

  var greenThrough = parseMinuteValue(
    valueAt(2, "5 min"),
    5
  );

  var yellowThrough = parseMinuteValue(
    valueAt(3, "8 min"),
    8
  );

  var menuStyle = String(
    valueAt(4, "Student Cards")
  ).trim();

  var topTitle = String(
    valueAt(5, "")
  ).trim();

  var mainTitle = String(
    valueAt(6, "")
  ).trim();

  var backgroundName = String(
    valueAt(7, "School Supplies")
  ).trim();

  var customBackgroundUrl = String(
    valueAt(8, "")
  ).trim();

  var fontName = String(
    valueAt(9, "Arial")
  ).trim();

  var titleOrder = String(
    valueAt(10, "Small First")
  ).trim();

  if (titleOrder !== "Big First") {
    titleOrder = "Small First";
  }

  var classSelectionMode = String(
    valueAt(11, "Manual")
  ).trim();

  if (classSelectionMode !== "Bell Schedule") {
    classSelectionMode = "Manual";
  }

  var outOfClassroomEnabled = kioskSettingOn_(
    valueAt(19, "ON"),
    true
  );

  var displayedBellScheduleEnabled = kioskSettingOn_(
    valueAt(20, "ON"),
    true
  );

  var liveBellScheduleEnabled = kioskSettingOn_(
    valueAt(21, "ON"),
    true
  );

  var liveBellScheduleUrl = normalizeLiveBellScheduleUrl_(
    valueAt(22, "https://bell.plus/907263")
  );

  var birthdayVideoEnabled = kioskSettingOn_(
    valueAt(23, "ON"),
    true
  );

  var birthdaySoundEnabled = kioskSettingOn_(
    valueAt(24, "ON"),
    true
  );

  var birthdayCustomVideoFileId = String(
    valueAt(25, "")
  ).trim();

  var birthdayCustomSoundFileId = String(
    valueAt(26, "")
  ).trim();

  var birthdayCustomVideoFileName = String(
    valueAt(27, "")
  ).trim();

  var birthdayCustomSoundFileName = String(
    valueAt(28, "")
  ).trim();

  var birthdayMessageEnabled = kioskSettingOn_(
    valueAt(29, "ON"),
    true
  );

  var allowedStyles = [
    "Student Cards",
    "Grouped Dropdown",
    "Tiered Menu"
  ];

  if (allowedStyles.indexOf(menuStyle) === -1) {
    menuStyle = "Student Cards";
  }

  if (yellowThrough < greenThrough) {
    yellowThrough = greenThrough;
  }

  return {
    greenThroughMinutes: greenThrough,
    yellowThroughMinutes: yellowThrough,
    studentMenuStyle: menuStyle,
    topTitle: topTitle,
    mainTitle: mainTitle,
    backgroundName: backgroundName,
    customBackgroundUrl: customBackgroundUrl,
    fontName: fontName,
    titleOrder: titleOrder,
    classSelectionMode: classSelectionMode,
    outOfClassroomEnabled: outOfClassroomEnabled,
    displayedBellScheduleEnabled: displayedBellScheduleEnabled,
    liveBellScheduleEnabled: liveBellScheduleEnabled,
    liveBellScheduleUrl: liveBellScheduleUrl,
    birthdayVideoEnabled: birthdayVideoEnabled,
    birthdaySoundEnabled: birthdaySoundEnabled,
    birthdayCustomVideoFileId: birthdayCustomVideoFileId,
    birthdayCustomSoundFileId: birthdayCustomSoundFileId,
    birthdayCustomVideoFileName: birthdayCustomVideoFileName,
    birthdayCustomSoundFileName: birthdayCustomSoundFileName,
    birthdayMessageEnabled: birthdayMessageEnabled
  };
}

function saveKioskSettings__write_(settings) {
  var sheet = ensureDisplaySettingsRows_();

  var greenThrough = parseMinuteValue(
    settings && settings.greenThroughMinutes,
    5
  );

  var yellowThrough = parseMinuteValue(
    settings && settings.yellowThroughMinutes,
    8
  );

  var menuStyle = String(
    settings && settings.studentMenuStyle
      ? settings.studentMenuStyle
      : "Student Cards"
  ).trim();

  var topTitle = String(
    settings && settings.topTitle != null
      ? settings.topTitle
      : ""
  ).trim();

  var mainTitle = String(
    settings && settings.mainTitle != null
      ? settings.mainTitle
      : ""
  ).trim();

  var backgroundName = String(
    settings && settings.backgroundName
      ? settings.backgroundName
      : "School Supplies"
  ).trim();

  var customBackgroundUrl = String(
    settings && settings.customBackgroundUrl
      ? settings.customBackgroundUrl
      : ""
  ).trim();

  var fontName = String(
    settings && settings.fontName
      ? settings.fontName
      : "Arial"
  ).trim();

  var titleOrder = String(
    settings && settings.titleOrder
      ? settings.titleOrder
      : "Small First"
  ).trim();

  if (titleOrder !== "Big First") {
    titleOrder = "Small First";
  }

  var classSelectionMode = String(
    settings && settings.classSelectionMode
      ? settings.classSelectionMode
      : "Manual"
  ).trim();

  if (classSelectionMode !== "Bell Schedule") {
    classSelectionMode = "Manual";
  }

  var outOfClassroomEnabled =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings &&
      settings.outOfClassroomEnabled,
      true
    );

  var displayedBellScheduleEnabled =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings &&
      settings.displayedBellScheduleEnabled,
      true
    );

  var liveBellScheduleEnabled =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings &&
      settings.liveBellScheduleEnabled,
      true
    );

  var liveBellScheduleUrl =
    normalizeLiveBellScheduleUrl_(
      settings &&
      settings.liveBellScheduleUrl
    );

  var birthdayVideoEnabled =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings &&
      settings.birthdayVideoEnabled,
      true
    );

  var birthdaySoundEnabled =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings &&
      settings.birthdaySoundEnabled,
      true
    );

  var birthdayMessageEnabled =
    kioskSettingOn_(
      settings &&
      settings.birthdayMessageEnabled,
      true
    );

  if (yellowThrough < greenThrough) {
    throw new Error(
      "Yellow Through must be greater than or equal to Green Through."
    );
  }

  var allowedStyles = [
    "Student Cards",
    "Grouped Dropdown",
    "Tiered Menu"
  ];

  if (allowedStyles.indexOf(menuStyle) === -1) {
    menuStyle = "Student Cards";
  }

  // Read the settings block once so we can tell whether the pass timing
  // thresholds actually changed. Rebuilding conditional-formatting rules is
  // relatively expensive and should not happen when the teacher only changes
  // the background, font, title, etc.
  var oldValues = sheet.getRange("B2:B11").getDisplayValues();
  var oldGreen = parseMinuteValue(oldValues[0][0], 5);
  var oldYellow = parseMinuteValue(oldValues[1][0], 8);

  // One batch write is considerably faster than ten individual setValue calls.
  sheet.getRange("B2:B11").setValues([
    [greenThrough + " min"],
    [yellowThrough + " min"],
    [menuStyle],
    [topTitle],
    [mainTitle],
    [backgroundName],
    [customBackgroundUrl],
    [fontName],
    [titleOrder],
    [classSelectionMode]
  ]);

  sheet.getRange("B19:B22").setValues([
    [outOfClassroomEnabled ? "ON" : "OFF"],
    [displayedBellScheduleEnabled ? "ON" : "OFF"],
    [liveBellScheduleEnabled ? "ON" : "OFF"],
    [liveBellScheduleUrl]
  ]);

  sheet.getRange("B23:B24").setValues([
    [birthdayVideoEnabled ? "ON" : "OFF"],
    [birthdaySoundEnabled ? "ON" : "OFF"]
  ]);

  sheet
    .getRange("B29")
    .setValue(
      birthdayMessageEnabled
        ? "ON"
        : "OFF"
    );

  var saved = {
    greenThroughMinutes: greenThrough,
    yellowThroughMinutes: yellowThrough,
    studentMenuStyle: menuStyle,
    topTitle: topTitle,
    mainTitle: mainTitle,
    backgroundName: backgroundName,
    customBackgroundUrl: customBackgroundUrl,
    fontName: fontName,
    titleOrder: titleOrder,
    classSelectionMode: classSelectionMode,
    outOfClassroomEnabled: outOfClassroomEnabled,
    displayedBellScheduleEnabled: displayedBellScheduleEnabled,
    liveBellScheduleEnabled: liveBellScheduleEnabled,
    liveBellScheduleUrl: liveBellScheduleUrl,
    birthdayVideoEnabled: birthdayVideoEnabled,
    birthdaySoundEnabled: birthdaySoundEnabled,
    birthdayMessageEnabled: birthdayMessageEnabled,
    birthdayCustomVideoFileId:
      String(sheet.getRange("B25").getDisplayValue() || "").trim(),
    birthdayCustomSoundFileId:
      String(sheet.getRange("B26").getDisplayValue() || "").trim(),
    birthdayCustomVideoFileName:
      String(sheet.getRange("B27").getDisplayValue() || "").trim(),
    birthdayCustomSoundFileName:
      String(sheet.getRange("B28").getDisplayValue() || "").trim()
  };

  if (oldGreen !== greenThrough || oldYellow !== yellowThrough) {
    applyLogConditionalFormatting(saved);
  }

  return saved;
}



function formatAttendanceReportDateText_(date) {
  var ss =
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  var tz =
    ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() ||
    Session.getScriptTimeZone();

  var dayNumber =
    Number(
      Utilities.formatDate(
        date,
        tz,
        "u"
      )
    );

  var dayNames = [
    "",
    "Mon",
    "Tues",
    "Wed",
    "Thurs",
    "Fri",
    "Sat",
    "Sun"
  ];

  return (
    (dayNames[dayNumber] || "") +
    " " +
    Utilities.formatDate(
      date,
      tz,
      "M/d/yy"
    )
  ).trim();
}

function applyAttendanceDayBanding_() {
  var sheet =
    ensureAttendanceSummarySheet_();

  var lastRow =
    sheet.getLastRow();

  if (lastRow < 2) {
    return true;
  }

  var dateValues =
    sheet
      .getRange(
        2,
        1,
        lastRow - 1,
        1
      )
      .getValues();

  var lastColumn =
    Math.max(
      17,
      sheet.getLastColumn()
    );

  var white =
    "#ffffff";

  var lightBlue =
    "#eaf4ff";

  var currentKey =
    "";

  var useBlue =
    true;

  var groupStart =
    2;

  function flushGroup(
    startRow,
    endRow,
    blue
  ) {
    if (
      endRow <
      startRow
    ) {
      return;
    }

    sheet
      .getRange(
        startRow,
        1,
        endRow - startRow + 1,
        lastColumn
      )
      .setBackground(
        blue
          ? lightBlue
          : white
      );
  }

  for (
    var i = 0;
    i < dateValues.length;
    i += 1
  ) {
    var value =
      dateValues[i][0];

    var key = "";

    if (
      value instanceof Date &&
      !isNaN(
        value.getTime()
      )
    ) {
      key =
        Utilities.formatDate(
          value,
          SpreadsheetApp
            .getActiveSpreadsheet()
            .getSpreadsheetTimeZone() ||
            Session.getScriptTimeZone(),
          "yyyy-MM-dd"
        );
    } else {
      key =
        String(
          value || ""
        ).trim();
    }

    if (!currentKey) {
      currentKey = key;
      groupStart = i + 2;
      continue;
    }

    if (key !== currentKey) {
      flushGroup(
        groupStart,
        i + 1,
        useBlue
      );

      useBlue =
        !useBlue;

      currentKey =
        key;

      groupStart =
        i + 2;
    }
  }

  flushGroup(
    groupStart,
    lastRow,
    useBlue
  );

  /* Re-assert the header style after row banding. */
  sheet
    .getRange("A1:Q1")
    .setBackground(
      "#17365D"
    )
    .setFontColor(
      "#FFFFFF"
    )
    .setFontWeight(
      "bold"
    );

  return true;
}

function sendAttendanceReportTestEmail(
  recipient
) {
  var settings =
    getAttendanceSettings();

  recipient =
    String(
      recipient ||
      settings.reportEmail ||
      ""
    ).trim();

  if (!recipient) {
    throw new Error(
      "Enter an email address first."
    );
  }

  var sampleReport = {
    dateText: "Wed 9/30/26",
    rows: [
      {
        period: "1",
        className: "ASL 1",
        present: [
          "Alex",

      {

  var html =
    buildAttendanceReportEmailHtml_(
      sampleReport,

    '</div>' +
    html;

  MailApp.sendEmail({
    to: recipient,
    subject:
      "TEST — Classroom Pass System Attendance Report — " +
      attendanceReportEmailSendTime_(),

      "Classroom Pass System",
    htmlBody:
      html,
    body:
      "TEST REPORT — Sample data only.\n\n" +
      "This test email uses sample attendance information and does not change any student records."
  });

  return {
    ok: true,
    recipient: recipient
  };
}


function attendanceSummaryName_(student) {
  if (!student) {
    return "";
  }

  return String(
    student.displayName ||
    student.firstName ||
    student.name ||
    ""
  ).trim();
}

function attendanceStudentKeyServer_(student) {
  if (!student) {
    return "";
  }

  return String(
    student.id ||
    student.studentId ||
    (
      String(student.firstName || "") +
      "|" +
      String(student.lastName || "")
    )
  ).trim();
}

/* A student is Never Returned when:
   1. they checked in for class,
   2. they later left using a destination/pass,
   3. that pass remained open through the end of class,
   4. they never checked back in before the class ended.
   This is intentionally distinct from Absent. */
function classifyNeverReturnedForSummary_(
  student,
  attendanceState,
  activePass
) {
  if (
    !student ||
    !attendanceState ||
    !activePass
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  var attendanceStatus =
    String(
      attendanceState.status || ""
    ).trim();

  var checkedIn =
    attendanceStatus === "Present" ||
    attendanceStatus === "Tardy" ||
    attendanceStatus === "Pending Present" ||
    attendanceStatus === "Pending Tardy" ||
    !!attendanceState.arrivalAtMillis ||
    !!attendanceState.originalArrivalAtMillis;

  if (!checkedIn) {
    return false;
  }

  var passStillOpen =
    !activePass.returnedAt &&
    !activePass.returnedAtMillis &&
    !activePass.checkedInAt &&
    !activePass.checkedInAtMillis &&
    !activePass.endTime;

  return !!passStillOpen;
}


function normalizeAttendanceReportName_(value) {
  return String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
}

function attendanceReportPeriodKey_(value) {
  var text = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  var numeric = text.match(/\d+/);
  return numeric ? numeric[0] : text.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "");
}

function attendanceReportDateKey_(date, tz) {
  return Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, "yyyy-MM-dd");
}

function getTodayAttendanceReportSchedule_() {
  var status = getBellScheduleStatus("__AUTO__");

  if (status && status.schoolDay === false) {
    return {
      schoolDay: false,
      scheduleName: "",
      bellRows: []
    };
  }

  var scheduleName = String(
    status && status.scheduleName || ""
  ).trim();

  return {
    schoolDay: true,
    scheduleName: scheduleName,
    bellRows:
      scheduleName
        ? (getBellScheduleDisplay(scheduleName).rows || [])
        : []
  };
}

function attendanceReportClassWindow_(group, bellRows) {
  var groupKey = attendanceReportPeriodKey_(
    group && group.period
  );

  var matching = (bellRows || []).filter(function(row) {
    return attendanceReportPeriodKey_(
      row.rosterPeriod || row.period
    ) === groupKey;
  });

  if (!matching.length) {
    return {
      scheduled: false,
      lateBellMinutes: null,
      classEndMinutes: null
    };
  }

  var lateValues = matching
    .map(function(row) {
      return parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row.lateBell);
    })
    .filter(function(value) {
      return value != null;
    });

  var endValues = matching
    .map(function(row) {
      return parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row.classEnds);
    })
    .filter(function(value) {
      return value != null;
    });

  return {
    scheduled: true,
    lateBellMinutes:
      lateValues.length ? Math.min.apply(null, lateValues) : null,
    classEndMinutes:
      endValues.length ? Math.max.apply(null, endValues) : null
  };
}

function attendanceReportEventData_(reportDate) {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var tz = ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() || Session.getScriptTimeZone();
  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  var targetKey = attendanceReportDateKey_(reportDate, tz);
  var byClass = {};

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return byClass;

  var rowCount = sheet.getLastRow() - 1;
  kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, ATTENDANCE_LATE_MINUTES_COLUMN_);
  var values = sheet.getRange(2, 1, rowCount, 16).getValues();
  var display = sheet.getRange(2, 1, rowCount, 16).getDisplayValues();

  for (var i = 0; i < rowCount; i++) {
    var rawDate = values[i][1];
    var rowKey =
      rawDate instanceof Date
        ? attendanceReportDateKey_(rawDate, tz)
        : normalizeScheduleDateKey_(display[i][1]);

    var targetDisplayKey =
      normalizeScheduleDateKey_(
        Utilities.formatDate(reportDate, tz, "M/d/yyyy")
      );

    if (
      rowKey !== targetKey &&
      normalizeScheduleDateKey_(display[i][1]) !== targetDisplayKey
    ) {
      continue;
    }

    var period = String(display[i][3] || "").trim();
    var className = String(display[i][4] || "").trim();
    var classKey =
      attendanceReportPeriodKey_(period) +
      "||" +
      className.toLowerCase();

    if (!byClass[classKey]) {
      byClass[classKey] = {};
    }

    var studentKey = attendanceStudentKey_(
      display[i][6],
      display[i][5]
    );

    if (!byClass[classKey][studentKey]) {
      byClass[classKey][studentKey] = {
        latest: null,
        hadEarlyDeparture: false,
        lastAttendanceStatus: "",
        lastAttendanceEventId: "",
        lastAttendanceCheckIn: null
      };
    }

    var item = byClass[classKey][studentKey];
    var eventName = String(display[i][7] || "").trim();

    if (eventName === "Early Departure") {
      item.hadEarlyDeparture = true;
    }

    var rowStatus =
      String(
        display[i][10] || ""
      ).trim();

    if (
      rowStatus === "Present" ||
      rowStatus === "Tardy" ||
      rowStatus === "Pending Present" ||
      rowStatus === "Pending Tardy"
    ) {
      item.lastAttendanceStatus =
        rowStatus;

      item.lastAttendanceEventId =
        String(
          display[i][0] || ""
        );
      item.lastAttendanceLateType =
        String(display[i][14] || "").trim();
      item.lastAttendanceMinutesLate =
        String(display[i][15] || "").trim();

      item.lastAttendanceCheckIn =
        values[i][8] instanceof Date
          ? values[i][8]
          : item.lastAttendanceCheckIn;
    }

    item.latest = {
      eventId: String(display[i][0] || ""),
      studentName: String(display[i][5] || ""),
      studentId: String(display[i][6] || ""),
      event: eventName,
      originalCheckIn:
        values[i][8] instanceof Date ? values[i][8] : null,
      confirmedAt:
        values[i][9] instanceof Date ? values[i][9] : null,
      status: String(display[i][10] || "").trim(),
      lateType: String(display[i][14] || "").trim(),
      minutesLate: String(display[i][15] || "").trim()
    };
  }

  return byClass;
}

function attendanceReportPasses_(reportDate) {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var tz = ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() || Session.getScriptTimeZone();
  var sheet = ensureLogHeaders();
  var result = {};
  var targetKey = attendanceReportDateKey_(reportDate, tz);

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return result;

  var values = sheet
    .getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 5)
    .getValues();

  values.forEach(function(row) {
    var name = String(row[0] || "").trim();
    var timeOut = row[2] instanceof Date ? row[2] : null;
    var timeIn = row[3] instanceof Date ? row[3] : null;

    if (
      !name ||
      !timeOut ||
      attendanceReportDateKey_(timeOut, tz) !== targetKey
    ) {
      return;
    }

    var key = normalizeAttendanceReportName_(name);

    if (!result[key]) result[key] = [];

    result[key].push({
      destination: String(row[1] || "").trim(),
      timeOut: timeOut,
      timeIn: timeIn
    });
  });

  return result;
}

function attendanceReportMinutesForDate_(date, tz) {
  if (!(date instanceof Date)) return null;

  return (
    Number(Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, "H")) * 60 +
    Number(Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, "m")) +
    Number(Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, "s")) / 60
  );
}

function studentNeverReturnedAtClassEnd_(
  student,
  latestEvent,
  passes,
  lateBellMinutes,
  classEndMinutes,
  tz
) {
  if (!student || !latestEvent || classEndMinutes == null) {
    return false;
  }

  var status = String(latestEvent.status || "");

  var checkedIn =
    status === "Present" ||
    status === "Tardy" ||
    status === "Pending Present" ||
    status === "Pending Tardy" ||
    !!latestEvent.originalCheckIn;

  if (!checkedIn) return false;

  var candidateKeys = [
    normalizeAttendanceReportName_(student.fullName),
    normalizeAttendanceReportName_(student.displayName),
    normalizeAttendanceReportName_(student.firstName)
  ].filter(Boolean);

  var studentPasses = [];

  candidateKeys.some(function(key) {
    if (passes[key] && passes[key].length) {
      studentPasses = passes[key];
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  });

  if (!studentPasses.length) return false;

  var arrivalMinutes = attendanceReportMinutesForDate_(
    latestEvent.originalCheckIn,
    tz
  );

  return studentPasses.some(function(pass) {
    var outMinutes = attendanceReportMinutesForDate_(
      pass.timeOut,
      tz
    );

    var inMinutes = attendanceReportMinutesForDate_(
      pass.timeIn,
      tz
    );

    if (outMinutes == null || outMinutes > classEndMinutes) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      lateBellMinutes != null &&
      outMinutes < lateBellMinutes
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    if (
      arrivalMinutes != null &&
      outMinutes < arrivalMinutes
    ) {
      return false;
    }

    return inMinutes == null || inMinutes > classEndMinutes;
  });
}


function attendanceReportDestinationNames_() {
  return [
    "Bathroom",
    "Water",
    "Counselor",
    "Library",
    "Locker",
    "AP Office",
    "Nurse",
    "Chromebook Help Desk",
    "Bookstore",
    "Main Office",
    "Printer",
    "Cafeteria",
    "Lost & Found",
    "Back to my last class",
    "Other"
  ];
}

function attendanceReportPassesForStudent_(
  student,
  passes
) {
  var keys = [
    normalizeAttendanceReportName_(
      student && student.fullName
    ),
    normalizeAttendanceReportName_(
      student && student.displayName
    ),
    normalizeAttendanceReportName_(
      student && student.firstName
    )
  ].filter(Boolean);

  var found = [];

  keys.some(function(key) {
    if (
      passes[key] &&
      passes[key].length
    ) {
      found =
        passes[key];

      return true;
    }

    return false;
  });

  return found;
}

function attendanceReportPassesDuringClass_(
  student,
  passes,
  lateBellMinutes,
  classEndMinutes,
  tz
) {
  var studentPasses =
    attendanceReportPassesForStudent_(
      student,
      passes
    );

  return studentPasses.filter(
    function(pass) {
      var outMinutes =
        attendanceReportMinutesForDate_(
          pass.timeOut,
          tz
        );

      if (outMinutes == null) {
        return false;
      }

      if (
        lateBellMinutes != null &&
        outMinutes <
          lateBellMinutes
      ) {
        return false;
      }

      if (
        classEndMinutes != null &&
        outMinutes >
          classEndMinutes
      ) {
        return false;
      }

      return true;
    }
  );
}

function attendanceReportUniquePush_(
  list,
  value
) {
  value =
    String(
      value || ""
    ).trim();

  if (!value) {
    return;
  }

  if (
    list.indexOf(value) ===
      -1
  ) {
    list.push(value);
  }
}

function attendanceReportExistingRows_(sheet) {
  var map = {};

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return map;

  var values = sheet
    .getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 33)
    .getDisplayValues();

  values.forEach(function(row, index) {
    var key =
      String(row[0] || "").trim() +
      "||" +
      attendanceReportPeriodKey_(row[2]) +
      "||" +
      String(row[3] || "").trim().toLowerCase();

    map[key] = index + 2;
  });

  return map;
}

function attendanceReportConsecutiveAlerts_(
  sheet,
  group,
  absentNames,
  threshold
) {
  threshold = Math.max(1, Number(threshold || 3));

  if (!absentNames.length) return [];

  if (threshold <= 1) {
    return absentNames.map(function(name) {
      return name + " — 1 consecutive absence";
    });
  }

  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return [];

  var rows = sheet
    .getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 33)
    .getDisplayValues()
    .filter(function(row) {
      return (
        attendanceReportPeriodKey_(row[2]) ===
          attendanceReportPeriodKey_(group.period) &&
        String(row[3] || "").trim().toLowerCase() ===
          String(group.classTitle || "").trim().toLowerCase()
      );
    })
    .reverse();

  var alerts = [];

  absentNames.forEach(function(name) {
    var count = 1;
    var normalized = normalizeAttendanceReportName_(name);

    for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      var priorNames = String(rows[i][6] || "")
        .split(",")
        .map(normalizeAttendanceReportName_)
        .filter(Boolean);

      if (priorNames.indexOf(normalized) === -1) {
        break;
      }

      count += 1;

      if (count >= threshold) break;
    }

    if (count >= threshold) {
      alerts.push(
        name + " — " + count + " consecutive absences"
      );
    }
  });

  return alerts;
}

function generateAttendanceReportForDate_(
  reportDate,
  options
) {
  options =
    options || {};

  var settings =
    getAttendanceSettings();

  var ss =
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  var tz =
    ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() ||
    Session.getScriptTimeZone();

  var now =
    new Date();

  var reportDay =
    reportDate instanceof Date
      ? reportDate
      : now;

  var schedule =
    getTodayAttendanceReportSchedule_();

  if (
    schedule.schoolDay === false
  ) {
    return {
      success: true,
      schoolDay: false,
      rows: [],
      message:
        "No attendance report was generated because today is marked as a no-school day."
    };
  }

  var roster =
    getStudentRoster();

  var eventData =
    attendanceReportEventData_(
      reportDay
    );

  var passes =
    attendanceReportPasses_(
      reportDay
    );

  var summary =
    ensureAttendanceSummarySheet_();

  var existing =
    attendanceReportExistingRows_(
      summary
    );

  var dateText =
    formatAttendanceReportDateText_(
      reportDay
    );

  var destinationNames =
    attendanceReportDestinationNames_();

  var rowsForEmail = [];

  roster.forEach(function(group) {
    var windowInfo =
      attendanceReportClassWindow_(
        group,
        schedule.bellRows
      );

    if (
      schedule.bellRows.length &&
      !windowInfo.scheduled
    ) {
      return;
    }

    var classKey =
      attendanceReportPeriodKey_(
        group.period
      ) +
      "||" +
      String(
        group.classTitle || ""
      )
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase();

    var states =
      eventData[classKey] || {};

    var present = [];
    var tardy = [];
    var latePass = [];
    var absent = [];
    var neverReturned = [];
    var earlyDepartures = [];
    var leftEarly = [];
    var unresolved = [];

    var passUsage = {};

    destinationNames.forEach(
      function(destination) {
        passUsage[destination] = [];
      }
    );

    group.students.forEach(
      function(student) {
        var key =
          attendanceStudentKey_(
            student.id,
            student.fullName
          );

        var info =
          states[key];

        var latest =
          info &&
          info.latest
            ? info.latest
            : null;

        var name =
          String(
            student.displayName ||
            student.firstName ||
            student.fullName ||
            ""
          ).trim();

        /* Hall pass use is independent of attendance status. A student may
           appear in several destination columns and also be Tardy, Left Early,
           Never Returned, etc. */
        var classPasses =
          attendanceReportPassesDuringClass_(
            student,
            passes,
            windowInfo.lateBellMinutes,
            windowInfo.classEndMinutes,
            tz
          );

        classPasses.forEach(
          function(pass) {
            var rawDestination =
              String(
                pass.destination || ""
              ).trim();

            var canonical =
              destinationNames.filter(
                function(destination) {
                  return (
                    destination.toLowerCase() ===
                    rawDestination.toLowerCase()
                  );
                }
              )[0];

            if (!canonical) {
              canonical = "Other";
            }

            attendanceReportUniquePush_(
              passUsage[canonical],
              name
            );
          }
        );

        if (!latest) {
          absent.push(name);
          return;
        }

        var status =
          String(
            latest.status || ""
          ).trim();

        if (
          status !== "Present" &&
          status !== "Tardy" &&
          status !== "Pending Present" &&
          status !== "Pending Tardy"
        ) {
          status =
            String(
              info.lastAttendanceStatus || ""
            ).trim();
        }

        if (
          status.indexOf(
            "Pending "
          ) === 0
        ) {
          var pendingCheckIn =
            latest.originalCheckIn instanceof Date
              ? latest.originalCheckIn
              : info.lastAttendanceCheckIn;

          var pendingEventId =
            latest.eventId ||
            info.lastAttendanceEventId;

          var due =
            pendingCheckIn instanceof Date
              ? (
                  pendingCheckIn.getTime() +
                  Number(
                    settings.confirmationSeconds ||
                    120
                  ) *
                    1000
                )
              : 0;

          if (
            due &&
            now.getTime() >=
              due
          ) {
            var finalized =
              confirmAttendanceCheckIn(
                pendingEventId
              );

            if (
              finalized &&
              finalized.success
            ) {
              status =
                finalized.status;

              latest.status =
                status;
            }
          } else {
            unresolved.push(
              name
            );
          }
        }

        /* Primary attendance status. */
        if (
          status === "Tardy" ||
          status ===
            "Pending Tardy"
        ) {
          /* Late students are split into "Late with Pass" and "Tardy",
             each with the minutes after the scheduled class start. */
          var lateCheckIn =
            latest.originalCheckIn instanceof Date
              ? latest.originalCheckIn
              : info.lastAttendanceCheckIn;

          var lateMinutesValue =
            lateCheckIn instanceof Date &&
            windowInfo.lateBellMinutes != null
              ? attendanceMinutesLate_(
                  attendanceNowMinutes_(lateCheckIn, tz),
                  windowInfo.lateBellMinutes
                )
              : "";

          if (lateMinutesValue === "") {
            lateMinutesValue =
              String(
                latest.minutesLate ||
                info.lastAttendanceMinutesLate ||
                ""
              ).trim();
          }

          var lateLabel =
            lateMinutesValue !== ""
              ? name + " (" + lateMinutesValue + " min)"
              : name;

          var lateTypeValue =
            normalizeAttendanceLateType_(
              latest.lateType ||
              info.lastAttendanceLateType
            );

          attendanceReportUniquePush_(
            lateTypeValue === ATTENDANCE_LATE_PASS_
              ? latePass
              : tardy,
            lateLabel
          );
        } else if (
          status === "Present" ||
          status ===
            "Pending Present"
        ) {
          attendanceReportUniquePush_(
            present,
            name
          );
        } else {
          attendanceReportUniquePush_(
            absent,
            name
          );
        }

        /* Additional categories are intentionally NOT mutually exclusive. */
        if (
          info &&
          info.hadEarlyDeparture
        ) {
          attendanceReportUniquePush_(
            earlyDepartures,
            name
          );

          attendanceReportUniquePush_(
            leftEarly,
            name
          );
        }

        var attendanceEventForPassCheck = {
          status: status,
          originalCheckIn:
            latest.originalCheckIn instanceof Date
              ? latest.originalCheckIn
              : info.lastAttendanceCheckIn
        };

        if (
          studentNeverReturnedAtClassEnd_(
            student,
            attendanceEventForPassCheck,
            passes,
            windowInfo.lateBellMinutes,
            windowInfo.classEndMinutes,
            tz
          )
        ) {
          attendanceReportUniquePush_(
            neverReturned,
            name
          );
        }
      }
    );

    var alerts =
      attendanceReportConsecutiveAlerts_(
        summary,
        group,
        absent,
        settings.absenceAlertThreshold
      );

    var rowKey =
      dateText +
      "||" +
      attendanceReportPeriodKey_(
        group.period
      ) +
      "||" +
      String(
        group.classTitle || ""
      )
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase();

    var existingRow =
      existing[rowKey] || null;

    if (
      existingRow &&
      settings.reportReconcile ===
        false &&
      !options.forceUpdate
    ) {
      return;
    }

    var reportStatus =
      unresolved.length
        ? "Needs Review"
        : (
            existingRow
              ? "Updated"
              : "Final"
          );

    var finalizedAt =
      existingRow
        ? summary
            .getRange(
              existingRow,
              12
            )
            .getValue()
        : now;

    if (
      !(finalizedAt instanceof Date) ||
      isNaN(
        finalizedAt.getTime()
      )
    ) {
      finalizedAt =
        now;
    }

    var rowValues = [
      dateText,
      schedule.scheduleName || "",
      String(group.period || ""),
      String(group.classTitle || ""),
      settings.reportIncludePresent === false
        ? ""
        : present.join(", "),
      settings.reportIncludeTardy === false
        ? ""
        : tardy.join(", "),
      settings.reportIncludeAbsent === false
        ? ""
        : absent.join(", "),
      settings.reportIncludeNeverReturned === false
        ? ""
        : neverReturned.join(", "),
      settings.reportIncludeCounts === false
        ? ""
        : present.length,
      settings.reportIncludeCounts === false
        ? ""
        : tardy.length,
      settings.reportIncludeCounts === false
        ? ""
        : absent.length,
      finalizedAt,
      settings.reportIncludeAbsenceAlerts === false
        ? ""
        : alerts.join(", "),
      settings.reportIncludeCounts === false
        ? ""
        : group.students.length,
      settings.reportIncludeEarlyDepartures === false
        ? ""
        : earlyDepartures.join(", "),
      reportStatus,
      now,
      settings.reportIncludeEarlyDepartures === false
        ? ""
        : leftEarly.join(", ")
    ];

    destinationNames.forEach(
      function(destination) {
        rowValues.push(
          passUsage[destination].join(
            ", "
          )
        );
      }
    );

    /* Columns AH and AI: students who arrived late with a pass. */
    while (rowValues.length < 33) {
      rowValues.push("");
    }
    rowValues.push(
      settings.reportIncludeTardy === false
        ? ""
        : latePass.join(", ")
    );
    rowValues.push(
      settings.reportIncludeCounts === false
        ? ""
        : latePass.length
    );
    kioskEnsureColumns_(summary, 35);

    if (existingRow) {
      summary
        .getRange(
          existingRow,
          1,
          1,
          35
        )
        .setValues([
          rowValues
        ]);
    } else {
      summary
        .getRange(
          summary.getLastRow() + 1,
          1,
          1,
          35
        )
        .setValues([
          rowValues
        ]);
    }

    rowsForEmail.push({
      period:
        String(
          group.period || ""
        ),
      className:
        String(
          group.classTitle || ""
        ),
      present: present,
      tardy: tardy,
      latePass: latePass,
      absent: absent,
      neverReturned:
        neverReturned,
      earlyDepartures:
        earlyDepartures,
      leftEarly:
        leftEarly,
      passUsage:
        passUsage,
      alerts:
        alerts,
      status:
        reportStatus,
      unresolved:
        unresolved
    });
  });

  applyAttendanceDayBanding_();

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return {
    success: true,
    schoolDay: true,
    dateText:
      dateText,
    scheduleName:
      schedule.scheduleName || "",
    rows:
      rowsForEmail,
    generatedAt:
      now.getTime()
  };
}

function buildAttendanceReportEmailHtml_(
  report,
  settings
) {
  report =
    report || {
      rows: []
    };

  settings =
    settings ||
    getAttendanceSettings();

  var headerStyle =
    "padding:8px;border:1px solid #cbd8e6;background:#dbeeff;color:#20344d;font-weight:700;text-align:left;vertical-align:top;";

  var cellStyle =
    "padding:8px;border:1px solid #d9e1e8;color:#263b52;vertical-align:top;";

  var columns = [
    {
      key: "period",
      label: "Period",
      enabled: true
    },
    {
      key: "className",
      label: "Class",
      enabled: true
    },
    {
      key: "present",
      label: "Present Students",
      enabled:
        settings.reportIncludePresent !== false
    },
    {
      key: "tardy",
      label: "Tardy Students",
      enabled:
        settings.reportIncludeTardy !== false
    },
    {
      key: "latePass",
      label: "Late with a Pass",
      enabled:
        settings.reportIncludeTardy !== false
    },
    {
      key: "absent",
      label: "Absent Students",
      enabled:
        settings.reportIncludeAbsent !== false
    },
    {
      key: "neverReturned",
      label: "Never Returned",
      enabled:
        settings.reportIncludeNeverReturned !== false
    },
    {
      key: "leftEarly",
      label: "Left Early",
      enabled:
        settings.reportIncludeEarlyDepartures !== false
    },
    {
      key: "alerts",
      label: "Consecutive Absence Alerts",
      enabled:
        settings.reportIncludeAbsenceAlerts !== false
    }
  ].filter(
    function(column) {
      return column.enabled;
    }
  );

  function esc(value) {
    return String(
      value == null
        ? ""
        : value
    )
      .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
      .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
      .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
      .replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
  }

  var headings =
    columns
      .map(
        function(column) {
          return (
            '<th style="' +
            headerStyle +
            '">' +
            esc(
              column.label
            ) +
            "</th>"
          );
        }
      )
      .join("");

  var bodyRows =
    (report.rows || [])
      .map(
        function(row) {
          return (
            "<tr>" +
            columns
              .map(
                function(column) {
                  var value =
                    row[
                      column.key
                    ];

                  if (
                    Array.isArray(
                      value
                    )
                  ) {
                    value =
                      value.length
                        ? value.join(
                            ", "
                          )
                        : "—";
                  }

                  if (
                    value == null ||
                    String(
                      value
                    ).trim() ===
                      ""
                  ) {
                    value =
                      "—";
                  }

                  return (
                    '<td style="' +
                    cellStyle +
                    '">' +
                    esc(value) +
                    "</td>"
                  );
                }
              )
              .join("") +
            "</tr>"
          );
        }
      )
      .join("");

  if (!bodyRows) {
    bodyRows =
      '<tr><td colspan="' +
      columns.length +
      '" style="' +
      cellStyle +
      '">No class attendance rows were generated.</td></tr>';
  }

  /* Aggregate hall-pass use across all classes. A student appears once
     under each pass type they used that day. Pass types with no use are
     omitted completely from the email. */
  var passSummary = {};

  attendanceReportDestinationNames_()
    .forEach(
      function(destination) {
        passSummary[
          destination
        ] = [];
      }
    );

  (report.rows || [])
    .forEach(
      function(row) {
        var usage =
          row.passUsage || {};

        var periodText =
          String(
            row.period || ""
          ).trim();

        var periodKey =
          attendanceReportPeriodKey_(
            periodText
          );

        var periodNumber =
          Number(
            periodKey
          );

        if (
          !isFinite(
            periodNumber
          )
        ) {
          periodNumber =
            9999;
        }

        attendanceReportDestinationNames_()
          .forEach(
            function(destination) {
              var names =
                usage[
                  destination
                ] || [];

              names.forEach(
                function(name) {
                  name =
                    String(
                      name || ""
                    ).trim();

                  if (!name) {
                    return;
                  }

                  var exists =
                    passSummary[
                      destination
                    ].some(
                      function(item) {
                        return (
                          item.periodText ===
                            periodText &&
                          normalizeAttendanceReportName_(
                            item.name
                          ) ===
                            normalizeAttendanceReportName_(
                              name
                            )
                        );
                      }
                    );

                  if (!exists) {
                    passSummary[
                      destination
                    ].push({
                      periodText:
                        periodText,
                      periodNumber:
                        periodNumber,
                      name:
                        name
                    });
                  }
                }
              );
            }
          );
      }
    );

  attendanceReportDestinationNames_()
    .forEach(
      function(destination) {
        passSummary[
          destination
        ].sort(
          function(a, b) {
            if (
              a.periodNumber !==
              b.periodNumber
            ) {
              return (
                a.periodNumber -
                b.periodNumber
              );
            }

            return String(
              a.name || ""
            ).localeCompare(
              String(
                b.name || ""
              ),
              undefined,
              {
                sensitivity: "base"
              }
            );
          }
        );
      }
    );

  function passLabel_(
    destination
  ) {
    if (
      destination ===
      "Back to my last class"
    ) {
      return "Back to Last Class";
    }

    return destination;
  }

  var usedPasses =
    attendanceReportDestinationNames_()
      .filter(
        function(destination) {
          return (
            passSummary[
              destination
            ] &&
            passSummary[
              destination
            ].length
          );
        }
      );

  var passSummaryHtml =
    "";

  if (
    usedPasses.length
  ) {
    passSummaryHtml =
      '<div style="padding:10px 12px;background:#f3f8fc;border:1px solid #cbd8e6;border-radius:8px;">' +
        '<div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:6px;color:#20344d;">Hall Passes Used Today</div>' +
        usedPasses
          .map(
            function(destination) {
              return (
                '<div style="margin:4px 0;font-size:13px;color:#263b52;">' +
                  '<strong>' +
                    esc(
                      passLabel_(
                        destination
                      )
                    ) +
                  ':</strong> ' +
                  esc(
                    passSummary[
                      destination
                    ]
                      .map(
                        function(item) {
                          return (
                            item.name
                          );
                        }
                      )
                      .join(
                        ", "
                      )
                  ) +
                '</div>'
              );
            }
          )
          .join("") +
      '</div>';
  }

  return (
    '<div style="font-family:Arial,sans-serif;max-width:1100px;color:#24364b;">' +
      '<h2 style="margin:0 0 4px;">Classroom Pass System</h2>' +
      '<div style="margin-bottom:2px;font-size:15px;font-weight:700;">' +
        esc(
          report.dateText || ""
        ) +
      "</div>" +
      '<div style="margin-bottom:10px;font-size:11px;color:#6a7888;">Generated ' +
        esc(
          attendanceReportEmailSendTime_()
        ) +
      "</div>" +
      kioskTodoEmailHtml_() +
      passSummaryHtml +
      '<div style="margin-top:16px;"></div>' +
      '<table style="border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:12px;">' +
        "<thead><tr>" +
          headings +
        "</tr></thead>" +
        "<tbody>" +
          bodyRows +
        "</tbody>" +
      "</table>" +
    "</div>"
  );
}


function attendanceReportEmailSendTime_() {
  var ss =
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  var tz =
    ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() ||
    Session.getScriptTimeZone();

  return Utilities.formatDate(
    new Date(),
    tz,
    "h:mm a"
  );
}

function sendAttendanceReportEmail_(report, recipient) {
  var settings = getAttendanceSettings();

  recipient = String(
    recipient ||
    settings.reportEmail ||
    ""
  ).trim();

  if (!recipient) {
    throw new Error(
      "No daily attendance report email address is saved in Settings."
    );
  }

  MailApp.sendEmail({
    to: recipient,
    subject:
      "Classroom Pass System Attendance Report — " +
      String(report.dateText || "") +
      " — " +
