      attendanceReportEmailSendTime_(),
    name: "Classroom Pass System",
    htmlBody:
      buildAttendanceReportEmailHtml_(
        report,
        settings
      ),
    body:
      "Classroom Pass System attendance report for " +
      String(report.dateText || "") +
      ". Please view this message in HTML format for the report table."
  });

  return {
    success: true,
    recipient: recipient
  };
}

function generateTodayAttendanceReport() {
  return generateAttendanceReportForDate_(
    new Date(),
    { forceUpdate: true }
  );
}

function sendTodayAttendanceReportNow() {
  var settings =
    getAttendanceSettings();

  var recipient =
    String(
      settings.reportEmail || ""
    ).trim();

  if (!recipient) {
    throw new Error(
      "No Daily report recipient is saved in Settings."
    );
  }

  var report =
    generateAttendanceReportForDate_(
      new Date(),
      {
        forceUpdate: true
      }
    );

  if (
    report.schoolDay === false
  ) {
    return {
      success: true,
      skipped: true,
      message:
        report.message
    };
  }

  var sent =
    sendAttendanceReportEmail_(
      report,
      recipient
    );

  return {
    success: true,
    recipient:
      sent.recipient,
    rows:
      report.rows.length,
    dateText:
      report.dateText
  };
}

function attendanceReportTriggerHandler_() {
  var settings = getAttendanceSettings();

  if (settings.reportEnabled === false) {
    return;
  }

  var report =
    generateAttendanceReportForDate_(
      new Date(),
      { forceUpdate: false }
    );

  if (report.schoolDay === false) {
    return;
  }

  if (
    settings.reportEmailEnabled !== false &&
    String(settings.reportEmail || "").trim()
  ) {
    sendAttendanceReportEmail_(
      report,
      settings.reportEmail
    );
  }
}


function getAttendanceReportAutomationStatus() {
  var settings =
    getAttendanceSettings();

  var handler =
    "attendanceReportTriggerHandler_";

  var matching =
    ScriptApp
      .getProjectTriggers()
      .filter(function(trigger) {
        return (
          trigger.getHandlerFunction() ===
          handler
        );
      });

  return {
    enabled:
      settings.reportEnabled !== false,
    installed:
      matching.length > 0,
    triggerCount:
      matching.length,
    time:
      normalizeAttendanceReportTime_(
        settings.reportTime
      ),
    recipient:
      String(
        settings.reportEmail || ""
      ).trim(),
    emailEnabled:
      settings.reportEmailEnabled !== false,
    timezone:
      SpreadsheetApp
        .getActiveSpreadsheet()
        .getSpreadsheetTimeZone() ||
      Session.getScriptTimeZone()
  };
}


function uninstallAttendanceReportAutomation__write_() {
  var handler =
    "attendanceReportTriggerHandler_";

  var removed = 0;

  ScriptApp
    .getProjectTriggers()
    .forEach(function(trigger) {
      if (
        trigger.getHandlerFunction() ===
        handler
      ) {
        ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(
          trigger
        );

        removed += 1;
      }
    });

  var status =
    getAttendanceReportAutomationStatus();

  status.installed = false;
  status.triggerCount = 0;
  status.removed = removed;

  return status;
}

function installOrRefreshAttendanceReportAutomation__write_() {
  var result =
    syncAttendanceReportTrigger_();

  var status =
    getAttendanceReportAutomationStatus();

  status.syncResult =
    result;

  return status;
}

function syncAttendanceReportTrigger_() {
  var settings = getAttendanceSettings();
  var handler = "attendanceReportTriggerHandler_";

  var time =
    normalizeAttendanceReportTime_(
      settings.reportTime
    );

  // Changing timers is one of the slowest things a script can do, and every
  // Save used to delete and remake this one. Now it is only remade when the
  // report's on/off, time or time zone changed (or the timer is missing).
  var wanted = [
    settings.reportEnabled === false ? "off" : "on",
    time,
    SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSpreadsheetTimeZone() || Session.getScriptTimeZone()
  ].join("|");
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var existing = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().filter(function(trigger) {
    return trigger.getHandlerFunction() === handler;
  });

  if (
    props.getProperty("kioskReportTimer") === wanted &&
    existing.length === (settings.reportEnabled === false ? 0 : 1)
  ) {
    return { enabled: settings.reportEnabled !== false, time: time, unchanged: true };
  }

  existing.forEach(function(trigger) {
    ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(trigger);
  });

  if (settings.reportEnabled === false) {
    props.setProperty("kioskReportTimer", wanted);
    return { enabled: false };
  }

  var parts = time.split(":");
  var hour = Number(parts[0] || 15);
  var minute = Number(parts[1] || 15);

  var tz =
    SpreadsheetApp
      .getActiveSpreadsheet()
      .getSpreadsheetTimeZone() ||
    Session.getScriptTimeZone();

  ScriptApp
    .newTrigger(handler)
    .timeBased()
    .atHour(hour)
    .nearMinute(minute)
    .everyDays(1)
    .inTimezone(tz)
    .create();

  props.setProperty("kioskReportTimer", wanted);

  return {
    enabled: true,
    time: time,
    timezone: tz
  };
}

function getKioskSettings() {
  return readKioskSettings();
}

function applyLogConditionalFormatting(settings) {
  var sheet = ensureLogHeaders();

  if (!settings) {
    settings = readKioskSettings();
  }

  var greenMinutes = settings.greenThroughMinutes;
  var yellowMinutes = settings.yellowThroughMinutes;

  var targetRange = sheet.getRange(
    2,
    5,
    Math.max(sheet.getMaxRows() - 1, 1),
    1
  );

  var existingRules = sheet.getConditionalFormatRules();

  var preservedRules = existingRules.filter(function(rule) {
    var ranges = rule.getRanges();

    return !ranges.some(function(range) {
      var firstColumn = range.getColumn();
      var lastColumn = firstColumn + range.getNumColumns() - 1;
      return firstColumn <= 5 && lastColumn >= 5;
    });
  });

  var greenFormula =
    '=AND($C2<>"",$D2<>"",($D2-$C2)<=' +
    greenMinutes +
    '/1440)';

  var yellowFormula =
    '=AND($C2<>"",$D2<>"",($D2-$C2)>' +
    greenMinutes +
    '/1440,($D2-$C2)<=' +
    yellowMinutes +
    '/1440)';

  var redFormula =
    '=AND($C2<>"",$D2<>"",($D2-$C2)>' +
    yellowMinutes +
    '/1440)';

  var greenRule = SpreadsheetApp.newConditionalFormatRule()
    .whenFormulaSatisfied(greenFormula)
    .setBackground("#d9ead3")
    .setFontColor("#274e13")
    .setRanges([targetRange])
    .build();

  var yellowRule = SpreadsheetApp.newConditionalFormatRule()
    .whenFormulaSatisfied(yellowFormula)
    .setBackground("#fff2cc")
    .setFontColor("#7f6000")
    .setRanges([targetRange])
    .build();

  var redRule = SpreadsheetApp.newConditionalFormatRule()
    .whenFormulaSatisfied(redFormula)
    .setBackground("#f4cccc")
    .setFontColor("#990000")
    .setBold(true)
    .setRanges([targetRange])
    .build();

  sheet.setConditionalFormatRules(
    preservedRules.concat([greenRule, yellowRule, redRule])
  );
}

function onEdit(e) {
  // Any edit typed into the spreadsheet resets the saved answers, so the
  // kiosk always reads the new values on its next request.
  invalidateKioskCache_();

  if (!e || !e.range) return;

  var sheet = e.range.getSheet();

  // A new background picture pasted in column D: forget the old Drive link
  // so the kiosk makes a fresh copy of the new picture.
  if (
    sheet.getName() === "Backgrounds" &&
    e.range.getColumn() <= 4 &&
    e.range.getLastColumn() >= 4 &&
    e.range.getLastRow() >= 2 &&
    sheet.getMaxColumns() >= BACKGROUND_FILE_COLUMN_
  ) {
    var firstRow = Math.max(2, e.range.getRow());
    sheet
      .getRange(firstRow, BACKGROUND_LINK_COLUMN_, e.range.getLastRow() - firstRow + 1, 2)
      .clearContent();
  }

  if (
    sheet.getName() === "Settings" &&
    e.range.getColumn() === 2 &&
    e.range.getRow() >= 2 &&
    e.range.getRow() <= 9
  ) {
    applyLogConditionalFormatting(readKioskSettings());
  }

  var destCols = sheet.getName() === "Destinations" ? kioskHeadingMap_(sheet, KIOSK_DEST_COLS_).cols.map(function(c) { return c + 1; }) : null;
  var editedDestCol = destCols ? destCols.indexOf(e.range.getColumn()) : -1;      // 0 Destination, 1 Collection, 2 Category
  if (
    destCols &&
    e.range.getRow() >= 2 &&
    editedDestCol >= 0 &&
    editedDestCol <= 2
  ) {
    var row = e.range.getRow();

    if (editedDestCol === 0) {
      var destination = String(sheet.getRange(row, destCols[0]).getDisplayValue() || "").trim();
      if (destination && !sheet.getRange(row, destCols[1]).getDisplayValue()) {
        sheet.getRange(row, destCols[1]).setValue("Doodle");
      }
    } else if (editedDestCol === 1) {
      sheet.getRange(row, destCols[2]).clearContent();
      sheet.getRange(row, destCols[3]).clearContent();
    } else if (editedDestCol === 2) {
      sheet.getRange(row, destCols[3]).clearContent();
    }

    applyDestinationDependentValidationForRow_(
      sheet,
      row,
      getDestinationIconOptions_()
    );
  }
}

function getActivePasses() {
  var sheet = ensureLogHeaders();
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  var active = [];

  if (lastRow < 2) {
    return active;
  }

  // Every row above the first pass still out is finished, so start there.
  var firstRow = kioskOpenPassStartRow_(lastRow);

  if (firstRow > lastRow) {
    kioskSaveOpenPassStartRow_(lastRow + 1);
    return active;
  }

  var data = sheet
    .getRange(firstRow, 1, lastRow - firstRow + 1, Math.max(4, Math.min(6, sheet.getLastColumn())))
    .getValues();

  // Passes still open after their class ended close as "Never returned"
  // (checked at most once a minute).
  try {
    var cache = kioskCache_();
    if (!cache.get("kiosk:closeCheck")) {
      cache.put("kiosk:closeCheck", "1", 60);
      kioskCloseForgottenPasses_(sheet, firstRow, data);
    }
  } catch (closeErr) {}

  var firstOpenRow = lastRow + 1;

  for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (data[i][0] && !data[i][3]) {
      if (firstOpenRow > lastRow) firstOpenRow = i + firstRow;

      var storedName = String(data[i][0] || "");
      var timeOut = new Date(data[i][2]);

      active.push({
        row: i + firstRow,
        name: storedName,
        displayName: extractFirstName_(storedName),
        destination: data[i][1],
        timeOut: timeOut.toLocaleTimeString([], {
          hour: '2-digit',
          minute: '2-digit'
        }),
        timeOutMs: timeOut.getTime(),
        // A pass delivered to class (Counselor, AP...) doesn't use one of the
        // room's hall passes.
        delivered: String(data[i][5] || "").trim() === KIOSK_CALLED_BY_PASS_
      });
    }
  }

  kioskSaveOpenPassStartRow_(firstOpenRow);

  return active;
}

function extractFirstName_(fullName) {
  fullName = String(fullName || "").trim();
  if (!fullName) return "";

  var parts = fullName.split(/\s+/);
  return parts.length ? parts[0] : fullName;
}

/* ===== Passes: delivered passes, forgotten passes, weekly minutes ===== */
var KIOSK_CALLED_BY_PASS_ = "Called by pass";
var KIOSK_NEVER_RETURNED_ = "Never returned";
var KIOSK_TEACHER_APPROVED_ = "Teacher approved";
var KIOSK_WEEKLY_REMINDER_MINUTES_ = 6;   // minutes out per week before the reminder screen

function kioskEnsureLogNoteHeader_(sheet) {
  try {
    var head = sheet.getRange(1, 6, 1, 3);
    var current = head.getDisplayValues()[0];
    if (current[0] !== "Note" || current[1] !== "Class" || current[2] !== "Student ID") {
      head.setValues([["Note", "Class", "Student ID"]]);
    }
  } catch (err) {}
}

// The class blocks (start, late bell, end in minutes after midnight) of the
// bell schedule used on `date`, following the Schedule Calendar.
function kioskClassBlocksForDate_(date) {
  var tz = kioskTimeZone_();
  var dateKey = Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, "M/d/yyyy");
  var weekday = Utilities.formatDate(date, tz, "EEEE");
  var scheduleName = "";
  var specific = null, weekly = null;

  (kioskScheduleCalendarRows_() || []).forEach(function(row) {
    var type = String(row[0] || "").trim();
    var value = String(row[1] || "").trim();
    if (type === "Specific Date" && normalizeScheduleDateKey_(value) === normalizeScheduleDateKey_(dateKey)) specific = row;
    if (type === "Weekday" && value.toLowerCase() === weekday.toLowerCase()) weekly = row;
  });

  var chosen = specific || weekly;
  if (chosen) {
    if (String(chosen[3] || "Yes").trim().toLowerCase() === "no") return [];
    scheduleName = String(chosen[2] || "").trim();
  }
  if (!scheduleName) return [];

  var blocks = [];
  (kioskBellScheduleRows_() || []).forEach(function(row) {
    if (String(row[0] || "").trim().toUpperCase() !== "ON") return;
    if (String(row[1] || "").trim() !== scheduleName) return;
    var opens = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row[4]);
    var late = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row[5]);
    var ends = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(row[6]);
    if (opens == null || late == null || ends == null) return;
    blocks.push({ period: String(row[2] || "").trim(), openMinutes: opens, lateMinutes: late, endMinutes: ends,
      blockType: String(row[7] || "Class").trim().toLowerCase() });
  });

  blocks.sort(function(a, b) { return a.openMinutes - b.openMinutes; });
  return blocks;
}

function kioskAtMinutes_(date, minutes) {
  var d = new Date(date.getTime());
  d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  return new Date(d.getTime() + Math.round(minutes * 60000));
}

// When a pass that started at `timeOut` closes on its own: the end of that
// class period (a pass taken between classes belongs to the next one). With
// no schedule that day: 4:00 PM, or an hour after it started if later.
function kioskForgottenPassCloseTime_(timeOut) {
  var startMinutes = timeOut.getHours() * 60 + timeOut.getMinutes();
  var blocks = kioskClassBlocksForDate_(timeOut).filter(function(b) { return b.blockType !== "lunch"; });

  for (var i = 0; i < blocks.length; i++) {
    if (startMinutes < blocks[i].endMinutes) return kioskAtMinutes_(timeOut, blocks[i].endMinutes);
  }

  var fallback = kioskAtMinutes_(timeOut, 16 * 60);
  var hourLater = new Date(timeOut.getTime() + 3600000);
  return hourLater > fallback ? hourLater : fallback;
}

// Closes passes whose class has ended (getActivePasses runs it once a minute),
// marking them "Never returned". Returns true when anything changed.
function kioskCloseForgottenPasses_(sheet, firstRow, data) {
  var now = new Date();
  var changed = false;

  for (var i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (!data[i][0] || data[i][3]) continue;
    var timeOut = data[i][2] instanceof Date ? data[i][2] : new Date(data[i][2]);
    if (isNaN(timeOut.getTime())) continue;

    var closeAt;
    try { closeAt = kioskForgottenPassCloseTime_(timeOut); } catch (err) { continue; }
    if (now < closeAt) continue;

    sheet.getRange(firstRow + i, 4, 1, 2).setValues([[closeAt, KIOSK_NEVER_RETURNED_]]);
    sheet.getRange(firstRow + i, 4).setNumberFormat("M/d/yyyy h:mm:ss");
    data[i][3] = closeAt;
    changed = true;
  }

  return changed;
}

/* ===== Out-of-class reminder settings (Settings > Attendance) =====
   minutes:      minutes out per week before the reminder (more than this)
   trips:        trips per week before the reminder (this many or more)
   graceMinutes: passes started during passing time or in the first this-many
                 minutes after the late bell never count and never show it
   offClasses:   classes ("period|class") the reminder doesn't apply to; their
                 trips don't count either (e.g. a lunch study hall) */
var KIOSK_REMINDER_DEFAULTS_ = { minutes: 6, trips: 3, graceMinutes: 5, offClasses: [], patternEmail: true, studentEmail: false, emailOffClasses: [], passCount: 2, fridayEmail: true,
  weekTitle: "", weekText: "", redTitle: "", redText: "" };   // the teacher's own reminder wording ("" = the standard wording)

function getReminderSettings() {
  var saved = kioskReadJsonProp_("kioskReminderSettings") || {};
  var out = {};
  Object.keys(KIOSK_REMINDER_DEFAULTS_).forEach(function(k) {
    out[k] = saved[k] == null ? KIOSK_REMINDER_DEFAULTS_[k] : saved[k];
  });
  return out;
}

function saveReminderSettings(settings) {
  settings = settings || {};
  function whole(v, low, high, fallback) {
    var n = Math.round(Number(v));
    return isFinite(n) ? Math.max(low, Math.min(high, n)) : fallback;
  }
  var clean = {
    minutes: whole(settings.minutes, 1, 120, 6),
    trips: whole(settings.trips, 1, 50, 3),
    graceMinutes: whole(settings.graceMinutes, 0, 30, 5),
    offClasses: (settings.offClasses || []).map(String).filter(Boolean),
    patternEmail: settings.patternEmail !== false,
    studentEmail: settings.studentEmail === true,
    emailOffClasses: (settings.emailOffClasses || []).map(String).filter(Boolean),
    passCount: whole(settings.passCount, 0, 20, 2),   // 0 = no limit
    fridayEmail: settings.fridayEmail !== false,
    weekTitle: String(settings.weekTitle || "").trim().slice(0, 120),
    weekText: String(settings.weekText || "").trim().slice(0, 800),
    redTitle: String(settings.redTitle || "").trim().slice(0, 120),
    redText: String(settings.redText || "").trim().slice(0, 800)
  };
  kioskWriteJsonProp_("kioskReminderSettings", clean);
  try { kioskCache_().remove("kiosk:weekStats:" + kioskTodayKey_()); } catch (cacheErr) {}
  return clean;
}

// Does this Logs row count toward a student's weekly minutes and trips?
// Not: delivered passes, passes from classes the reminder is off for, and
// passes started in passing time or the start-of-class grace minutes.
function kioskPassCounts_(row, reminder, blocksByDay, tz) {
  if (String(row[5] || "").trim() === KIOSK_CALLED_BY_PASS_) return false;
  if (row[6] && reminder.offClasses.indexOf(String(row[6])) !== -1) return false;

  var timeOut = row[2];
  var day = kioskDayKeyOf_(timeOut, tz);
  if (!blocksByDay[day]) {
    try { blocksByDay[day] = kioskClassBlocksForDate_(timeOut).filter(function(b) { return b.blockType !== "lunch"; }); }
    catch (err) { blocksByDay[day] = []; }
  }
  var m = timeOut.getHours() * 60 + timeOut.getMinutes() + timeOut.getSeconds() / 60;
  var blocks = blocksByDay[day];
  for (var i = 0; i < blocks.length; i++) {
    if (m < blocks[i].endMinutes) {
      return m >= blocks[i].lateMinutes + reminder.graceMinutes;
    }
  }
  return true;   // no schedule that day: it counts
}

// Monday of this week (yyyy-MM-dd); shiftDays moves it (-7 = last week).
function kioskWeekStartKey_(shiftDays) {
  var d = new Date();
  var back = (d.getDay() + 6) % 7;   // Monday = 0
  d.setDate(d.getDate() - back + (shiftDays || 0));
  return Utilities.formatDate(d, kioskTimeZone_(), "yyyy-MM-dd");
}

// Minutes each student has been out of class this week (Monday on), from
// the Logs tab. Passes delivered to class ("Called by pass") don't count.
// { weekStart, limit, minutes: { "first last": n } }
// The teacher view (every 12 s) and the kiosk (every 60 s) both ask for this
// week's counts, so a copy is kept for 60 seconds.
function getWeekPassMinutes(extraOffClasses) {
  if (extraOffClasses && extraOffClasses.length) return kioskWeekPassMinutes_(extraOffClasses);
  var cache = kioskCache_();
  var key = "kiosk:weekStats:" + kioskTodayKey_();
  try {
    var hit = cache.get(key);
    if (hit) return JSON.parse(hit);
  } catch (err) {}
  var fresh = kioskWeekPassMinutes_();
  try {
    var text = JSON.stringify(fresh);
    if (text.length < 90000) cache.put(key, text, 60);
  } catch (err2) {}
  return fresh;
}

function kioskWeekPassMinutes_(extraOffClasses) {
  var sheet = ensureLogHeaders();
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  var weekStart = kioskWeekStartKey_();
  var lastWeekStart = kioskWeekStartKey_(-7);
  var reminder = getReminderSettings();
  if (extraOffClasses && extraOffClasses.length) {
    reminder.offClasses = reminder.offClasses.concat(extraOffClasses);
  }
  var out = {
    weekStart: weekStart,
    limit: reminder.minutes,
    tripLimit: reminder.trips,
    graceMinutes: reminder.graceMinutes,
    offClasses: reminder.offClasses,
    // "ID:123" or "NAME:first last" -> { minutes, trips, lastMinutes, lastTrips,
    //   lastWeekOver, twoWeekTrips } (counted trips only)
    students: {},
    minutes: {}       // "first last" -> minutes this week (older pages)
  };
  if (lastRow < 2) return out;

  var firstRow = kioskStartRowForDay_(sheet, 3, lastWeekStart, "lastweek");
  if (firstRow > lastRow) return out;

  var cols = Math.min(8, Math.max(5, sheet.getLastColumn()));
  var data = sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1, lastRow - firstRow + 1, cols).getValues();
  var tz = kioskTimeZone_();
  var now = new Date();
  var blocksByDay = {};

  data.forEach(function(row) {
    var name = String(row[0] || "").trim();
    var timeOut = row[2];
    if (!name || !(timeOut instanceof Date)) return;
    var day = kioskDayKeyOf_(timeOut, tz);
    if (day < lastWeekStart) return;
    if (!kioskPassCounts_(row, reminder, blocksByDay, tz)) return;

    var timeIn = row[3] instanceof Date ? row[3] : now;
    var minutes = Math.max(0, (timeIn.getTime() - timeOut.getTime()) / 60000);
    var key = attendanceStudentKey_(row[7], name);
    var st = out.students[key] = out.students[key] ||
      { name: name, minutes: 0, trips: 0, lastMinutes: 0, lastTrips: 0, lastWeekOver: false, twoWeekTrips: 0 };

    if (day >= weekStart) {
      st.minutes = Math.round((st.minutes + minutes) * 10) / 10;
      st.trips++;
      var lower = name.toLowerCase();
      out.minutes[lower] = Math.round(((out.minutes[lower] || 0) + minutes) * 10) / 10;
    } else {
      st.lastMinutes = Math.round((st.lastMinutes + minutes) * 10) / 10;
      st.lastTrips++;
    }
    st.twoWeekTrips = st.trips + st.lastTrips;
    st.lastWeekOver = st.lastMinutes > reminder.minutes || st.lastTrips >= reminder.trips;
  });

  return out;
}

/* ===== Patterns worth a conversation =====
   Leaving: over the weekly reminder limit (minutes or trips) this week AND
   last week (the kiosk also shows the student the red warning).
   Tardies: 3 or more real tardies in the last 14 days (not commuters, not
   "I have a pass").
   Once a day (afternoon timer) the teacher gets one email listing what is
   new; each student is mentioned once per week for leaving and once per 14
   days for tardies. Settings > Attendance > Out-of-class reminders turns the
   email off. Run checkPatternsNow in the editor to see the list right away. */
var KIOSK_TARDY_PATTERN_COUNT_ = 3;
var KIOSK_TARDY_PATTERN_DAYS_ = 14;

function kioskFindPatterns_() {
  var tz = kioskTimeZone_();
  // Classes unchecked under "Classes included in emails" are left out.
  var emailOff = getReminderSettings().emailOffClasses;
  var week = getWeekPassMinutes(emailOff);
  var leaving = [];

  Object.keys(week.students).forEach(function(key) {
    var st = week.students[key];
    var overNow = st.minutes > week.limit || st.trips >= week.tripLimit;
    if (overNow && st.lastWeekOver) {
      leaving.push({ key: key, name: st.name, trips: st.trips, minutes: Math.round(st.minutes),
        lastTrips: st.lastTrips, lastMinutes: Math.round(st.lastMinutes) });
    }
  });

  var tardies = [];
  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  var since = new Date();
  since.setDate(since.getDate() - (KIOSK_TARDY_PATTERN_DAYS_ - 1));
  var sinceKey = Utilities.formatDate(since, tz, "yyyy-MM-dd");

  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    var firstRow = kioskStartRowForDay_(sheet, 2, sinceKey, "tardy");
    if (firstRow <= lastRow) {
      var cols = Math.min(16, sheet.getLastColumn());
      var rows = sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1, lastRow - firstRow + 1, cols).getValues();
      var byStudent = {};

      rows.forEach(function(r) {
        var day = kioskDayKeyOf_(r[1], tz);
        if (!day || day < sinceKey) return;
        if (String(r[10] || "").indexOf("Tardy") === -1) return;
        if (normalizeAttendanceLateType_(r[14]) !== ATTENDANCE_LATE_TARDY_) return;
        if (emailOff.indexOf(String(r[3] || "").trim() + "|" + String(r[4] || "").trim()) !== -1) return;
        var key = attendanceStudentKey_(r[6], r[5]);
        var t = byStudent[key] = byStudent[key] || { key: key, name: String(r[5] || key), days: [] };
        t.days.push(Utilities.formatDate(r[1], tz, "EEE M/d") +
          (r[4] ? " " + r[4] : "") + (r[15] !== "" && r[15] != null ? " (" + r[15] + " min)" : ""));
      });

      Object.keys(byStudent).forEach(function(k) {
        if (byStudent[k].days.length >= KIOSK_TARDY_PATTERN_COUNT_) tardies.push(byStudent[k]);
      });
    }
  }

  return { weekStart: week.weekStart, leaving: leaving, tardies: tardies,
    limits: { minutes: week.limit, trips: week.tripLimit } };
}

// For the editor: what would the next email say? (Sends nothing.)
function checkPatternsNow() {
  var p = kioskFindPatterns_();
  var report = kioskPatternEmailText_(p.leaving, p.tardies, p.limits) || "No patterns right now.";
  Logger.log(report);
  return report;
}

function kioskPatternEmailText_(leaving, tardies, limits) {
  var lines = [];
  if (leaving.length) {
    lines.push("LEAVING THE ROOM (over the limit two weeks in a row: more than " + limits.minutes +
      " minutes or " + limits.trips + "+ trips a week)");
    leaving.forEach(function(s) {
      var trips = function(n) { return n + (n === 1 ? " trip" : " trips"); };
      lines.push("• " + s.name + ": this week " + trips(s.trips) + ", " + s.minutes + " min; last week " +
        trips(s.lastTrips) + ", " + s.lastMinutes + " min.");
    });
    lines.push("");
  }
  if (tardies.length) {
    lines.push("TARDIES (" + KIOSK_TARDY_PATTERN_COUNT_ + "+ in the last " + KIOSK_TARDY_PATTERN_DAYS_ + " days)");
    tardies.forEach(function(t) {
      lines.push("• " + t.name + ": " + t.days.length + " tardies — " + t.days.join("; ") + ".");
    });
    lines.push("");
  }
  if (!lines.length) return "";
  return "These students may be ready for a conversation:\n\n" + lines.join("\n") +
    "Details: the Weekly Pass Summary tab and the Logs / Attendance Events tabs of the kiosk Sheet.\n" +
    "(Hall Pass Kiosk. Turn these emails off in Settings > Attendance > Out-of-class reminders.)";
}

// Daily (afternoon timer): email the teacher about patterns not yet mentioned.
function kioskPatternEmailHandler_() {
  if (!getReminderSettings().patternEmail) return "Pattern email is off.";

  var p = kioskFindPatterns_();
  var sent = kioskReadJsonProp_("kioskPatternsSent") || {};
  var today = kioskTodayKey_();
  var fresh = function(id, days) {
    var last = sent[id];
    if (!last) return true;
    return (new Date(today) - new Date(last)) / 86400000 >= days;
  };

  var leaving = p.leaving.filter(function(s) { return fresh("leave|" + s.key + "|" + p.weekStart, 9999); });
  var tardies = p.tardies.filter(function(t) { return fresh("tardy|" + t.key, KIOSK_TARDY_PATTERN_DAYS_); });
  if (!leaving.length && !tardies.length) return "Nothing new.";

  var to = String(getAttendanceSettings().reportEmail || "").trim() ||
    Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail();
  var count = leaving.length + tardies.length;
  MailApp.sendEmail(to, "Hall Pass Kiosk: " + count + (count === 1 ? " student" : " students") +
    " may need a conversation", kioskPatternEmailText_(leaving, tardies, p.limits));

  leaving.forEach(function(s) { sent["leave|" + s.key + "|" + p.weekStart] = today; });
  tardies.forEach(function(t) { sent["tardy|" + t.key] = today; });
  Object.keys(sent).forEach(function(k) {          // forget entries older than 60 days
    if ((new Date(today) - new Date(sent[k])) / 86400000 > 60) delete sent[k];
  });
  kioskWriteJsonProp_("kioskPatternsSent", sent);
  return "Emailed " + to + " about " + count + ".";
}

/* ===== Email to the student after the red warning =====
   Off until the teacher turns it on (Settings > Attendance > Out-of-class
   reminders); meant for a school Google account. School addresses are the
   first 3 letters of the last name + the student ID, @d204.lths.net
   (e.g. smi123456@d204.lths.net). One email per student per week, with a
   copy to the teacher; replies go to the teacher. */
var KIOSK_STUDENT_EMAIL_DOMAIN_ = "d204.lths.net";

function kioskStudentEmailAddress_(lastName, studentId) {
  var letters = String(lastName || "").toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z]/g, "").slice(0, 3);
  var id = String(studentId || "").replace(/\D/g, "");
  if (!letters || !id) return "";
  return letters + id + "@" + KIOSK_STUDENT_EMAIL_DOMAIN_;
}

function kioskFindRosterStudent_(studentId, studentName) {
  var key = attendanceStudentKey_(studentId, studentName);
  var found = null;
  (getStudentRoster() || []).some(function(group) {
    return (group.students || []).some(function(st) {
      if (attendanceStudentKey_(st.id, st.fullName) === key) { found = { student: st, group: group }; return true; }
      return false;
    });
  });
  return found;
}

// Called by the kiosk when it shows a student the red warning.
function notifyRedWarning(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  var settings = getReminderSettings();
  if (!settings.studentEmail) return { sent: false, reason: "off" };
  if (payload.classKey && settings.emailOffClasses.indexOf(String(payload.classKey)) !== -1) {
    return { sent: false, reason: "emails off for this class" };
  }

  var match = kioskFindRosterStudent_(payload.studentId, payload.studentName);
  if (!match) return { sent: false, reason: "student not found" };
  var to = kioskStudentEmailAddress_(match.student.lastName, match.student.id);
  if (!to) return { sent: false, reason: "no address" };

  var week = getWeekPassMinutes();
  var sentLog = kioskReadJsonProp_("kioskPatternsSent") || {};
  var id = "student|" + attendanceStudentKey_(match.student.id, match.student.fullName) + "|" + week.weekStart;
  if (sentLog[id]) return { sent: false, reason: "already this week" };

  var stats = week.students[attendanceStudentKey_(match.student.id, match.student.fullName)] ||
    week.students["NAME:" + String(match.student.fullName || "").toLowerCase()] || {};
  var teacher = Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail();
  var first = match.student.firstName || match.student.fullName;
  var className = match.group.classTitle || "class";

  MailApp.sendEmail({
    to: to,
    cc: teacher,
    replyTo: teacher,
    subject: "About time out of " + className,
    body: "Hi " + first + ",\n\n" +
      "This is a note from your " + className + " teacher. You've left class " + (stats.twoWeekTrips || "several") +
      " times in the last 2 weeks. That's a lot of missed time. If you need time for personal business, " +
      "please spread it out across different classes instead of always taking it from this one.\n\n" +
      "If something is going on and causing this, let's talk about it please! Come see me, or reply to this email."
  });

  sentLog[id] = kioskTodayKey_();
  kioskWriteJsonProp_("kioskPatternsSent", sentLog);
  return { sent: true, to: to };
}

// For the editor: the school email address the kiosk would use for each
// student (first 10, plus any it can't make). Sends nothing.
function checkStudentEmails() {
  var lines = [], missing = [];
  (getStudentRoster() || []).forEach(function(group) {
    (group.students || []).forEach(function(st) {
      var address = kioskStudentEmailAddress_(st.lastName, st.id);
      if (!address) missing.push(st.fullName + " (" + group.period + ")");
      else if (lines.length < 10) lines.push(st.fullName + " → " + address);
    });
  });
  var report = "Examples:\n" + lines.join("\n") +
    (missing.length ? "\n\nNO ADDRESS (missing last name or ID): " + missing.join(", ") : "\n\nEvery student has an address.");
  Logger.log(report);
  return report;
}

/* ===== Waiting line for the room's hall passes =====
   When every hall pass is out (Settings: "Hall passes in your room"), a
   student who wants to leave joins the line. Only the first person in line
   may sign out when a pass comes back; the returning student is told to hand
   their pass to that person. Each entry leaves the line when that student
   signs out, taps "Leave the line", or their class period ends.
   Kept in Script Property kioskPassQueue: [{ key, name, displayName, at }]. */
function kioskReadQueue_() {
  var list = kioskReadJsonProp_("kioskPassQueue") || [];
  var now = new Date();
  return list.filter(function(e) {
    var at = new Date(e.at);
    if (isNaN(at.getTime())) return false;
    try { return now < kioskForgottenPassCloseTime_(at); } catch (err) { return now - at < 3600000; }
  });
}

function getPassQueue() {
  return kioskReadQueue_();
}

function kioskChangeQueue_(change) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try { lock.waitLock(20000); } catch (err) { return kioskReadQueue_(); }
  try {
    var list = change(kioskReadQueue_());
    kioskWriteJsonProp_("kioskPassQueue", list);
    return list;
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

// { studentId, studentName, displayName } -> the line after joining.
function joinPassQueue(student) {
  student = student || {};
  var key = attendanceStudentKey_(student.studentId, student.studentName);
  return kioskChangeQueue_(function(list) {
    if (!list.some(function(e) { return e.key === key; })) {
      list.push({ key: key, name: String(student.studentName || ""),
        displayName: String(student.displayName || student.studentName || ""),
        classLabel: String(student.classLabel || ""), at: new Date().toISOString() });
    }
    return list;
  });
}

function leavePassQueue(student) {
  student = student || {};
  var key = attendanceStudentKey_(student.studentId, student.studentName);
  return kioskChangeQueue_(function(list) {
    return list.filter(function(e) { return e.key !== key; });
  });
}

/* ===== Weekly Pass Summary tab =====
   One row per student per week: passes, minutes out, delivered passes,
   passes never returned, where they went and in which period. Rebuilt every
   day around 4 PM (its own timer); run buildWeeklyPassSummary in the editor
   to refresh it now. Older weeks stay below for comparison. */
function buildWeeklyPassSummary() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var logs = ensureLogHeaders();
  var weekStart = kioskWeekStartKey_();
  var tz = kioskTimeZone_();
  var reminder = getReminderSettings();
  var lastRow = logs.getLastRow();
  var students = {};

  if (lastRow >= 2) {
    var firstRow = kioskStartRowForDay_(logs, 3, weekStart, "week");
    if (firstRow <= lastRow) {
      var cols = Math.min(8, Math.max(5, logs.getLastColumn()));
      var data = logs.getRange(firstRow, 1, lastRow - firstRow + 1, cols).getValues();
      var blocksByDay = {};

      data.forEach(function(row) {
        var name = String(row[0] || "").trim();
        var timeOut = row[2];
        if (!name || !(timeOut instanceof Date) || kioskDayKeyOf_(timeOut, tz) < weekStart) return;

        var key = attendanceStudentKey_(row[7], name);
        var s = students[key] = students[key] ||
          { name: name, passes: 0, counted: 0, minutes: 0, delivered: 0, never: 0, places: {}, periods: {} };
        var timeIn = row[3] instanceof Date ? row[3] : new Date();

        s.passes++;
        if (String(row[5] || "").trim() === KIOSK_CALLED_BY_PASS_) s.delivered++;
        if (kioskPassCounts_(row, reminder, blocksByDay, tz)) {
          s.counted++;
          s.minutes += Math.max(0, (timeIn - timeOut) / 60000);
        }
        if (String(row[4] || "").trim() === KIOSK_NEVER_RETURNED_) s.never++;

        var place = String(row[1] || "").trim() || "Other";
        s.places[place] = (s.places[place] || 0) + 1;

        var m = timeOut.getHours() * 60 + timeOut.getMinutes();
        var block = (blocksByDay[kioskDayKeyOf_(timeOut, tz)] || []).filter(function(b) {
          return m >= b.openMinutes && m < b.endMinutes;
        })[0];
        var when = block ? block.period + " period" : Utilities.formatDate(timeOut, tz, "h a");
        s.periods[when] = (s.periods[when] || 0) + 1;
      });
    }
  }

  function top(counts, n) {
    return Object.keys(counts).sort(function(a, b) { return counts[b] - counts[a]; })
      .slice(0, n).map(function(k) { return k + (counts[k] > 1 ? " ×" + counts[k] : ""); }).join(", ");
  }

  var weekLabel = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(weekStart + "T12:00:00"), tz, "M/d/yyyy");
  var rows = Object.keys(students).map(function(k) {
    var s = students[k];
    var minutes = Math.round(s.minutes);
    var flags = [];
    if (minutes > reminder.minutes) flags.push("Over " + reminder.minutes + " min");
    if (s.counted >= reminder.trips) flags.push(s.counted + " trips");
    return [weekLabel, s.name, s.passes, s.counted, minutes, flags.join(", "),
      s.delivered, s.never, top(s.places, 3), top(s.periods, 2)];
  }).sort(function(a, b) { return (b[5] ? 1 : 0) - (a[5] ? 1 : 0) || b[4] - a[4]; });

  var header = ["Week Of", "Student", "All Passes", "Counted Trips", "Counted Minutes", "Flag",
    "Delivered Passes", "Never Returned", "Where", "When"];
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName("Weekly Pass Summary") || ss.insertSheet("Weekly Pass Summary");
  var old = sheet.getLastRow() >= 2
    ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, header.length).getDisplayValues()
        .filter(function(r) { return r[0] && r[0] !== weekLabel; })
    : [];

  sheet.clearContents();
  sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, header.length).setValues([header]).setFontWeight("bold");
  sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  var all = rows.concat(old);
  if (all.length) sheet.getRange(2, 1, all.length, header.length).setValues(all);
  try { sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, header.length).setNote(
    "Counted trips and minutes leave out delivered passes, passes from classes the reminder is off for, " +
    "and passes started during passing time or the first " + reminder.graceMinutes + " minutes of class. " +
    "Flag = over " + reminder.minutes + " minutes or " + reminder.trips + "+ trips this week. " +
    "This week is at the top; refreshed every afternoon."); } catch (err) {}

  var report = "Weekly Pass Summary: " + rows.length + " students this week (" +
    rows.filter(function(r) { return r[5]; }).length + " flagged).";
  Logger.log(report);
  return report;
}

/* ===== Yearly archive =====
   In July, rows from before July 1 move from Logs and Attendance Events to
   "Archive 2025-26 Logs" / "Archive 2025-26 Attendance" tabs (same columns),
   so the working tabs stay small and quick. Runs on its own each July (a
   monthly timer that only acts in July); run archiveLastSchoolYear in the
   editor to do it by hand. Only the older rows at the top of each tab move. */
function archiveLastSchoolYear() {
  var now = new Date();
  var cutoff = new Date(now.getMonth() >= 6 ? now.getFullYear() : now.getFullYear() - 1, 6, 1);
  var label = (cutoff.getFullYear() - 1) + "-" + String(cutoff.getFullYear()).slice(2);
  var lines = [];

  [["Logs", 3, "Logs"], ["Attendance Events", 2, "Attendance"]].forEach(function(item) {
    var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName(item[0]);
    if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) { lines.push(item[0] + ": nothing to move."); return; }

    var dates = sheet.getRange(2, item[1], sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
    var count = 0;
    while (count < dates.length) {
      var d = dates[count][0];
      if (!(d instanceof Date) || d >= cutoff) break;
      count++;
    }
    if (!count) { lines.push(item[0] + ": nothing from before " + Utilities.formatDate(cutoff, kioskTimeZone_(), "M/d/yyyy") + "."); return; }

    var width = sheet.getLastColumn();
    var name = "Archive " + label + " " + item[2];
    var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    var archive = ss.getSheetByName(name);
    if (!archive) {
      archive = ss.insertSheet(name);
      archive.getRange(1, 1, 1, width).setValues(sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, width).getValues());
      archive.setFrozenRows(1);
    }
    var values = sheet.getRange(2, 1, count, width).getValues();
    archive.getRange(archive.getLastRow() + 1, 1, count, width).setValues(values);
    sheet.deleteRows(2, count);
    lines.push(item[0] + ": moved " + count + " rows to \"" + name + "\".");
  });

  kioskForgetStartRows_();
  invalidateKioskCache_();
  var report = lines.join("\n");
  Logger.log(report);
  return report;
}

function kioskArchiveTimerHandler_() {
  if (new Date().getMonth() === 6) archiveLastSchoolYear();   // July
}

// Refreshes the Weekly Pass Summary every afternoon (its own timer, so it
// works whether or not the daily attendance report is turned on).
function kioskWeeklySummaryTimerHandler_() {
  try { buildWeeklyPassSummary(); } catch (summaryErr) {}
  try { kioskPatternEmailHandler_(); } catch (patternErr) {}
  if (Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), kioskTimeZone_(), "EEEE") === "Friday") {
    kioskSendFridaySummary_();
  }
}

// Fridays (afternoon timer): this week's Weekly Pass Summary as an email
// table, to the daily report address (or the owner). Once per week; turn off
// in Settings > Attendance > Out-of-class reminders. Run sendWeeklySummaryNow
// in the editor to send it any day.
function kioskSendFridaySummary_(force) {
  var settings = getReminderSettings();
  if (!settings.fridayEmail && !force) return "Friday email is off.";
  var weekStart = kioskWeekStartKey_();
  var sent = kioskReadJsonProp_("kioskPatternsSent") || {};
  if (sent["friday|" + weekStart] && !force) return "Already sent this week.";

  buildWeeklyPassSummary();
  var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Weekly Pass Summary");
  var tz = kioskTimeZone_();
  var weekLabel = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(weekStart + "T12:00:00"), tz, "M/d/yyyy");
  var rows = sheet && sheet.getLastRow() >= 2
    ? sheet.getRange(1, 1, sheet.getLastRow(), 10).getDisplayValues()
    : [];
  var header = rows[0] || [];
  var week = rows.slice(1).filter(function(r) { return r[0] === weekLabel; });

  function esc(t) { return String(t).replace(/[&<>]/g, function(c) { return { "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;" }[c]; }); }
  var cols = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9];   // everything but "Week Of"
  var html = "<p>Weekly Pass Summary for the week of " + esc(weekLabel) + ". Flagged students are at the top.</p>" +
    '<table style="border-collapse:collapse;font:14px Arial,sans-serif">' +
    "<tr>" + cols.map(function(c) { return '<th style="text-align:left;border-bottom:2px solid #333;padding:4px 8px">' + esc(header[c] || "") + "</th>"; }).join("") + "</tr>" +
    week.map(function(r) {
      var flagged = !!r[5];
      return '<tr style="' + (flagged ? "background:#ffe8ec" : "") + '">' +
        cols.map(function(c) { return '<td style="border-bottom:1px solid #ddd;padding:4px 8px">' + esc(r[c]) + "</td>"; }).join("") + "</tr>";
    }).join("") + "</table>" +
    "<p style=\"color:#666\">Counted trips and minutes leave out delivered passes, unchecked classes, and passes started " +
    "during passing time or the first " + settings.graceMinutes + " minutes of class.</p>";
  var text = "Weekly Pass Summary, week of " + weekLabel + "\n\n" + week.map(function(r) {
    return r[1] + ": " + r[3] + " counted trips, " + r[4] + " min" + (r[5] ? " (" + r[5] + ")" : "");
  }).join("\n");

  if (!week.length) {
    html = "<p>No passes this week (week of " + esc(weekLabel) + ").</p>";
    text = "No passes this week.";
  }

  var to = String(getAttendanceSettings().reportEmail || "").trim() || Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail();
  MailApp.sendEmail({ to: to, subject: "Hall Pass Kiosk: Weekly Pass Summary (week of " + weekLabel + ")",
    body: text, htmlBody: html });
  sent["friday|" + weekStart] = kioskTodayKey_();
  kioskWriteJsonProp_("kioskPatternsSent", sent);
  return "Emailed the Weekly Pass Summary to " + to + ".";
}

function sendWeeklySummaryNow() {
  var report = kioskSendFridaySummary_(true);
  Logger.log(report);
  return report;
}

// Installs the monthly archive timer and the daily summary timer once
// (checked at most every 6 hours).
function kioskEnsureArchiveTimer_() {
  try {
    var cache = kioskCache_();
    if (cache.get("kiosk:timersChecked")) return;
    var handlers = ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().map(function(t) { return t.getHandlerFunction(); });
    if (handlers.indexOf("kioskArchiveTimerHandler_") === -1) {
      ScriptApp.newTrigger("kioskArchiveTimerHandler_").timeBased().onMonthDay(15).atHour(3).create();
    }
    if (handlers.indexOf("kioskWeeklySummaryTimerHandler_") === -1) {
      ScriptApp.newTrigger("kioskWeeklySummaryTimerHandler_").timeBased().everyDays(1).atHour(16).create();
    }
    cache.put("kiosk:timersChecked", "1", 21600);
  } catch (err) {}
}

/* ===== Teacher PIN =====
   Set in Settings > Attendance. Stored only as a scrambled code (SHA-256)
   in Script Properties, never as the digits. Used on the kiosk to approve
   an optional trip for a student who is over the weekly limit. */
function kioskPinHash_(pin) {
  var bytes = Utilities.computeDigest(
    Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256,
    "hall-pass-kiosk|" + String(pin || "").trim()
  );
  return bytes.map(function(b) { return ((b + 256) % 256).toString(16).replace(/^(.)$/, "0$1"); }).join("");
}

function kioskTeacherPinIsSet_() {
  return !!PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("kioskTeacherPin");
}

// Settings > Attendance > Teacher PIN. A new PIN is 4 to 8 digits; changing
// an existing PIN needs the current one.
function setTeacherPin(newPin, currentPin) {
  newPin = String(newPin || "").trim();
  if (!/^\d{4,8}$/.test(newPin)) {
    return { success: false, message: "A PIN is 4 to 8 numbers." };
  }
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var saved = props.getProperty("kioskTeacherPin");
  if (saved && saved !== kioskPinHash_(currentPin)) {
    return { success: false, message: "The current PIN is not right." };
  }
  props.setProperty("kioskTeacherPin", kioskPinHash_(newPin));
  return { success: true, message: "Teacher PIN saved." };
}

// Settings > Attendance > Turn off PIN: needs the current PIN. With no PIN,
// students over the weekly limit can leave for optional trips on their own.
function removeTeacherPin(currentPin) {
  var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  var saved = props.getProperty("kioskTeacherPin");
  if (!saved) return { success: true, message: "No PIN is set." };
  if (saved !== kioskPinHash_(currentPin)) {
    return { success: false, message: "Enter the current PIN to turn it off." };
  }
  props.deleteProperty("kioskTeacherPin");
  return { success: true, message: "PIN turned off. Students can leave for optional trips without it." };
}

// The kiosk's number pad. Slows down guessing: after 5 wrong tries, waits
// 5 minutes.
function verifyTeacherPin(pin) {
  var cache = kioskCache_();
  var misses = Number(cache.get("kiosk:pinMisses") || 0);
  if (misses >= 5) {
    return { ok: false, message: "Too many tries. Wait a few minutes." };
  }
  var saved = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("kioskTeacherPin");
  if (!saved) return { ok: false, message: "No teacher PIN has been set yet." };
  if (saved === kioskPinHash_(pin)) {
    cache.remove("kiosk:pinMisses");
    return { ok: true };
  }
  cache.put("kiosk:pinMisses", String(misses + 1), 300);
  return { ok: false, message: "That PIN is not right." };
}

function checkOut(name, destination, fullName, calledByPass, classKey, studentId) {
  var sheet = ensureLogHeaders();
  var now = new Date();

  var storedName = String(fullName || name || "").trim();

  // Column F notes a pass delivered to class (Counselor, AP...), which does
  // not count toward the student's weekly minutes, or a trip the teacher
  // approved with the PIN (which does count).
  var note = calledByPass === "teacher" ? KIOSK_TEACHER_APPROVED_
    : calledByPass ? KIOSK_CALLED_BY_PASS_ : "";
  kioskEnsureLogNoteHeader_(sheet);
  // Columns G and H: the class ("period|class") and the student ID, so
  // reminders can skip classes and never mix up students with the same name.
  sheet.appendRow([storedName, destination, now, "", "", note,
    String(classKey || ""), String(studentId || "")]);

  try { leavePassQueue({ studentId: studentId, studentName: storedName }); } catch (queueErr) {}
  try { kioskCache_().remove("kiosk:weekStats:" + kioskTodayKey_()); } catch (cacheErr) {}

  var newRow = sheet.getLastRow();

  sheet.getRange(newRow, 3)
    .setNumberFormat("M/d/yyyy h:mm:ss");

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return getActivePasses();
}

function checkIn(rowNumber) {
  var sheet = ensureLogHeaders();
  var now = new Date();

  sheet.getRange(rowNumber, 4)
    .setValue(now)
    .setNumberFormat("M/d/yyyy h:mm:ss");

  var timeOut = new Date(sheet.getRange(rowNumber, 3).getValue());

  var elapsedSeconds = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.round((now.getTime() - timeOut.getTime()) / 1000)
  );

  sheet.getRange(rowNumber, 5)
    .setValue(formatDuration(elapsedSeconds));

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  return getActivePasses();
}

function formatDuration(totalSeconds) {
  totalSeconds = Math.max(0, Math.round(totalSeconds));

  var hours = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600);
  var minutes = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60);
  var seconds = totalSeconds % 60;

  if (hours > 0) {
    var hourText = hours + (hours === 1 ? " hr" : " hrs");

    if (minutes > 0) {
      return hourText + " " + minutes + " min " + seconds + " sec";
    }

    return hourText + " " + seconds + " sec";
  }

  if (minutes > 0) {
    return minutes + " min " + seconds + " sec";
  }

  return seconds + " sec";
}

/* =====================================================================
   TEACHER DASHBOARD
   Open the kiosk link with ?view=teacher added to the end.
   Everything here is additive: the student kiosk does not depend on it.
   ===================================================================== */
var KIOSK_TEACHER_CHANGE_KEY_ = "kiosk:teacherAttendanceChange";

function teacherIsDate_(value) {
  return Object.prototype.toString.call(value) === "[object Date]";
}

function teacherDashboardTz_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  return ss.getSpreadsheetTimeZone() || Session.getScriptTimeZone();
}

function teacherClockMillis_(clockText, now, tz) {
  var minutes = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(String(clockText || ""));
  if (minutes == null) return 0;
  var nowMinutes = attendanceNowMinutes_(now, tz);
  return Math.round(now.getTime() + (minutes - nowMinutes) * 60000);
}

function teacherDashboardGroup_(roster, options, status) {
  roster = roster || [];
  var wantedKey = String(options.classKey || "").trim();
  var i;

  if (wantedKey) {
    for (i = 0; i < roster.length; i++) {
      if (String(roster[i].key) === wantedKey) return roster[i];
    }
  }

  var active = normalizeRosterPeriodForMatch_(
    status && status.activeRosterPeriod
  );

  if (active) {
    for (i = 0; i < roster.length; i++) {
      if (normalizeRosterPeriodForMatch_(roster[i].period) === active) {
        return roster[i];
      }
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function teacherTodayPassLog_(now, tz) {
  var sheet = ensureLogHeaders();
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  var out = [];
  if (lastRow < 2) return out;

  // Only today's passes (newest 400 at most).
  var firstRow = Math.max(kioskTodayStartRow_(sheet, 3), lastRow - 399);
  var count = lastRow - firstRow + 1;
  if (count < 1) return out;
  var values = sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1, count, 5).getValues();
  var display = sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1, count, 5).getDisplayValues();
  var todayKey = Utilities.formatDate(now, tz, "yyyy-MM-dd");

  for (var i = values.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    var name = String(values[i][0] || "").trim();
    var timeOut = values[i][2];
    if (!name || !(teacherIsDate_(timeOut)) || isNaN(timeOut.getTime())) {
      continue;
    }
    if (Utilities.formatDate(timeOut, tz, "yyyy-MM-dd") !== todayKey) {
      continue;
    }
    var timeIn = values[i][3];
    var hasIn = teacherIsDate_(timeIn) && !isNaN(timeIn.getTime());
    out.push({
      row: firstRow + i,
      name: name,
      destination: String(values[i][1] || ""),
      outMs: timeOut.getTime(),
      inMs: hasIn ? timeIn.getTime() : 0,
      duration: String(display[i][4] || "")
    });
    if (out.length >= 150) break;
  }

  return out;
}

/* One request gives the dashboard everything it shows. */
// Each student's emoji for today and its name, for the teacher view
// ("What's my emoji?"). Names come from the Attendance Emoji Bank notes.
function kioskEmojiNames_() {
  var names = {};
  (getAttendanceEmojiBank() || []).forEach(function(item) {
    var emoji = String(item.emoji || "").trim();
    var note = String(item.notes || "").split(/\s+[—-]\s+/)[0].trim();
    if (/^CUSTOM_/.test(emoji)) {
      names[emoji] = (note.split(/\s+/)[0] || "Custom") + " (dog stamp)";
    } else {
      names[emoji] = note || String(item.category || "").trim();
    }
  });
  return names;
}

// Students in a class who forgot to check in (teacher check-ins marked
// "Forgot to check in") 2 or more times in the last 4 weeks, most first.
var KIOSK_FORGOT_DAYS_ = 28;
var KIOSK_FORGOT_MIN_ = 2;

function kioskForgetfulStudents_(period, className) {
  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  var lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2 || sheet.getLastColumn() < ATTENDANCE_TEACHER_REASON_COLUMN_) return [];
  var tz = kioskTimeZone_();
  var since = new Date();
  since.setDate(since.getDate() - (KIOSK_FORGOT_DAYS_ - 1));
  var sinceKey = Utilities.formatDate(since, tz, "yyyy-MM-dd");
  var firstRow = kioskStartRowForDay_(sheet, 2, sinceKey, "forgot");
  if (firstRow > lastRow) return [];

  var rows = sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1, lastRow - firstRow + 1, ATTENDANCE_TEACHER_REASON_COLUMN_).getValues();
  var counts = {};
  rows.forEach(function(r) {
    if (r[ATTENDANCE_TEACHER_REASON_COLUMN_ - 1] !== KIOSK_REASON_FORGOT_) return;
    if (String(r[3] || "").trim() !== String(period || "").trim()) return;
    if (String(r[4] || "").trim() !== String(className || "").trim()) return;
    if (kioskDayKeyOf_(r[1], tz) < sinceKey) return;
    var key = attendanceStudentKey_(r[6], r[5]);
    var c = counts[key] = counts[key] || { key: key, name: String(r[5] || ""), times: 0, last: "" };
    c.times++;
    c.last = Utilities.formatDate(r[1], tz, "EEE M/d");
  });

  return Object.keys(counts).map(function(k) { return counts[k]; })
    .filter(function(c) { return c.times >= KIOSK_FORGOT_MIN_; })
    .sort(function(a, b) { return b.times - a.times || a.name.localeCompare(b.name); });
}

/* ===== Seating charts (from ASL Study) =====
   The seating page (Seating.html, ?view=seating) is ASL Study's seating
   chart, group maker and name picker, built by dev/build-seating.py. It
   calls these with ASL Study's names (api_seating, api_seatingSave), so the
   copied code runs unchanged. A class is its label ("1 Period — ASL 1");
   a student is their kiosk key ("ID:41000"). One "using now" chart per
   class on the Seating Charts tab; reusable layouts on the Seating Layouts
   tab (desks only, or desks with students). */
var KIOSK_SEAT_HEAD_ = ["Class", "Chart", "Updated"];
var KIOSK_LAYOUT_HEAD_ = ["Layout", "Chart", "With students", "Updated"];

function kioskSeatTab_(name, head) {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  var sheet = ss.getSheetByName(name) || ss.insertSheet(name);
  var cur = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).getValues()[0];
  if (cur.join("|") !== head.join("|")) sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, head.length).setValues([head]).setFontWeight("bold");
  return sheet;
}

function kioskSeatClasses_() {
  return (getStudentRoster() || []).map(function(g) {
    return { label: g.label || (g.period + " " + g.classTitle), group: g };
  });
}

// ASL Study's api_seating: the class list, one class's students and its chart.
function api_seating(auth, cls, opts) {
  var all = kioskSeatClasses_();
  var labels = all.map(function(c) { return c.label; });
  cls = String(cls || "");
  if (!cls) {      // no class chosen yet (a Tools window): the class in session now
    try {
      var live = normalizeRosterPeriodForMatch_((getBellScheduleStatus("__AUTO__") || {}).activeRosterPeriod);
      var liveMatch = live ? all.filter(function(c) { return normalizeRosterPeriodForMatch_(c.group.period) === live; })[0] : null;
      if (liveMatch) cls = liveMatch.label;
    } catch (liveErr) {}
  }
  if (labels.indexOf(cls) < 0) cls = labels[0] || "";
  var counts = {};
  all.forEach(function(c) { counts[c.label] = (c.group.students || []).length; });
  var match = all.filter(function(c) { return c.label === cls; })[0];
  // Student pictures from the Students tab (column F), the same ones the kiosk shows.
  if (opts && opts.absentOnly) {      // the Name picker's once-a-minute check: attendance only, no photos
    var awayOnly = kioskSeatAbsent_(match);
    return { cls: cls, absent: awayOnly ? awayOnly.absent : [], absentNote: awayOnly ? awayOnly.note : "" };
  }
  var pics = {};
  if (match) {
    try {
      pics = getStudentPhotos((match.group.students || []).map(function(st) { return st.rowNumber; })) || {};
    } catch (picErr) { pics = {}; }
  }
  var students = match ? (match.group.students || []).map(function(st) {
    return [attendanceStudentKey_(st.id, st.fullName), st.fullName || st.firstName, pics[st.rowNumber] || "", ""];
  }) : [];
  students.sort(function(a, b) { return a[1].toLowerCase() < b[1].toLowerCase() ? -1 : 1; });

  var chart = null;
  var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Seating Charts");
  if (sheet && sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues().forEach(function(r) {
      if (String(r[0]) === cls && r[1]) { try { chart = JSON.parse(r[1]); } catch (e) {} }
    });
  }
  var away = kioskSeatAbsent_(match);
  return { classes: labels, counts: counts, cls: cls, students: students, chart: chart,
    absent: away ? away.absent : [], absentNote: away ? away.note : "" };
}

// Who is not here today, from kiosk check-ins, for the Group maker and Name picker's "Who Is Here
// Today?". Students out on a pass count as here (they are coming back); not checked in, or left for
// the day (early departure), count as absent. Nothing is marked until someone in the class checks in.
function kioskSeatAbsent_(match) {
  if (!match) return null;
  try {
    if ((getAttendanceSettings() || {}).enabled === false) return null;
    var states = (getAttendanceState({ period: match.group.period, className: match.group.classTitle }) || {}).states || [];
    var byKey = {};
    states.forEach(function(st) { byKey[st.studentKey] = String(st.status || ""); });
    var absent = [], anyHere = false;
    (match.group.students || []).forEach(function(st) {
      var key = attendanceStudentKey_(st.id, st.fullName), raw = byKey[key] || "";
      var here = raw.indexOf("Pending") === 0 || raw === "Present" || raw === "Tardy";
      if (here) anyHere = true; else absent.push(key);
    });
    return anyHere ? { absent: absent, note: "From today's check-ins" } : null;
  } catch (err) {
    return null;
  }
}

function api_seatingSave(auth, cls, chart) {
  cls = String(cls || "");
  if (!cls) throw new Error("Pick a class first.");
  var json = JSON.stringify(chart || {});
  if (json.length > 45000) throw new Error("That seating chart is too big to save.");
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var sheet = kioskSeatTab_("Seating Charts", KIOSK_SEAT_HEAD_);
    var n = sheet.getLastRow(), row = 0;
    if (n >= 2) sheet.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, 1).getValues().forEach(function(r, i) { if (!row && String(r[0]) === cls) row = i + 2; });
    sheet.getRange(row || Math.max(n, 1) + 1, 1, 1, 3).setValues([[cls, json, new Date()]]);
  });
  return { ok: true };
}

// Saved layouts: reuse a room (desks only) or a whole arrangement (with students).
function listSeatingLayouts() {
  var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Seating Layouts");
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return [];
  return sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 4).getValues()
    .filter(function(r) { return r[0] && r[1]; })
    .map(function(r) {
      var chart = null; try { chart = JSON.parse(r[1]); } catch (e) {}
      return { name: String(r[0]), withStudents: r[2] === true || String(r[2]).toLowerCase() === "yes",
        updated: r[3] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(r[3], kioskTimeZone_(), "M/d/yyyy") : "",
        desks: chart && chart.desks ? chart.desks.length : 0, chart: chart };
    });
}

function saveSeatingLayout(name, chart, withStudents) {
  name = String(name || "").replace(/[<>]/g, "").trim().slice(0, 60);
  if (!name) throw new Error("Give the layout a name.");
  var copy = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(chart || {}));
  if (!withStudents) { copy.seats = {}; copy.pins = {}; copy.rules = []; }
  var json = JSON.stringify(copy);
  if (json.length > 45000) throw new Error("That layout is too big to save.");
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var sheet = kioskSeatTab_("Seating Layouts", KIOSK_LAYOUT_HEAD_);
    var n = sheet.getLastRow(), row = 0;
    if (n >= 2) sheet.getRange(2, 1, n - 1, 1).getValues().forEach(function(r, i) { if (!row && String(r[0]) === name) row = i + 2; });
    sheet.getRange(row || Math.max(n, 1) + 1, 1, 1, 4).setValues([[name, json, withStudents ? "Yes" : "No", new Date()]]);
  });
  return listSeatingLayouts();
}

function deleteSeatingLayout(name) {
  var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Seating Layouts");
  if (sheet && sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    var names = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
    for (var i = names.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (String(names[i][0]) === String(name)) sheet.deleteRow(i + 2);
  }
  return listSeatingLayouts();
}

// For the kiosk: the seating page's address and which classes have a chart.
function getSeatingForKiosk() {
  var classes = [];
  var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Seating Charts");
  if (sheet && sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues().forEach(function(r) {
      try { var c = JSON.parse(r[1]); if (c && c.desks && c.desks.length) classes.push(String(r[0])); } catch (e) {}
    });
  }
  return { link: getSeatingLink(), classes: classes };
}

// The seating page's web address (teacher view button, kiosk "Where do I sit?").
function getSeatingLink() {
  try { return ScriptApp.getService().getUrl(); } catch (err) { return ""; }
}

// ----- Class memories: funny moments the teacher types in the teacher view, kept on the
// "Class Memories" tab (newest at the bottom of the tab, newest first in the teacher view).
var KIOSK_MEMORY_HEAD_ = ["When", "Class", "Memory", "ID", "Photo"];      // Photo = a Drive file in "Hall Pass Kiosk Memories"

function getClassMemories() {
  var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Class Memories");
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return [];
  var tz = teacherDashboardTz_();
  return sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 5).getValues()
    .filter(function(r) { return String(r[2] || "").trim() || String(r[4] || "").trim(); })
    .map(function(r) {
      return {
        id: String(r[3] || ""),
        when: r[0] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(r[0], tz, "EEE, MMM d, yyyy · h:mm a") : String(r[0] || ""),
        cls: String(r[1] || ""),
        text: String(r[2] || ""),
        photo: !!String(r[4] || "").trim()
      };
    })
    .reverse();
}

function saveClassMemory(text, cls, photoDataUrl) {
  text = String(text || "").trim().slice(0, 2000);
  var photo = String(photoDataUrl || "");
  if (!text && !photo) throw new Error("Type the memory or add a photo first.");
  cls = String(cls || "").replace(/^—$/, "").trim().slice(0, 80);
  var photoId = "";
  if (photo) {      // a picture the teacher view already made smaller; kept in their own Drive, never shared
    var m = photo.match(/^data:(image\/(?:jpeg|png|webp|gif));base64,([A-Za-z0-9+\/=]+)$/);
    if (!m) throw new Error("That picture could not be read. Try a JPG or PNG.");
    if (m[2].length > 8 * 1024 * 1024) throw new Error("That picture is too big.");
    var folders = DriveApp.getFoldersByName("Hall Pass Kiosk Memories");
    var folder = folders.hasNext() ? folders.next() : DriveApp.createFolder("Hall Pass Kiosk Memories");
    var ext = m[1] === "image/jpeg" ? "jpg" : m[1].split("/")[1];
    photoId = folder.createFile(Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(m[2]), m[1], "memory-" + Date.now() + "." + ext)).getId();
  }
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var sheet = kioskSeatTab_("Class Memories", KIOSK_MEMORY_HEAD_);
    sheet.appendRow([new Date(), cls, text, "m" + Date.now() + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000), photoId]);
  });
  return getClassMemories();
}

// A memory's photo as a data: link, only for photos listed on the Class Memories tab (no other Drive files).
function kioskMemoryPhotoIdFor_(memoryId) {
  var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Class Memories");
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return "";
  var hit = "";
  sheet.getRange(2, 4, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues().forEach(function(r) { if (!hit && String(r[0]) === String(memoryId)) hit = String(r[1] || "").trim(); });
  return hit;
}
function kioskPhotoDataUrl_(fileId) {
  try {
    var blob = DriveApp.getFileById(fileId).getBlob();
    return "data:" + blob.getContentType() + ";base64," + Utilities.base64Encode(blob.getBytes());
  } catch (err) { return ""; }
}
function getMemoryPhoto(memoryId) {
  var fileId = kioskMemoryPhotoIdFor_(memoryId);
  return fileId ? kioskPhotoDataUrl_(fileId) : "";
}

function kioskLiveVideoId_(url) {
  var m = String(url || "").match(/(?:v=|youtu\.be\/|\/live\/|\/embed\/|\/shorts\/)([\w-]{11})/);
  return m ? m[1] : "";
}

function kioskLiveTab_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), fresh = !ss.getSheetByName("Living Screensavers");
  var sheet = kioskSeatTab_("Living Screensavers", KIOSK_LIVE_HEAD_);
  if (fresh && sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {      // the first time: her own picks
    var now = new Date();
    var rows = KIOSK_LIVE_SEED_.map(function(c, i) {
      var vid = kioskLiveVideoId_(c[4]);
      return ["c" + (Date.now() + i) + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000), c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3], c[4], vid, c[5], vid ? "https://i.ytimg.com/vi/" + vid + "/hqdefault.jpg" : "",
        c[6], "", "", c[7], "", c[8], "", c[9], i + 1, now];
    });
    sheet.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, KIOSK_LIVE_HEAD_.length).setValues(rows);
  }
  return sheet;
}

function getLiveCams() {
  var sheet = kioskLiveTab_(), out = [];
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return out;
  sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, KIOSK_LIVE_HEAD_.length).getValues().forEach(function(r) {
    if (!String(r[0] || "").trim()) return;
    out.push({ id: String(r[0]), name: String(r[1] || ""), animal: String(r[2] || ""), habitat: String(r[3] || ""), org: String(r[4] || ""), url: String(r[5] || ""),
      vid: String(r[6] || "") || kioskLiveVideoId_(r[5]), page: String(r[7] || ""), thumb: String(r[8] || ""), cats: String(r[9] || "").split(/\s*,\s*/).filter(String),
      desc: String(r[10] || ""), fav: String(r[11] || "") === "Yes", activity: String(r[12] || ""), hours: String(r[13] || ""), tz: String(r[14] || ""),
      notes: String(r[15] || ""), status: String(r[16] || ""), order: Number(r[17]) || 0 });
  });
  out.sort(function(a, b) { return a.order - b.order; });
  return out;
}

// Add a camera or change one (same ID): every field, including a new YouTube link for the same camera.
function saveLiveCam(c) {
  c = c || {};
  var name = String(c.name || "").trim().slice(0, 120), url = String(c.url || "").trim().slice(0, 500);
  if (!name) throw new Error("Give the camera a name.");
  var vid = kioskLiveVideoId_(url);
  var cats = (Array.isArray(c.cats) ? c.cats : String(c.cats || "").split(",")).map(function(x) { return String(x).trim(); }).filter(String).join(", ");
  var activity = ["Calm", "Moderate", "Active"].indexOf(c.activity) >= 0 ? c.activity : "Calm";
  var row = [String(c.id || ""), name, String(c.animal || "").slice(0, 120), String(c.habitat || "").slice(0, 160), String(c.org || "").slice(0, 120), url, vid,
    String(c.page || "").trim().slice(0, 500), vid ? "https://i.ytimg.com/vi/" + vid + "/hqdefault.jpg" : String(c.thumb || ""), cats, String(c.desc || "").slice(0, 500),
    c.fav ? "Yes" : "", activity, String(c.hours || "").slice(0, 120), String(c.tz || "").slice(0, 120), String(c.notes || "").slice(0, 1000), String(c.status || "").slice(0, 120)];
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var sheet = kioskLiveTab_(), last = sheet.getLastRow(), ids = last >= 2 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, 1).getValues().map(function(r) { return String(r[0]); }) : [];
    var at = row[0] ? ids.indexOf(row[0]) : -1;
    if (at >= 0) sheet.getRange(at + 2, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    else {
      row[0] = "c" + Date.now() + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000);
      var orders = last >= 2 ? sheet.getRange(2, 18, last - 1, 1).getValues().map(function(r) { return Number(r[0]) || 0; }) : [0];
      sheet.appendRow(row.concat([Math.max.apply(null, orders.concat([0])) + 1, new Date()]));
    }
  });
  return getLiveCams();
}

function kioskLiveChange_(id, fn) {
  id = String(id || "");
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var sheet = kioskLiveTab_(); if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return;
    var ids = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
    for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) if (String(ids[i][0]) === id) { fn(sheet, i + 2); break; }
  });
  return getLiveCams();
}
function setLiveCamFavorite(id, on) { return kioskLiveChange_(id, function(sheet, row) { sheet.getRange(row, 12).setValue(on ? "Yes" : ""); }); }
function deleteLiveCam(id) { return kioskLiveChange_(id, function(sheet, row) { sheet.deleteRow(row); }); }
// Move a camera up or down the list (the gallery's order).
function moveLiveCam(id, dir) {
  var cams = getLiveCams(), i = cams.map(function(c) { return c.id; }).indexOf(String(id)), j = i + (dir < 0 ? -1 : 1);
  if (i < 0 || j < 0 || j >= cams.length) return cams;
  var a = cams[i], b = cams[j];
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var sheet = kioskLiveTab_(), ids = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().map(function(r) { return String(r[0]); });
    sheet.getRange(ids.indexOf(a.id) + 2, 18).setValue(b.order || j + 1);
    sheet.getRange(ids.indexOf(b.id) + 2, 18).setValue(a.order || i + 1);
  });
  return getLiveCams();
}

// A pasted YouTube link: its video ID plus the title, channel and picture from YouTube's public oEmbed (no key, no account).
// embeddable: false when YouTube answers 401 (the owner turned embedding off); found: false when the video is gone or private.
function lookupLiveCam(url) {
  url = String(url || "").trim();
  var vid = kioskLiveVideoId_(url), playlist = /[?&]list=/.test(url) && !vid;
  var out = { url: url, vid: vid, playlist: playlist, found: false, embeddable: null, title: "", org: "", orgUrl: "", thumb: vid ? "https://i.ytimg.com/vi/" + vid + "/hqdefault.jpg" : "" };
  if (!vid && !playlist) return out;
  try {
    var r = UrlFetchApp.fetch("https://www.youtube.com/oembed?format=json&url=" + encodeURIComponent(vid ? "https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=" + vid : url), { muteHttpExceptions: true });
    var code = r.getResponseCode();
    if (code === 200) { var d = JSON.parse(r.getContentText()); out.found = true; out.embeddable = true; out.title = d.title || ""; out.org = d.author_name || ""; out.orgUrl = d.author_url || ""; if (!vid && d.thumbnail_url) out.thumb = d.thumbnail_url; }
    else if (code === 401 || code === 403) { out.found = true; out.embeddable = false; }
  } catch (err) {}
  return out;
}
