    facts: "Almost 30,000 people voted to name her Opal, after the shimmer inside an abalone shell.", photo: "https://d1v5k86lqgwnzg.cloudfront.net/assets/images/exhibits/sea-otters/sea-otter-opal-TR25-0692.png" },
  { vid: "abbR-Ttd-cA", name: "Willow", species: "Southern sea otter", age: "Born 2022", personality: "Industrious, resourceful and dexterous; she enjoys playing and wrestling with her raft mates.",
    background: "Found stranded on Carmel State Beach in 2022 as a six-week-old pup, declared non-releasable, and raised at Aquarium of the Pacific.",
    facts: "Lived three years at Shedd Aquarium in Chicago before coming to Monterey.", joined: "April 2026", photo: "https://d1v5k86lqgwnzg.cloudfront.net/assets/images/animals/exhibit-otters/Sea-otter-Willow-TR26-0196.jpeg" },
  { vid: "abbR-Ttd-cA", name: "Suri", species: "Southern sea otter", age: "Born 2022", personality: "Bold and independent.",
    background: "Found alone in the waves at Asilomar State Beach in 2022 as a four-week-old pup, rescued and raised at Aquarium of the Pacific.",
    facts: "Charmed guests at Shedd Aquarium in Chicago for three years before returning to Monterey.", joined: "April 2026" }
];
var KIOSK_RES_SEED_SRC_ = { "abbR-Ttd-cA": "https://www.montereybayaquarium.org/visit/exhibits/sea-otters/meet-our-otters https://www.montereybayaquarium.org/cams-videos/live-cams/sea-otter-cam" };

function kioskResTab_() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet(), fresh = !ss.getSheetByName("Living Residents");
  var sheet = kioskSeatTab_("Living Residents", KIOSK_RES_HEAD_);
  if (fresh && sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
    var byVid = {}; getLiveCams().forEach(function(c) { if (c.vid) byVid[c.vid] = c.id; });
    var rows = KIOSK_RES_SEED_.filter(function(r) { return byVid[r.vid]; }).map(function(r, i) {
      return ["r" + (Date.now() + i) + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000), byVid[r.vid], r.name, r.species || "", r.photo || "", r.age || "", r.sex || "", r.recognize || "", r.personality || "",
        r.favorites || "", r.background || "", r.facts || "", r.joined || "", KIOSK_RES_SEED_SRC_[r.vid] || "", "2026-10-17", "Yes", "", "Suggested", i + 1];
    });
    if (rows.length) sheet.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, KIOSK_RES_HEAD_.length).setValues(rows);
  }
  return sheet;
}

function getLiveResidents() {
  var sheet = kioskResTab_(), out = [];
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return out;
  sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, KIOSK_RES_HEAD_.length).getValues().forEach(function(r) {
    if (!String(r[0] || "").trim() || !String(r[2] || "").trim()) return;
    var o = {}; KIOSK_RES_KEYS_.forEach(function(k, i) { o[k] = r[i] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(r[i], teacherDashboardTz_(), "yyyy-MM-dd") : String(r[i] == null ? "" : r[i]); });
    o.show = o.show !== "No"; o.archived = o.archived === "Yes"; o.order = Number(o.order) || 0; o.suggested = o.status === "Suggested";
    out.push(o);
  });
  out.sort(function(a, b) { return a.order - b.order; });
  return out;
}

// Add or change one resident (same ID). approve: true turns a Suggested profile into an approved one.
function saveLiveResident(r) {
  r = r || {};
  var name = String(r.name || "").trim().slice(0, 80), cam = String(r.cam || "").trim();
  if (!name) throw new Error("Give the animal a name.");
  if (!cam) throw new Error("Choose the camera this animal lives on.");
  var clip = function(k, n) { return String(r[k] == null ? "" : r[k]).trim().slice(0, n); };
  var row = [String(r.id || ""), cam, name, clip("species", 120), clip("photo", 600), clip("age", 80), clip("sex", 40), clip("recognize", 600), clip("personality", 600),
    clip("favorites", 400), clip("background", 800), clip("facts", 800), clip("joined", 80), clip("sources", 1200), clip("verified", 40),
    r.show === false ? "No" : "Yes", r.archived ? "Yes" : "", r.approve ? "" : clip("status", 40)];
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var sheet = kioskResTab_(), last = sheet.getLastRow(), ids = last >= 2 ? sheet.getRange(2, 1, last - 1, 1).getValues().map(function(x) { return String(x[0]); }) : [];
    var at = row[0] ? ids.indexOf(row[0]) : -1;
    if (at >= 0) sheet.getRange(at + 2, 1, 1, row.length).setValues([row]);
    else {
      row[0] = "r" + Date.now() + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000);
      var orders = last >= 2 ? sheet.getRange(2, 19, last - 1, 1).getValues().map(function(x) { return Number(x[0]) || 0; }) : [0];
      sheet.appendRow(row.concat([Math.max.apply(null, orders.concat([0])) + 1]));
    }
  });
  return getLiveResidents();
}

function deleteLiveResident(id) {
  id = String(id || "");
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var sheet = kioskResTab_(); if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return;
    var ids = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues();
    for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) if (String(ids[i][0]) === id) { sheet.deleteRow(i + 2); break; }
  });
  return getLiveResidents();
}

// Move a resident earlier or later among the residents of the same camera.
function moveLiveResident(id, dir) {
  var all = getLiveResidents(), me = all.filter(function(r) { return r.id === String(id); })[0]; if (!me) return all;
  var same = all.filter(function(r) { return r.cam === me.cam && r.archived === me.archived; }), i = same.indexOf(me), j = i + (dir < 0 ? -1 : 1);
  if (j < 0 || j >= same.length) return all;
  var other = same[j];
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var sheet = kioskResTab_(), ids = sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().map(function(x) { return String(x[0]); });
    sheet.getRange(ids.indexOf(me.id) + 2, 19).setValue(other.order || j + 1);
    sheet.getRange(ids.indexOf(other.id) + 2, 19).setValue(me.order || i + 1);
  });
  return getLiveResidents();
}

// A photo from the teacher's computer: saved in the Drive folder "Living Screensavers Residents", view-only for anyone with the link
// (the same way the kiosk keeps its background pictures), so the Projector window can show it.
function uploadLiveResidentPhoto(dataUrl, name) {
  var m = String(dataUrl || "").match(/^data:(image\/(png|jpeg|webp|gif));base64,(.+)$/);
  if (!m) throw new Error("That file is not a picture (PNG, JPG, WEBP or GIF).");
  if (m[3].length > 7000000) throw new Error("That picture is too big. Use one under 5 MB.");
  var blob = Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(m[3]), m[1], String(name || "resident").replace(/[^\w .-]/g, "").slice(0, 60) + "." + m[2].replace("jpeg", "jpg"));
  var folders = DriveApp.getFoldersByName("Living Screensavers Residents"), folder = folders.hasNext() ? folders.next() : DriveApp.createFolder("Living Screensavers Residents");
  var file = folder.createFile(blob);
  file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
  return "https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/d/" + file.getId() + "=w800";
}

// ----- To-do checklist (teacher view): reminders in two lists, Now and Later, on the "To-Do" tab.
// Open ones (and ones checked off today) show in the teacher view and in the end-of-day email.
var KIOSK_TODO_HEAD_ = ["ID", "List", "Task", "Done", "Added", "Done At", "Due"];

function kioskTodoRows_() {
  var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("To-Do");
  if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return { sheet: sheet, rows: [] };
  return { sheet: sheet, rows: sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 7).getValues() };
}

function getTodos() {
  var tz = teacherDashboardTz_();
  var today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), tz, "yyyy-MM-dd");
  var out = [];
  kioskTodoRows_().rows.forEach(function(r) {
    if (!String(r[2] || "").trim()) return;
    var done = String(r[3] || "") === "Yes";
    var doneDay = r[5] instanceof Date ? Utilities.formatDate(r[5], tz, "yyyy-MM-dd") : "";
    if (done && doneDay !== today) return;      // checked off on an earlier day: kept on the tab only
    var due = kioskTodoDueKey_(r[6], tz), list = String(r[1]) === "Later" ? "Later" : "Now", dueNow = false;
    if (list === "Later" && due && due <= today) { list = "Now"; dueNow = true; }      // a Later item moves to Now on its day
    out.push({ id: String(r[0]), list: list, text: String(r[2]), done: done, due: due,
      dueText: due ? Utilities.formatDate(kioskTodoDueDate_(due), tz, "EEE, MMM d") : "",
      dueState: !due ? "" : due < today ? "overdue" : due === today ? "today" : "later", dueNow: dueNow });
  });
  return out;
}

// A due date as "yyyy-MM-dd" (from a date cell, or text like 2026-10-24 or 10/24/2026); "" when none.
function kioskTodoDueKey_(v, tz) {
  if (v instanceof Date && !isNaN(v)) return Utilities.formatDate(v, tz, "yyyy-MM-dd");
  var t = String(v || "").trim(), m;
  if ((m = t.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})$/))) return m[1] + "-" + ("0" + m[2]).slice(-2) + "-" + ("0" + m[3]).slice(-2);
  if ((m = t.match(/^(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})$/))) return m[3] + "-" + ("0" + m[1]).slice(-2) + "-" + ("0" + m[2]).slice(-2);
  return "";
}
function kioskTodoDueDate_(key) {      // noon that day, so time zones never shift the date
  var p = key.split("-"); return new Date(Number(p[0]), Number(p[1]) - 1, Number(p[2]), 12, 0, 0);
}

function kioskTodoChange_(id, fn) {
  id = String(id || "");
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var t = kioskTodoRows_();
    for (var i = 0; i < t.rows.length; i++) {
      if (String(t.rows[i][0]) === id) { fn(t.sheet, i + 2, t.rows[i]); break; }
    }
  });
  return getTodos();
}

function addTodo(text, list, due) {
  text = String(text || "").trim().slice(0, 500);
  if (!text) throw new Error("Type the reminder first.");
  var dueKey = kioskTodoDueKey_(due, teacherDashboardTz_());
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var sheet = kioskSeatTab_("To-Do", KIOSK_TODO_HEAD_);
    sheet.appendRow(["t" + Date.now() + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000), list === "Later" ? "Later" : "Now", text, "", new Date(), "",
      dueKey ? kioskTodoDueDate_(dueKey) : ""]);
  });
  return getTodos();
}

// Set or clear (due = "") a to-do's due date.
function setTodoDue(id, due) {
  var dueKey = kioskTodoDueKey_(due, teacherDashboardTz_());
  return kioskTodoChange_(id, function(sheet, row) { sheet.getRange(row, 7).setValue(dueKey ? kioskTodoDueDate_(dueKey) : ""); });
}

function setTodoDone(id, done) {
  return kioskTodoChange_(id, function(sheet, row) {
    sheet.getRange(row, 4, 1, 3).setValues([[done ? "Yes" : "", sheet.getRange(row, 5).getValue(), done ? new Date() : ""]]);
  });
}

function moveTodo(id, list) {
  var tz = teacherDashboardTz_(), today = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), tz, "yyyy-MM-dd");
  return kioskTodoChange_(id, function(sheet, row, r) {
    sheet.getRange(row, 2).setValue(list === "Later" ? "Later" : "Now");
    // moving an item that is already due back to Later: forget the date, or it would jump straight back to Now
    var due = kioskTodoDueKey_(r[6], tz);
    if (list === "Later" && due && due <= today) sheet.getRange(row, 7).setValue("");
  });
}

function deleteTodo(id) {
  return kioskTodoChange_(id, function(sheet, row) { sheet.deleteRow(row); });
}

// The to-do part of the end-of-day email ("" when there is nothing on the list).
function kioskTodoEmailHtml_() {
  var todos = [];
  try { todos = getTodos(); } catch (err) { return ""; }
  if (!todos.length) return "";
  var esc = function(t) { return String(t).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function(c) { return { "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" }[c]; }); };
  var part = function(list, title) {
    var open = todos.filter(function(t) { return t.list === list && !t.done; });
    if (!open.length) return "";
    return '<div style="font-weight:700;margin:8px 0 4px;">' + title + "</div>" +
      '<ul style="margin:0 0 6px;padding-left:20px;">' + open.map(function(t) {
        return "<li>☐ " + esc(t.text) + (t.dueText ? ' <span style="color:' + (t.dueState === "overdue" ? "#c0504d" : "#6a7888") + ';">(' +
          (t.dueState === "overdue" ? "overdue, " : t.dueState === "today" ? "due today, " : "due ") + esc(t.dueText) + ")</span>" : "") + "</li>";
      }).join("") + "</ul>";
  };
  var doneToday = todos.filter(function(t) { return t.done; });
  var html = part("Now", "Now") + part("Later", "Later") +
    (doneToday.length ? '<div style="font-weight:700;margin:8px 0 4px;color:#2e7d5b;">Done today ✓</div>' +
      '<ul style="margin:0;padding-left:20px;color:#6a7888;">' + doneToday.map(function(t) { return '<li><s>' + esc(t.text) + "</s></li>"; }).join("") + "</ul>" : "");
  return '<div style="margin:6px 0 14px;padding:12px 14px;border:1px solid #d9e1e8;border-radius:10px;background:#fbfcfe;">' +
    '<div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:700;">📝 Your to-do list</div>' + html + "</div>";
}

// The year-end page: every memory of one school year (August to July), by month, ready to print or
// save as a PDF. ?year= picks the school year (the August it starts in); &cls= keeps one class.
function kioskMemoriesPage_(params) {
  params = params || {};
  var tz = teacherDashboardTz_();
  var esc = function(t) { return String(t == null ? "" : t).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function(c) { return { "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" }[c]; }); };
  var schoolYear = function(d) { return d.getMonth() >= 7 ? d.getFullYear() : d.getFullYear() - 1; };   // August starts a school year
  var rows = [];
  var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Class Memories");
  if (sheet && sheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
    sheet.getRange(2, 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 5).getValues().forEach(function(r) {
      if (r[0] instanceof Date && (String(r[2] || "").trim() || String(r[4] || "").trim())) rows.push({ when: r[0], cls: String(r[1] || ""), text: String(r[2] || ""), photo: String(r[4] || "").trim() });
    });
  }
  var years = {};
  rows.forEach(function(m) { years[schoolYear(m.when)] = true; });
  var yearList = Object.keys(years).map(Number).sort(function(a, b) { return b - a; });
  var year = Number(params.year) || (yearList[0] || schoolYear(new Date()));
  var cls = String(params.cls || "");
  var mine = rows.filter(function(m) { return schoolYear(m.when) === year && (!cls || m.cls === cls); })
    .sort(function(a, b) { return a.when - b.when; });
  var classes = {};
  rows.filter(function(m) { return schoolYear(m.when) === year && m.cls; }).forEach(function(m) { classes[m.cls] = true; });
  var base = "";
  try { base = ScriptApp.getService().getUrl(); } catch (err) {}
  var link = function(y, c) { return esc(base + "?view=memories&year=" + y + (c ? "&cls=" + encodeURIComponent(c) : "")); };
  var months = [], byMonth = {};
  mine.forEach(function(m) {
    var key = Utilities.formatDate(m.when, tz, "yyyy-MM");
    if (!byMonth[key]) { byMonth[key] = { name: Utilities.formatDate(m.when, tz, "MMMM yyyy"), list: [] }; months.push(key); }
    byMonth[key].list.push(m);
  });
  var picks = '<div class="picks noprint">' +
    (yearList.length > 1 ? yearList.map(function(y) { return '<a class="chip' + (y === year ? " on" : "") + '" href="' + link(y, "") + '" target="_top">' + y + "–" + String(y + 1).slice(2) + "</a>"; }).join("") : "") +
    (Object.keys(classes).length > 1 ? '<a class="chip' + (!cls ? " on" : "") + '" href="' + link(year, "") + '" target="_top">All classes</a>' +
      Object.keys(classes).sort().map(function(c) { return '<a class="chip' + (c === cls ? " on" : "") + '" href="' + link(year, c) + '" target="_top">' + esc(c) + "</a>"; }).join("") : "") +
    '<button class="chip print" onclick="window.print()">🖨️ Print / Save as PDF</button></div>';
  var body = months.length ? months.map(function(k) {
    var mo = byMonth[k];
    return '<section><h2>' + esc(mo.name) + '</h2><div class="cards">' + mo.list.map(function(m) {
      var pic = m.photo ? kioskPhotoDataUrl_(m.photo) : "";
      return '<article>' + (pic ? '<img src="' + pic + '" alt="">' : '') + (m.text ? '<p>' + esc(m.text).replace(/\n/g, "<br>") + '</p>' : '') + '<footer>' + esc(Utilities.formatDate(m.when, tz, "EEE, MMM d")) +
        (m.cls && !cls ? " · " + esc(m.cls) : "") + "</footer></article>";
    }).join("") + "</div></section>";
  }).join("") : '<p class="empty">No memories saved for this school year yet. Type them in the 😂 Class memories box in the teacher view.</p>';
  return '<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><base target="_top"><meta charset="utf-8"><style>' +
    'body{margin:0;background:#fbf7ef;color:#2f2a26;font-family:"Segoe UI",system-ui,-apple-system,Roboto,Arial,sans-serif}' +
    '.wrap{max-width:1000px;margin:0 auto;padding:28px 22px 60px}' +
    'h1{font-size:40px;margin:0;text-align:center}.sub{text-align:center;color:#7a6e62;margin:6px 0 18px;font-size:17px}' +
    '.picks{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;justify-content:center;margin-bottom:22px}' +
    '.chip{font:inherit;font-size:14px;font-weight:700;padding:7px 12px;border-radius:999px;border:1px solid #e3d7c6;background:#fff;color:#2f2a26;text-decoration:none;cursor:pointer}' +
    '.chip.on{background:#2f6f73;color:#fff;border-color:#2f6f73}.chip.print{background:#f2b632;border-color:#f2b632}' +
    'h2{font-size:22px;margin:26px 0 10px;padding-bottom:6px;border-bottom:2px dashed #e3d7c6}' +
    '.cards{columns:2 300px;column-gap:14px}' +
    'article{break-inside:avoid;background:#fff;border-radius:14px;padding:14px 16px;margin:0 0 14px;box-shadow:0 2px 0 #eadfce,0 6px 16px rgba(80,60,30,.08)}' +
    'article:nth-child(3n+1){transform:rotate(-.4deg)}article:nth-child(3n+2){transform:rotate(.35deg)}' +
    'article img{display:block;width:100%;height:auto;border-radius:10px;margin:0 0 10px}article p{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:17px;line-height:1.45}article footer{font-size:13px;color:#8a7d6f;font-weight:600}' +
    '.empty{text-align:center;color:#7a6e62;font-size:18px;margin-top:40px}' +
    '@media print{body{background:#fff}.noprint{display:none}article{box-shadow:none;border:1px solid #ddd;transform:none!important}h2{break-after:avoid}}' +
    '</style></head><body><div class="wrap">' +
    '<h1>😂 Class Memories</h1><p class="sub">' + year + "–" + (year + 1) + " school year" + (cls ? " · " + esc(cls) : "") +
    " · " + mine.length + (mine.length === 1 ? " memory" : " memories") + "</p>" + picks + body +
    "</div></body></html>";
}

function deleteClassMemory(id) {
  id = String(id || "");
  kioskWithEmojiLock_(function() {
    var sheet = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet().getSheetByName("Class Memories");
    if (!id || !sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return;
    var ids = sheet.getRange(2, 4, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues();
    for (var i = ids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (String(ids[i][0]) === id) {
      if (String(ids[i][1] || "").trim()) { try { DriveApp.getFileById(String(ids[i][1]).trim()).setTrashed(true); } catch (e) {} }      // its photo goes to Drive's trash (can be restored)
      sheet.deleteRow(i + 2);
    }
  });
  return getClassMemories();
}

// The teacher view's "Drill view" button needs this app's web address.
function getDrillLink() {
  try {
    return ScriptApp.getService().getUrl() + "?view=drill";
  } catch (err) {
    return "";
  }
}

function getTeacherDashboard(options) {
  options = options || {};
  var tz = teacherDashboardTz_();
  var now = new Date();
  var kiosk = readKioskSettings() || {};
  var status = {};
  var roster = [];
  var attendanceSettings = {};

  try {
    status = getBellScheduleStatus(
      options.scheduleOverride || "__AUTO__"
    ) || {};
  } catch (err) {
    status = { phase: "manual", label: "Schedule unavailable" };
  }

  try { roster = getStudentRoster() || []; } catch (err2) { roster = []; }
  try { attendanceSettings = getAttendanceSettings() || {}; } catch (err3) {}

  var group = teacherDashboardGroup_(roster, options, status);
  var isLiveClass =
    !!group &&
    normalizeRosterPeriodForMatch_(group.period) ===
      normalizeRosterPeriodForMatch_(status.activeRosterPeriod);

  var students = [];

  if (group) {
    var stateByKey = {};

    if (attendanceSettings.enabled !== false) {
      try {
        var attendance = getAttendanceState({
          period: group.period,
          className: group.classTitle
        });
        (attendance && attendance.states || []).forEach(function(state) {
          stateByKey[state.studentKey] = state;
        });
      } catch (err4) {}
    }

    var plan = {};
    var emojiNames = {};
    var week = { students: {}, limit: 6, tripLimit: 3 };
    try { week = getWeekPassMinutes(); } catch (weekErr) {}
    try { plan = getDailyEmojiPlan().emojis || {}; } catch (planErr) {}
    try { emojiNames = kioskEmojiNames_(); } catch (namesErr) {}

    students = (group.students || []).map(function(student) {
      var key = attendanceStudentKey_(student.id, student.fullName);
      var state = stateByKey[key] || null;
      var emoji = String(state && state.emoji || plan[key] || "");
      var raw = state ? String(state.status || "") : "";
      var kind = "absent";

      if (raw.indexOf("Pending") === 0) {
        kind = raw.indexOf("Tardy") !== -1 ? "tardy" : "present";
      } else if (raw === "Present") {
        kind = "present";
      } else if (raw === "Tardy") {
        kind = "tardy";
      }

      if (
        kind === "tardy" &&
        normalizeAttendanceLateType_(state.lateType) ===
          ATTENDANCE_LATE_PASS_
      ) {
        kind = "latePass";
      }

      return {
        key: key,
        firstName: student.firstName || student.name || "",
        fullName: student.fullName || "",
        id: student.id || "",
        passRestricted: !!student.passRestricted,
        kind: kind,
        pending: raw.indexOf("Pending") === 0,
        leftEarly: !!(state && state.earlyDeparture),
        checkInText: state ? state.originalCheckInText || "" : "",
        emoji: emoji,
        emojiName: emojiNames[emoji] || "",
        // This week's counted trips and minutes (same rules as the reminder).
        weekTrips: (function() {
          var w = week.students[key] || week.students["NAME:" + String(student.fullName || "").toLowerCase()];
          return w ? w.trips : 0;
        })(),
        weekMinutes: (function() {
          var w = week.students[key] || week.students["NAME:" + String(student.fullName || "").toLowerCase()];
          return w ? Math.round(w.minutes) : 0;
        })(),
        weekFlag: (function() {
          var w = week.students[key] || week.students["NAME:" + String(student.fullName || "").toLowerCase()];
          if (!w) return "";
          var over = w.minutes > week.limit || w.trips >= week.tripLimit;
          return over && w.lastWeekOver ? "pattern" : over ? "over" : "";
        })(),
        minutesLate: state && kind !== "present" && kind !== "absent"
          ? String(state.minutesLate || "")
          : ""
      };
    });
  }

  var passes = [];
  try { passes = getActivePasses() || []; } catch (err5) { passes = []; }

  var log = [];
  try { log = teacherTodayPassLog_(now, tz); } catch (err6) { log = []; }

  kioskReportInUse_(false);      // at most once a day: lets Colleen know this copy is in use (not in her own kiosk)

  return {
    success: true,
    nowMs: now.getTime(),
    title: kiosk.mainTitle || kiosk.topTitle || "Hall Pass Kiosk",
    release: KIOSK_RELEASE,
    greenMinutes: Number(kiosk.greenThroughMinutes || 5),
    yellowMinutes: Number(kiosk.yellowThroughMinutes || 10),
    attendanceEnabled: attendanceSettings.enabled !== false,
    liveBellUrl: kiosk.liveBellScheduleEnabled !== false ? String(kiosk.liveBellScheduleUrl || "") : "",
    forgetful: group ? (function() { try { return kioskForgetfulStudents_(group.period, group.classTitle); } catch (e) { return []; } })() : [],
    passQueue: (function() { try { return getPassQueue(); } catch (e) { return []; } })(),
    status: {
      phase: String(status.phase || ""),
      label: String(status.label || ""),
      scheduleName: String(status.scheduleName || ""),
      period: String(status.period || ""),
      lateBell: String(status.lateBell || ""),
      classEnds: String(status.classEnds || ""),
      lateBellMs: teacherClockMillis_(status.lateBell, now, tz),
      classEndsMs: teacherClockMillis_(status.classEnds, now, tz),
      schoolDay: status.schoolDay !== false
    },
    classes: roster.map(function(item) {
      return {
        key: item.key,
        label: item.label || (item.period + " " + item.classTitle),
        count: (item.students || []).length
      };
    }),
    selected: group
      ? {
          key: group.key,
          label: group.label || "",
          period: group.period,
          classTitle: group.classTitle,
          live: isLiveClass
        }
      : null,
    students: students,
    passes: passes.map(function(pass) {
      return {
        row: pass.row,
        name: pass.name,
        displayName: pass.displayName,
        destination: String(pass.destination || ""),
        timeOutMs: pass.timeOutMs
      };
    }),
    log: log
  };
}

/* Check a student back in from the dashboard. Never overwrites a pass
   that was already closed on the kiosk. */
function teacherReturnPass(rowNumber) {
  var sheet = ensureLogHeaders();
  rowNumber = Number(rowNumber);

  if (!rowNumber || rowNumber < 2 || rowNumber > sheet.getLastRow()) {
    return { success: false, notFound: true };
  }

  var timeIn = sheet.getRange(rowNumber, 4).getValue();

  if (teacherIsDate_(timeIn) && !isNaN(timeIn.getTime())) {
    return { success: true, alreadyReturned: true };
  }

  checkIn(rowNumber);
  return { success: true };
}

function teacherFindAttendanceRow_(sheet, tz, period, className, studentId, studentName) {
  if (sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return null;

  var firstRow = kioskTodayStartRow_(sheet, 2);
  var count = sheet.getLastRow() - firstRow + 1;
  if (count < 1) return null;
  var values = sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1, count, 16).getValues();
  var display = sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1, count, 16).getDisplayValues();
  var todayKey = normalizeScheduleDateKey_(
    Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), tz, "M/d/yyyy")
  );
  var wantedKey = attendanceStudentKey_(studentId, studentName);
  var found = null;

  for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    var dateValue = values[i][1];
    var rowKey = teacherIsDate_(dateValue)
      ? normalizeScheduleDateKey_(Utilities.formatDate(dateValue, tz, "M/d/yyyy"))
      : normalizeScheduleDateKey_(display[i][1]);

    if (rowKey !== todayKey) continue;
    if (String(display[i][3] || "").trim() !== String(period || "").trim()) continue;
    if (String(display[i][4] || "").trim() !== String(className || "").trim()) continue;
    if (attendanceStudentKey_(display[i][6], display[i][5]) !== wantedKey) continue;

    found = {
      row: i + firstRow,
      status: String(display[i][10] || "").trim(),
      originalCheckIn: teacherIsDate_(values[i][8]) ? values[i][8] : null,
      minutesLate: String(display[i][15] || "").trim()
    };
  }

  return found;
}

/* Teacher correction from the dashboard.
   payload.status: "Present" | "Tardy" | "Late with Pass" | "Absent" */
// Attendance Events column Q: why the teacher checked a student in
// ("Forgot to check in" / "Kindness"), from the teacher view.
var ATTENDANCE_TEACHER_REASON_COLUMN_ = 17;
var KIOSK_REASON_FORGOT_ = "Forgot to check in";
var KIOSK_REASON_KINDNESS_ = "Kindness";

function kioskSaveTeacherReason_(sheet, row, reason) {
  reason = /forgot/i.test(String(reason || "")) ? KIOSK_REASON_FORGOT_
    : /kind/i.test(String(reason || "")) ? KIOSK_REASON_KINDNESS_ : "";
  if (!reason || !row) return;
  kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, ATTENDANCE_TEACHER_REASON_COLUMN_);
  if (!String(sheet.getRange(1, ATTENDANCE_TEACHER_REASON_COLUMN_).getDisplayValue() || "").trim()) {
    sheet.getRange(1, ATTENDANCE_TEACHER_REASON_COLUMN_).setValue("Teacher Reason");
  }
  sheet.getRange(row, ATTENDANCE_TEACHER_REASON_COLUMN_).setValue(reason);
}

function teacherSetAttendance(payload) {
  payload = payload || {};
  ensureAttendanceFoundation_();

  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, ATTENDANCE_LATE_MINUTES_COLUMN_);

  var tz = teacherDashboardTz_();
  var now = new Date();
  var wanted = String(payload.status || "").trim().toLowerCase();
  var period = String(payload.period || "").trim();
  var className = String(payload.className || "").trim();
  var studentName = String(payload.studentName || "").trim();
  var studentId = String(payload.studentId || "").trim();

  if (!studentName && !studentId) {
    return { success: false, message: "No student given." };
  }

  var existing = teacherFindAttendanceRow_(
    sheet, tz, period, className, studentId, studentName
  );

  if (existing && existing.status === "Cancelled") {
    existing = null;
  }

  var stamp = Utilities.formatDate(now, tz, "h:mm:ss a");
  var result = { success: true };

  if (wanted === "absent") {
    if (existing) {
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 8).setValue("Teacher Marked Absent");
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 10).setValue(now);
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 11).setValue("Cancelled");
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 13).setValue("Teacher");
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 14).setValue(
        "Marked absent by the teacher at " + stamp + "."
      );
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 15, 1, 2).setValues([["", ""]]);
    }
    result.status = "Absent";
  } else {
    var isLate = wanted !== "present";
    var lateType = !isLate
      ? ""
      : (wanted.indexOf("pass") !== -1
          ? ATTENDANCE_LATE_PASS_
          : ATTENDANCE_LATE_TARDY_);
    var finalStatus = isLate ? "Tardy" : "Present";
    var lateBellMinutes = parseScheduleClockMinutes_(
      String(payload.lateBell || "")
    );
    var minutes = "";

    if (isLate) {
      if (existing && existing.minutesLate) {
        minutes = existing.minutesLate;
      } else if (existing && existing.originalCheckIn) {
        minutes = attendanceMinutesLate_(
          attendanceNowMinutes_(existing.originalCheckIn, tz),
          lateBellMinutes
        );
      }
      if (minutes === "") {
        minutes = attendanceMinutesLate_(
          attendanceNowMinutes_(now, tz),
          lateBellMinutes
        );
      }
    }

    var note = "Set to " +
      (lateType || "Present") + " by the teacher at " + stamp + ".";

    if (existing) {
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 8).setValue("Teacher Updated");
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 10).setValue(now);
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 11).setValue(finalStatus);
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 13).setValue("Teacher");
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 14).setValue(note);
      sheet.getRange(existing.row, 15, 1, 2).setValues([[lateType, minutes]]);
      kioskSaveTeacherReason_(sheet, existing.row, payload.reason);
    } else {
      var emoji = "";
      try { emoji = chooseAttendanceEmoji_(studentId, studentName); } catch (err) { emoji = ""; }

      sheet
        .getRange(sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, 16)
        .setValues([[
          Utilities.getUuid(),
          now,
          String(payload.scheduleName || ""),
          period,
          className,
          studentName,
          studentId,
          "Teacher Check-In",
          now,
          now,
          finalStatus,
          emoji,
          "Teacher",
          note,
          lateType,
          minutes
        ]]);
      kioskSaveTeacherReason_(sheet, sheet.getLastRow(), payload.reason);
    }

    result.status = lateType || "Present";
    result.minutesLate = minutes;
  }

  SpreadsheetApp.flush();

  try {
    CacheService.getScriptCache().put(
      KIOSK_TEACHER_CHANGE_KEY_,
      String(now.getTime()),
      21600
    );
  } catch (err2) {}

  return result;
}

/* Polled by the student kiosk so teacher corrections appear on it.
   Costs one cache look-up unless the teacher actually changed something. */
function getTeacherAttendanceChanges(context) {
  context = context || {};
  var since = Number(context.since || 0);
  var stamp = 0;

  try {
    stamp = Number(
      CacheService.getScriptCache().get(KIOSK_TEACHER_CHANGE_KEY_) || 0
    );
  } catch (err) {
    stamp = 0;
  }

  if (!stamp || stamp <= since) {
    return { changed: false, stamp: stamp || since };
  }

  var sheet = ensureAttendanceEventsSheet_();
  kioskEnsureColumns_(sheet, ATTENDANCE_LATE_MINUTES_COLUMN_);
  var tz = teacherDashboardTz_();
  var teacherKeys = [];

  var firstRow = kioskTodayStartRow_(sheet, 2);

  if (sheet.getLastRow() >= firstRow) {
    var count = sheet.getLastRow() - firstRow + 1;
    var values = sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1, count, 16).getValues();
    var display = sheet.getRange(firstRow, 1, count, 16).getDisplayValues();
    var todayKey = normalizeScheduleDateKey_(
      Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), tz, "M/d/yyyy")
    );
    var seen = {};

    for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      if (String(display[i][12] || "").trim() !== "Teacher") continue;

      var dateValue = values[i][1];
      var rowKey = teacherIsDate_(dateValue)
        ? normalizeScheduleDateKey_(Utilities.formatDate(dateValue, tz, "M/d/yyyy"))
        : normalizeScheduleDateKey_(display[i][1]);

      if (rowKey !== todayKey) continue;
      if (String(display[i][3] || "").trim() !== String(context.period || "").trim()) continue;
      if (String(display[i][4] || "").trim() !== String(context.className || "").trim()) continue;

      var changedAt = teacherIsDate_(values[i][9]) ? values[i][9].getTime() : 0;
      if (changedAt <= since) continue;

      var key = attendanceStudentKey_(display[i][6], display[i][5]);
      if (!seen[key]) {
        seen[key] = true;
        teacherKeys.push(key);
      }
    }
  }

  var states = [];

  if (teacherKeys.length) {
    var current = getAttendanceState({
      period: context.period,
      className: context.className
    });
    states = (current && current.states || []).filter(function(state) {
      return teacherKeys.indexOf(state.studentKey) !== -1;
    });
  }

  return {
    changed: true,
    stamp: stamp,
    teacherKeys: teacherKeys,
    states: states
  };
}


/* ===== Sharing with other teachers (2026-10-17) =====
   1. Colleen: File > Make a copy of the Sheet, name the copy with the word TEMPLATE in it, then in the
      COPY: Extensions > Apps Script > choose cleanForSharing > Run. It empties everything personal
      and leaves 8 made-up sample students. It refuses to run in a Sheet without TEMPLATE in its name,
      so the real kiosk can never be wiped by mistake.
   2. A new teacher: opens the template's "make a copy" link, then Extensions > Apps Script >
      setUpMyKiosk > Run (once), then Deploy > New deployment > Web app (see SETUP-FOR-TEACHERS.md).
   School-specific values kept in the school edition: the Bell Schedule / Schedule Calendar tabs, the
   bell.plus link (Settings), and KIOSK_STUDENT_EMAIL_DOMAIN_. */
var KIOSK_TEMPLATE_CLEAR_TABS_ = ["Logs", "Attendance", "Attendance Events", "Weekly Pass Summary", "Seating Charts",
  "Seating Layouts", "Class Memories", "To-Do", "Classes"];
var KIOSK_TEMPLATE_SAMPLES_ = [["Avery", "Brooks"], ["Jordan", "Cruz"], ["Riley", "Patel"], ["Sam", "Nguyen"],
  ["Taylor", "Okafor"], ["Morgan", "Reyes"], ["Casey", "Kim"], ["Jamie", "Lopez"]];

// Deletes rows without ever deleting every row under a frozen heading (Google Sheets refuses that:
// "it is not possible to delete all non-frozen rows"), by first adding one blank row at the bottom.
function kioskDeleteRowsSafely_(sh, start, count) {
  if (count < 1) return;
  if (sh.getMaxRows() <= start + count - 1) sh.insertRowsAfter(sh.getMaxRows(), 1);
  sh.deleteRows(start, count);
}

function cleanForSharing() {
  var ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  if (!/TEMPLATE/.test(ss.getName())) {
    throw new Error("Stopped: this Sheet is named \"" + ss.getName() + "\". cleanForSharing only runs in a copy whose name " +
      "has the word TEMPLATE in it (File > Make a copy, then rename it), so your real kiosk is never wiped.");
  }
  var done = [];
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    // 1. Data tabs: keep the headings, remove every row under them.
    KIOSK_TEMPLATE_CLEAR_TABS_.forEach(function(name) {
      var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
      if (sh && sh.getLastRow() > 1) { kioskDeleteRowsSafely_(sh, 2, sh.getLastRow() - 1); done.push("Emptied " + name); }
    });
    // 2. Archive tabs (last year's logs and attendance): removed.
    ss.getSheets().forEach(function(sh) {
      if (/^Archive /.test(sh.getName())) { done.push("Removed " + sh.getName()); ss.deleteSheet(sh); }
    });
    // 3. Students: 8 made-up students in one sample class.
    var stu = ss.getSheetByName("Students");
    if (stu) {
      if (stu.getLastRow() > 1) kioskDeleteRowsSafely_(stu, 2, stu.getLastRow() - 1);
      var width = Math.max(5, stu.getLastColumn()), sc = kioskStudentColumns_(stu);
      var rows = KIOSK_TEMPLATE_SAMPLES_.map(function(n, i) {
        var r = []; for (var c = 0; c < width; c++) r.push("");
        r[sc.period - 1] = "1"; r[sc.classTitle - 1] = "Sample Class"; r[sc.first - 1] = n[0]; r[sc.last - 1] = n[1]; r[sc.id - 1] = String(900001 + i);
        return r;
      });
      stu.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, width).setValues(rows);
      done.push("Students: 8 made-up sample students in 1st period \"Sample Class\"");
    }
    // 4. Backgrounds and fonts Colleen uploaded herself (Notes say "Uploaded through Teacher Settings").
    //    The design backgrounds stay, even though their pictures are link-shared copies in her Drive.
    var fromDrive = function(v) { return /DRIVEFILE:|drive\.google\.com|googleusercontent\.com|docs\.google\.com/i.test(String(v || "")); };
    ["Fonts"].forEach(function(name) {      // Backgrounds all stay (2026-10-17: her designs were uploaded through Settings too; she removes personal ones by hand)
      var sh = ss.getSheetByName(name);
      if (!sh || sh.getLastRow() < 2) return;
      var vals = sh.getRange(2, 1, sh.getLastRow() - 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues(), gone = 0;
      for (var i = vals.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        var custom = vals[i].some(fromDrive) || /CUSTOM/i.test(vals[i].join("|"));
        if (custom) { kioskDeleteRowsSafely_(sh, i + 2, 1); gone++; }
      }
      if (gone) done.push(name + ": removed " + gone + " item" + (gone === 1 ? "" : "s") + " you uploaded yourself");
    });
    // 5. Settings: the report email and any setting that points at a Drive file go back to blank (= the default).
    var set = ss.getSheetByName("Settings");
    if (set && set.getLastRow() >= 2) {
      var sv = set.getRange(1, 1, set.getLastRow(), 2).getValues(), blanked = 0;
      sv.forEach(function(r, i) {
        var label = String(r[0] || ""), v = r[1];
        if (/@/.test(String(v || "")) || fromDrive(v) || /report email|email address/i.test(label) && String(v || "").trim()) {
          set.getRange(i + 1, 2).setValue(""); blanked++;
        }
      });
      if (blanked) done.push("Settings: blanked " + blanked + " personal value" + (blanked === 1 ? "" : "s") + " (email, uploaded files)");
    }
    // 6. Saved choices (teacher PIN, reminder settings and wording, waiting line, emails already sent) and timers.
    var props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties(), keys = props.getKeys();
    keys.forEach(function(k) { props.deleteProperty(k); });
    if (keys.length) done.push("Forgot " + keys.length + " saved choices (PIN, reminders, line, sent emails)");
    ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().forEach(function(t) { ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t); });
    try { CacheService.getScriptCache().removeAll(["kiosk:version", "kiosk:watcherChecked", "kiosk:timersChecked"]); } catch (e) {}
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
  var msg = "Ready to share. Kept: bell schedule, destinations, icons, emoji bank, backgrounds, dog stamps, animal cameras. " +
    "Check the Backgrounds tab and delete any personal photos.\n- " + done.join("\n- ");
  Logger.log(msg);
  return msg;
}

// A new teacher runs this once in their own copy (Extensions > Apps Script > setUpMyKiosk > Run):
// Google asks for permission, then every tab and the automatic timers are made.
function setUpMyKiosk() {
  setupAttendanceFoundation();
  try { ensureLogHeaders(); ensureBackgroundsSheet(); ensureFontsSheet(); ensureDisplaySettingsRows_(); ensureDestinationIconBankSheet_(); } catch (e) {}
  try { installOrRefreshAttendanceReportAutomation(); } catch (e) {}
  try { kioskEnsureChangeWatcher_(); kioskEnsureArchiveTimer_(); } catch (e) {}
  kioskReportInUse_(true);      // lets Colleen know this copy is in use (email, Sheet name, release)
  var msg = "Your kiosk is set up (release " + KIOSK_RELEASE + "). Colleen's kiosk was told you're using it, so she can let you know about updates. Next: Deploy > New deployment > Web app (Execute as: Me, Who has access: Anyone), " +
    "then open the web app link on the kiosk device. Add ?view=teacher to the end of the link for your teacher view.";
  Logger.log(msg);
  return msg;
}
